> DEVELOPER

Implement the following plan:

# Unify Issue Lifecycle Status & Surface History to Reviewers

## Context

Issue lifecycle is tracked in two places with overlapping semantics:
- **State**: `issue.status` — `open/fixed/wontfix/false_positive/auto_resolved`
- **Plan**: `plan.skipped[id].kind` — `temporary/permanent/false_positive/triaged_out`

When `plan skip --permanent` runs, it sets BOTH `plan.skipped[id].kind = "permanent"` AND `issue.status = "wontfix"`. But `temporary` and `triaged_out` skips leave `issue.status = "open"` — creating a lie. An issue can appear "open" in state while being intentionally deferred in plan. This means:
- Reviewers see `[open]` for deferred issues with no way to know they were already triaged
- `count_open_by_detector()` overcounts (includes secretly-deferred issues)
- Any state-only code (no plan access) gets a wrong picture

**Fix**: Add `deferred` and `triaged_out` to the Status enum. Make all skip operations update state status. `plan.skipped` continues to exist for queue management and metadata (review_after, attestation, etc.), but state is always the authoritative source for disposition.

Separately, review agents have no visibility into historical issues by default (`--retrospective` is opt-in) and no CLI exploration tools. Fix that too.

## Plan

### Part A: Unify the Status Enum

#### A1. Add new statuses to enum
**File**: `desloppify/base/enums.py`

Add `DEFERRED = "deferred"` and `TRIAGED_OUT = "triaged_out"` to the `Status` StrEnum. Keep `RESOLVED = "resolved"` as the legacy on-disk alias (it migrates to FIXED via `_LEGACY_STATUS_ALIASES`).

Update `_CANONICAL_ISSUE_STATUSES` to include both new values.

**NOT in `_RESOLVED_STATUSES`** — deferred/triaged_out are "acknowledged but not acted on", not resolved.

#### A2. Update scoring policy for new statuses
**File**: `desloppify/engine/_scoring/policy/core.py` (~line 192)

`FAILURE_STATUSES_BY_MODE` uses explicit allowlists, not a generic "unresolved" concept. Deferred/triaged_out issues are still real debt — they must count as failures:

```python
FAILURE_STATUSES_BY_MODE: dict[ScoreMode, frozenset[str]] = {
    "lenient": frozenset({"open", "deferred", "triaged_out"}),
    "strict": frozenset({"open", "wontfix", "auto_resolved", "deferred", "triaged_out"}),
    "verified_strict": frozenset({"open", "wontfix", "fixed", "false_positive", "deferred", "triaged_out"}),
}
```

Test all three modes to ensure deferred/triaged_out issues still hurt scores appropriately.

#### A3. Update skip ↔ status mapping
**File**: `desloppify/engine/_plan/skip_policy.py`

```python
def skip_kind_state_status(kind: str) -> str | None:
    return {
        "permanent": "wontfix",
        "false_positive": "false_positive",
        "temporary": "deferred",        # NEW
        "triaged_out": "triaged_out",   # NEW
    }.get(kind)

def skip_kind_needs_state_reopen(kind: str) -> bool:
    return kind in ("permanent", "false_positive", "temporary", "triaged_out")
```

This means `_apply_state_skip_resolution()` in `override_skip.py` will now update state status for temporary skips too. And `unskip_items()` will reopen deferred/triaged_out issues back to "open".

#### A4. Fix backlog path to reopen state
**File**: `desloppify/app/commands/plan/override_skip.py` (~line 335) and/or `desloppify/engine/_plan/operations/skip.py:172`

`backlog_items()` only removes from `plan.skipped` — it does NOT touch state. After A3, this would strand issues as `status: "deferred"` while no longer tracked by plan.

Fix: in `cmd_plan_backlog()`, after calling `backlog_items()`, reopen the removed issues in state:

```python
removed = backlog_items(plan, issue_ids)
if removed:
    # Reopen issues that were deferred/triaged_out so they don't get stranded
    resolve_issues(state, removed, "open", ...)
```

Only reopen issues whose current status is `deferred` or `triaged_out` (not wontfix/false_positive — those have separate semantics via permanent/false_positive skip kinds, and backlog for those is intentional "remove from plan tracking without changing disposition").

#### A5. Update triage dismiss to set state status
**File**: `desloppify/engine/_plan/triage/dismiss.py` (or `apply.py`)

Currently triage dismiss creates a plan skip with `kind: "triaged_out"` but doesn't touch state. Verify whether it flows through `_apply_state_skip_resolution()`. If not (triage likely calls `skip_items()` directly in the plan layer without the app-level state wrapper), add explicit state status update in the triage apply path.

#### A6. Handle scan reappearance
**File**: `desloppify/engine/_state/merge_issues.py`

In `upsert_issues()`, deferred/triaged_out issues should NOT auto-reopen when they reappear in scan (they were intentionally deferred). The current reopening condition checks `status in ("fixed", "auto_resolved", "false_positive")` — deferred/triaged_out are already excluded, so no change needed here. Verify this is correct.

#### A7. Add status icons
**File**: `desloppify/app/commands/show/render.py`

```python
status_icon = {
    ...,
    "deferred": "⏸",       # NEW
    "triaged_out": "△",    # NEW
}
```

#### A8. Update plan/status reporting surfaces
**Files**:
- `desloppify/engine/planning/render_sections.py:15` — `summary_lines()` totals only sum `open/fixed/wontfix/auto_resolved`. Add `deferred` and `triaged_out` to the total count and decide how they affect "addressed" math (they're acknowledged but not addressed — probably count toward total but not addressed).
- `desloppify/engine/planning/render.py:65` — plan header line shows `N open | N fixed | N wontfix | N auto-resolved`. Add `N deferred | N triaged-out` or collapse into a single "N deferred" bucket.
- Any other reporting surface that enumerates status buckets explicitly.

#### A9. Data migration for existing state
**File**: `desloppify/engine/_plan/reconcile.py`

Add a reconciliation step in `reconcile_plan_after_scan()` that syncs status for any `open` issue found in `plan.skipped`:
- `open` + skip kind `temporary` → set `status: "deferred"`
- `open` + skip kind `triaged_out` → set `status: "triaged_out"`

This runs on every scan, so existing data gets migrated on next scan. State normalization (`ensure_state_defaults`) doesn't have plan access, so reconciliation is the right place.

### Part B: Surface History to Reviewers

#### B1. Default `--retrospective` to True
**File**: `desloppify/app/cli_support/parser_groups_admin_review_options_core.py`

Change from `action="store_true"` to `default=True` with `--no-retrospective` opt-out.

#### B2. Rewrite `render_historical_focus` with grouping and CLI hints
**File**: `desloppify/app/commands/review/prompt_sections.py`

Now that state status is authoritative, rendering is straightforward — no plan cross-referencing needed:

```
Previously flagged issues — navigation aid, not scoring evidence:
Check whether open issues still exist. Do not re-report resolved or deferred items.
If several past issues share a root cause, call that out.

  Still open (2):
    - generic_verb_process: processData is vague — callers use it for reconciliation
    - stale_alias_pattern: compatibility aliases persist after rename

  Deferred (1):
    - [deferred] naming_drift: low-priority naming inconsistency in helpers/

  Triaged out (1):
    - [triaged out] verbose_handlers: verbose error handling in batch flow

  Resolved (2):
    - [fixed] misleading_load_fixer: renamed to resolve_fixer_config
    - [wontfix] short_util_names: deliberately terse utility names

Explore past review issues:
  desloppify show review --no-budget              # all open review issues
  desloppify show review --status deferred         # deferred issues
  desloppify show <dimension_name>                 # dimension-specific view
```

#### B3. Add dimension deferral context to batch prompt
**File**: `desloppify/app/commands/review/prompt_sections.py`

New `render_dimension_deferral_context()` function. Render:

```
Note: This dimension was deferred for 3 scan cycles while objective issues took priority.
Previous assessment may be stale — calibrate accordingly.
```

Wire into `render_batch_prompt` in `batch/prompt_template.py` AND the external review prompt in `external.py:225` (both paths must stay consistent).

#### B4. Thread plan path into review prepare flow
**File**: `desloppify/intelligence/review/prepare_holistic_payload_parts.py`

Do NOT call `load_plan()` with no args — that's unsafe when the user passes `--state` pointing elsewhere. The state path is already available in `prepare.py:17` as `_state_path`. Thread the resolved plan path through packet construction:

- `do_prepare()` has `_state_path` → derive plan path via `plan_path_for_state()`
- Pass through `build_review_packet_payload()` → `prepare_holistic_review_payload()` → `_attach_issue_history_context()` and dimension deferral attachment
- Extract `subjective_defer_meta` for B3

With Part A complete, we do NOT need plan data for issue status (state is authoritative). Plan is only needed for dimension deferral context (B3).

|------|--------|
| `base/enums.py` | Add DEFERRED, TRIAGED_OUT to Status, _CANONICAL_ISSUE_STATUSES |

## What stays the same

- **`plan.skipped` dict** — still exists for queue management and metadata. ~40 consumers unchanged.
- **`subjective_defer_meta`** — dimension scheduling, not issue lifecycle. Stays as-is. We surface it as context to reviewers (B3).
- **~50 `status == "open"` checks** — become more correct: deferred issues drop out naturally.

## Verification

1. Unit test: Status enum includes deferred/triaged_out, canonical_issue_status handles them
2. Unit test: skip_kind_state_status returns correct mapping for all 4 kinds
3. Unit test: FAILURE_STATUSES_BY_MODE includes new statuses in all 3 modes
4. Unit test: unskip reopens deferred/triaged_out to open
5. Unit test: cmd_plan_backlog reopens deferred issues to open in state
6. Unit test: upsert_issues does NOT reopen deferred/triaged_out
7. Unit test: data migration in reconcile syncs open+skipped → correct status
8. Unit test: render_historical_focus groups by status correctly
9. Unit test: summary_lines counts deferred/triaged_out correctly
10. Integration: `python -m pytest desloppify/tests/ -q` — all existing tests pass
11. Integration: `desloppify plan skip --temporary` sets issue status to deferred
12. Integration: `desloppify plan unskip` reopens deferred issue to open
13. Integration: `desloppify show review --status deferred` shows deferred review issues

    65→def _build_builtin_detector_scoring_policies() -> dict[str, DetectorScoringPolicy]:

   108→def _build_dimensions() -> list[Dimension]:

   110→

   115→    # and group detectors by dimension.
   116→    dim_tiers: dict[str, int] = {}

   118→    for detector, policy in DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES.items():

   122→            dim_tiers[policy.dimension] = policy.tier
   123→            grouped[policy.dimension] = []
   124→        grouped[policy.dimension].append(detector)
   125→    return [
   126→        Dimension(name=name, tier=tier, detectors=grouped[name])
   127→        for name, tier in dim_tiers.items()
   128→    ]

   153→    "file health": 2.0,
   154→    "code quality": 1.0,
   155→    "duplication": 1.0,
   156→    "test health": 1.0,
   157→    "security": 1.0,
   158→}
   159→
   160→# Per-dimension weighting within the subjective pool.
   161→# Rationale (kept in sync with review metadata modules):
   162→# - High/mid elegance carry the most weight because architectural decomposition
   163→#   and seam quality drive broad maintainability and change velocity.
   164→# - Low elegance, contracts, and type safety remain high because they prevent
   165→#   correctness drift and interface ambiguity.
   166→# - Design coherence is a medium-high bridge between architecture intent and
   167→#   the detector-led concern stream.
   168→# - Structure navigation and error consistency are meaningful but secondary
   169→#   signals compared to core architecture/correctness dimensions.
   170→# - Naming quality and AI-generated debt are intentionally low-weight nudges:
   171→#   useful for polish/cleanup, but they should not dominate score movement.
   172→SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS: dict[str, float] = {
   173→    "high elegance": 22.0,
   174→    "mid elegance": 22.0,
   175→    "low elegance": 12.0,
   176→    "contracts": 12.0,
   177→    "type safety": 12.0,
   178→    "abstraction fit": 8.0,
   179→    "logic clarity": 6.0,
   180→    # Low-but-meaningful structural signal (about half of the subjective
   181→    # average weight) so it matters without dominating craftsmanship axes.
   182→    "structure nav": 5.0,
   183→    "error consistency": 3.0,
   184→    "naming quality": 2.0,
   185→    "ai generated debt": 1.0,
   186→    "design coherence": 10.0,
   187→}
   188→
   189→# Synthetic check count for subjective dimensions in dimension_scores.
   190→SUBJECTIVE_CHECKS = 10
   191→
   192→FAILURE_STATUSES_BY_MODE: dict[ScoreMode, frozenset[str]] = {
   193→    "lenient": frozenset({"open"}),
   194→    "strict": frozenset({"open", "wontfix", "auto_resolved"}),
   195→    "verified_strict": frozenset({"open", "wontfix", "fixed", "false_positive"}),
   196→}
   197→
   198→# Tolerance for treating a subjective score as "on target" in integrity checks.
   199→# Scores within this band of the target are flagged as potential gaming.
   200→SUBJECTIVE_TARGET_MATCH_TOLERANCE = 0.05
   201→
   202→
   203→def matches_target_score(
   204→    score: object,
   205→    target: object,
   206→    *,
   207→    tolerance: float = SUBJECTIVE_TARGET_MATCH_TOLERANCE,
   208→) -> bool:
   209→    """Return True when score is within tolerance of target."""
   210→    try:
   211→        score_value = float(score)
   212→        target_value = float(target)
   213→        tolerance_value = max(0.0, float(tolerance))
   214→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   215→        return False
   216→    return abs(score_value - target_value) <= tolerance_value
   217→
   218→
   219→def register_scoring_policy(policy: DetectorScoringPolicy) -> None:
   220→    """Register a scoring policy at runtime (used by generic plugins)."""
   221→    DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES[policy.detector] = policy
   222→    _rebuild_derived()
   223→
   224→
   225→def reset_registered_scoring_policies() -> None:
   226→    """Reset runtime-added scoring policies to built-in defaults."""
   227→    DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES.clear()
   228→    DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES.update(_BASE_DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES)
   229→    _rebuild_derived()
   230→
   231→
   232→def _rebuild_derived() -> None:
   233→    """Rebuild DIMENSIONS, DIMENSIONS_BY_NAME, FILE_BASED_DETECTORS from current state.
   234→
   235→    Mutates existing objects in-place so that all references (including imports
   236→    that bound the original objects) see the updates.
   237→    """
   238→    new_dims = _build_dimensions()
   239→    DIMENSIONS.clear()
   240→    DIMENSIONS.extend(new_dims)
   241→    DIMENSIONS_BY_NAME.clear()
   242→    DIMENSIONS_BY_NAME.update({d.name: d for d in DIMENSIONS})
   243→    FILE_BASED_DETECTORS.clear()
   244→    FILE_BASED_DETECTORS.update(
   245→        det for det, pol in DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES.items() if pol.file_based
   246→    )
   247→
   248→
   249→def detector_policy(detector: str) -> DetectorScoringPolicy:
   250→    """Get scoring policy for a detector, with a safe default fallback."""
   251→    return DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES.get(
   252→        detector,
   253→        DetectorScoringPolicy(detector=detector, dimension=None, tier=None),
   254→    )
   255→
   256→
   257→__all__ = [
   258→    "CONFIDENCE_WEIGHTS",
   259→    "DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES",
   260→    "DIMENSIONS",
   261→    "DIMENSIONS_BY_NAME",
   262→    "FAILURE_STATUSES_BY_MODE",
   263→    "FILE_BASED_DETECTORS",
   264→    "HOLISTIC_MULTIPLIER",
   265→    "HOLISTIC_POTENTIAL",
   266→    "MECHANICAL_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS",
   267→    "MECHANICAL_WEIGHT_FRACTION",
   268→    "MIN_SAMPLE",
   269→    "SCORING_MODES",
   270→    "SECURITY_EXCLUDED_ZONES",
   271→    "SUBJECTIVE_CHECKS",

   180→def cmd_plan_skip(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   181→    """Skip issues — unified command for temporary/permanent/false-positive."""
   182→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   183→    state = runtime.state
   184→    if not require_issue_inventory(state):
   185→        return
   186→
   187→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   188→    reason: str | None = getattr(args, "reason", None)
   189→    review_after: int | None = getattr(args, "review_after", None)
   190→    permanent: bool = getattr(args, "permanent", False)
   191→    false_positive: bool = getattr(args, "false_positive", False)
   192→    note: str | None = getattr(args, "note", None)
   193→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attest", None)
   194→

   196→
   197→    if not _validate_temporary_skip_confirmation(
   198→        confirm=bool(getattr(args, "confirm", False)),
   199→        kind=kind,
   200→        reason=reason,
   201→        attestation=attestation,
   202→    ):
   203→        return
   204→    if not _validate_skip_requirements(kind=kind, attestation=attestation, note=note):
   205→        raise CommandError(

   207→            exit_code=2,
   208→        )
   209→
   210→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   211→    plan_file = _plan_file_for_state(state_file)
   212→    plan = load_plan(plan_file)
   213→    issue_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
   214→    if not issue_ids:
   215→        print(colorize("  No matching issues found.", "yellow"))
   216→        return
   217→
   218→    _warn_or_block_bulk_skip(issue_ids, confirm=bool(getattr(args, "confirm", False)))
   219→
   220→    state_data = _apply_state_skip_resolution(
   221→        kind=kind,
   222→        state_file=state_file,
   223→        issue_ids=issue_ids,
   224→        note=note,
   225→        attestation=attestation,
   226→    )
   227→
   228→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count", 0)
   229→    count = skip_items(
   230→        plan,
   231→        issue_ids,
   232→        kind=kind,
   233→        reason=reason,
   234→        note=note,
   235→        attestation=attestation,
   236→        review_after=review_after,
   237→        scan_count=scan_count,
   238→    )
   239→
   240→    append_log_entry(
   241→        plan,
   242→        "skip",
   243→        issue_ids=issue_ids,
   244→        actor="user",
   245→        note=note,
   246→        detail={"kind": kind, "reason": reason},
   247→    )
   248→    clear_postflight_scan_completion(plan, issue_ids=issue_ids)
   249→    _save_skip_plan_state(
   250→        plan=plan,
   251→        plan_file=plan_file,
   252→        state_data=state_data,
   253→        state_file=state_file,
   254→    )
   255→
   256→    print(colorize(f"  {SKIP_KIND_LABELS[kind]} {count} item(s).", "green"))
   257→    if review_after:
   258→        print(colorize(f"  Will re-surface after {review_after} scan(s).", "dim"))
   259→    if kind == "temporary":
   260→        print(

   264→                "dim",
   265→            )
   266→        )
   267→    print_user_message(
   268→        "Hey — if skipping was the right call, just continue with"
   269→        " what you were doing. If you think a broader re-triage is"
   270→        " needed, use `desloppify plan triage`. Run `desloppify"
   271→        " plan --help` to see all available plan tools. Otherwise"
   272→        " no need to reply, just keep going."
   273→    )
   274→
   275→
   276→def cmd_plan_unskip(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   277→    """Unskip issues — bring back to queue."""
   278→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   279→    state = runtime.state
   280→    if not require_issue_inventory(state):
   281→        return
   282→
   283→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   284→
   285→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   286→    plan_file = _plan_file_for_state(state_file)
   287→    plan = load_plan(plan_file)
   288→    issue_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan, status_filter="all")
   289→    if not issue_ids:
   290→        print(colorize("  No matching issues found.", "yellow"))
   291→        return
   292→
   293→    include_protected = bool(getattr(args, "force", False))
   294→    count, need_reopen, protected_kept = unskip_items(
   295→        plan,
   296→        issue_ids,
   297→        include_protected=include_protected,
   298→    )
   299→    unskipped_ids = [fid for fid in issue_ids if fid not in protected_kept]
   300→    append_log_entry(
   301→        plan,
   302→        "unskip",
   303→        issue_ids=unskipped_ids,
   304→        actor="user",
   305→        detail={"need_reopen": need_reopen},
   306→    )
   307→    clear_postflight_scan_completion(plan, issue_ids=unskipped_ids)
   308→
   309→    reopened: list[str] = []
   310→    if need_reopen:
   311→        state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   312→        for fid in need_reopen:
   313→            reopened.extend(state_mod.resolve_issues(state_data, fid, "open"))
   314→        save_plan_state_transactional(
   315→            plan=plan,
   316→            plan_path=plan_file,
   317→            state_data=state_data,
   318→            state_path_value=state_file,
   319→        )
   320→        print(colorize(f"  Reopened {len(reopened)} issue(s) in state.", "dim"))
   321→    else:
   322→        save_plan(plan, plan_file)
   323→
   324→    print(colorize(f"  Unskipped {count} item(s) — back in queue.", "green"))
   325→    if protected_kept:
   326→        print(
   327→            colorize(

   329→                f"(permanent/false_positive with notes). Use --force to override.",
   330→                "yellow",
   331→            )
   332→        )
   333→
   334→
   335→def cmd_plan_backlog(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   336→    """Move deferred items to backlog — remove from plan tracking entirely."""
   337→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   338→    state = runtime.state
   339→    if not require_issue_inventory(state):
   340→        return
   341→
   342→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   343→
   344→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   345→    plan_file = _plan_file_for_state(state_file)
   346→    plan = load_plan(plan_file)
   347→    issue_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan, status_filter="all")
   348→    if not issue_ids:
   349→        print(colorize("  No matching issues found.", "yellow"))
   350→        return
   351→
   352→    removed = backlog_items(plan, issue_ids)
   353→    if not removed:
   354→        print(colorize("  No matching deferred items found.", "yellow"))
   355→        return
   356→
   357→    append_log_entry(
   358→        plan,
   359→        "backlog",
   360→        issue_ids=removed,
   361→        actor="user",
   362→    )
   363→    clear_postflight_scan_completion(plan, issue_ids=removed)
   364→    save_plan(plan, plan_file)
   365→
   366→    print(colorize(f"  Moved {len(removed)} item(s) to backlog.", "green"))
   367→

1→"""Skip/unskip mutations for plan operations."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations

     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine._plan.operations.lifecycle import clear_focus_if_cluster_empty
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.operations.queue import _remove_id_from_lists
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.promoted_ids import prune_promoted_ids
    10→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, SkipEntry, ensure_plan_defaults
    11→from desloppify.engine._plan.skip_policy import skip_kind_needs_state_reopen
    12→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
    13→
    14→
    15→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    16→class SkipOptions:
    17→    """Optional fields used when skipping issue IDs."""
    18→
    19→    kind: str = "temporary"
    20→    reason: str | None = None
    21→    note: str | None = None
    22→    attestation: str | None = None
    23→    review_after: int | None = None
    24→    scan_count: int = 0
    25→
    26→
    27→def _skip_options(
    28→    *,
    29→    options: SkipOptions | None,
    30→    legacy_kwargs: dict[str, object],
    31→) -> SkipOptions:
    32→    """Resolve skip options from a config object or legacy kwargs."""
    33→    allowed_keys = {
    34→        "kind",
    35→        "reason",
    36→        "note",
    37→        "attestation",
    38→        "review_after",
    39→        "scan_count",
    40→    }
    41→    unknown = sorted(set(legacy_kwargs) - allowed_keys)
    42→    if unknown:
    43→        joined = ", ".join(unknown)
    44→        raise TypeError(f"Unexpected keyword argument(s): {joined}")
    45→    if options is not None and legacy_kwargs:
    46→        raise TypeError("Pass either options=... or legacy keyword args, not both")
    47→    if options is not None:
    48→        return options
    49→    kind = legacy_kwargs.get("kind")
    50→    reason = legacy_kwargs.get("reason")
    51→    note = legacy_kwargs.get("note")
    52→    attestation = legacy_kwargs.get("attestation")
    53→    review_after = legacy_kwargs.get("review_after")
    54→    scan_count = legacy_kwargs.get("scan_count")
    55→    return SkipOptions(
    56→        kind=str(kind) if kind is not None else "temporary",
    57→        reason=None if reason is None else str(reason),
    58→        note=None if note is None else str(note),
    59→        attestation=None if attestation is None else str(attestation),
    60→        review_after=int(review_after) if isinstance(review_after, int) else None,
    61→        scan_count=int(scan_count) if isinstance(scan_count, int) else 0,
    62→    )
    63→
    64→
    65→def skip_items(
    66→    plan: PlanModel,
    67→    issue_ids: list[str],
    68→    *,
    69→    options: SkipOptions | None = None,
    70→    **legacy_kwargs,
    71→) -> int:
    72→    """Move issue IDs to the skipped dict. Returns count skipped."""
    73→    skip_options = _skip_options(
    74→        options=options,
    75→        legacy_kwargs=legacy_kwargs,
    76→    )
    77→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    78→    now = utc_now()
    79→    count = 0
    80→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
    81→    skip_set = set(issue_ids)
    82→    prune_promoted_ids(plan, skip_set)
    83→    for fid in issue_ids:
    84→        _remove_id_from_lists(plan, fid)
    85→        skipped[fid] = {
    86→            "issue_id": fid,
    87→            "kind": skip_options.kind,
    88→            "reason": skip_options.reason,
    89→            "note": skip_options.note,
    90→            "attestation": skip_options.attestation,
    91→            "created_at": now,
    92→            "review_after": skip_options.review_after,
    93→            "skipped_at_scan": skip_options.scan_count,
    94→        }
    95→        count += 1
    96→    clear_focus_if_cluster_empty(plan)
    97→    return count
    98→
    99→
   100→def _is_protected_skip(entry: SkipEntry) -> bool:
   101→    """A skip is protected if it was permanent/false_positive AND has a note.
   102→
   103→    Protected skips represent deliberate human judgment and should not be
   104→    silently undone by bulk unskip operations.
   105→    """
   106→    kind = str(entry.get("kind", ""))
   107→    has_note = bool(entry.get("note", ""))
   108→    return kind in ("permanent", "false_positive") and has_note
   109→
   110→
   111→def unskip_items(
   112→    plan: PlanModel,
   113→    issue_ids: list[str],
   114→    *,
   115→    include_protected: bool = False,
   116→) -> tuple[int, list[str], list[str]]:
   117→    """Bring issue IDs back from skipped to the end of queue_order.
   118→
   119→    Returns ``(count_unskipped, permanent_ids_needing_state_reopen, protected_ids_kept)``
   120→    where the second list contains IDs that were permanent or false_positive
   121→    and need their state-layer status reopened by the caller, and the third
   122→    list contains protected IDs that were NOT unskipped (unless
   123→    ``include_protected=True``).
   124→    """
   125→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   126→    count = 0
   127→    need_reopen: list[str] = []
   128→    protected_kept: list[str] = []
   129→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   130→    for fid in issue_ids:
   131→        entry = skipped.get(fid)
   132→        if entry is None:
   133→            continue
   134→        if not include_protected and _is_protected_skip(entry):
   135→            protected_kept.append(fid)
   136→            continue
   137→        skipped.pop(fid)
   138→        if skip_kind_needs_state_reopen(str(entry.get("kind", ""))):
   139→            need_reopen.append(fid)
   140→        if fid not in plan["queue_order"]:
   141→            plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   142→        count += 1
   143→    return count, need_reopen, protected_kept
   144→
   145→
   146→def resurface_stale_skips(
   147→    plan: PlanModel, current_scan_count: int
   148→) -> list[str]:
   149→    """Move temporary skips past their review_after threshold back to queue.
   150→
   151→    Returns list of resurfaced issue IDs.
   152→    """
   153→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   154→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   155→    resurfaced: list[str] = []
   156→    for fid in list(skipped):
   157→        entry = skipped[fid]
   158→        if entry.get("kind") != "temporary":
   159→            continue
   160→        review_after = entry.get("review_after")
   161→        if review_after is None:
   162→            continue
   163→        skipped_at = entry.get("skipped_at_scan", 0)
   164→        if current_scan_count >= skipped_at + review_after:
   165→            skipped.pop(fid)
   166→            if fid not in plan["queue_order"]:
   167→                plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   168→            resurfaced.append(fid)
   169→    return resurfaced
   170→
   171→
   172→def backlog_items(plan: PlanModel, issue_ids: list[str]) -> list[str]:
   173→    """Remove from plan.skipped without adding to queue_order.
   174→
   175→    Issues stay open in state but are no longer plan-tracked.
   176→    Returns the IDs that were actually removed.
   177→    """
   178→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   179→    removed: list[str] = []
   180→    for fid in issue_ids:
   181→        if fid in plan["skipped"]:
   182→            plan["skipped"].pop(fid)
   183→            removed.append(fid)
   184→    return removed
   185→
   186→
   187→__all__ = ["backlog_items", "resurface_stale_skips", "skip_items", "unskip_items"]
   188→
```

1→"""Issue upsert/verification helpers for scan merge."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.base.discovery.file_paths import matches_exclusion
     6→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import matched_ignore_pattern
     7→
     8→
     9→def find_suspect_detectors(
    10→    existing: dict,
    11→    current_by_detector: dict[str, int],
    12→    force_resolve: bool,
    13→    ran_detectors: set[str] | None = None,
    14→) -> set[str]:
    15→    """Detectors that had open issues but likely did not actually run this scan."""
    16→    if force_resolve:
    17→        return set()
    18→
    19→    previous_open_by_detector: dict[str, int] = {}
    20→    for issue in existing.values():
    21→        if issue["status"] != "open":
    22→            continue
    23→        detector = issue.get("detector", "unknown")
    24→        previous_open_by_detector[detector] = (
    25→            previous_open_by_detector.get(detector, 0) + 1
    26→        )
    27→
    28→    # 'review' issues enter via `desloppify review --import`, not via scan phases.
    29→    # They are always suspect so the scan never auto-resolves them — regardless
    30→    # of current issue status (open, wontfix, etc.).
    31→    import_only_detectors = {"review"}
    32→    suspect: set[str] = set(import_only_detectors)
    33→
    34→    for detector, previous_count in previous_open_by_detector.items():
    35→        if detector in import_only_detectors:
    36→            continue
    37→        if current_by_detector.get(detector, 0) > 0:
    38→            continue
    39→        if ran_detectors is not None:
    40→            if detector not in ran_detectors:
    41→                suspect.add(detector)
    42→            continue
    43→        if previous_count >= 3:
    44→            suspect.add(detector)
    45→
    46→    return suspect
    47→
    48→
    49→def _mark_scan_verified(
    50→    issue: dict,
    51→    now: str,
    52→    *,
    53→    note: str,
    54→    attestation_text: str,
    55→) -> None:
    56→    """Record scan corroboration without changing the manual disposition."""
    57→    issue["suppressed"] = False
    58→    issue["suppressed_at"] = None
    59→    issue["suppression_pattern"] = None
    60→    issue["note"] = note
    61→    existing = issue.get("resolution_attestation")
    62→    if not isinstance(existing, dict):
    63→        existing = {}
    64→        issue["resolution_attestation"] = existing
    65→    existing["scan_verified"] = True
    66→    existing["scan_verified_at"] = now
    67→    existing["scan_verification_text"] = attestation_text
    68→
    69→
    70→def verify_disappeared(
    71→    existing: dict,
    72→    current_ids: set[str],
    73→    suspect_detectors: set[str],
    74→    now: str,
    75→    *,
    76→    lang: str | None,
    77→    scan_path: str | None,
    78→    exclude: tuple[str, ...] = (),
    79→) -> tuple[int, int, int, set[str]]:
    80→    """Update scan corroboration for issues absent from scan.
    81→
    82→    Returns (resolved_count, skipped_other_lang, resolved_out_of_scope, changed_detectors).
    83→    Queue-tracked work stays user-controlled: disappearing from scan does not
    84→    change an open issue to resolved. Manually resolved items can be marked as
    85→    scan-verified when they remain absent.
    86→    """
    87→    resolved = skipped_other_lang = resolved_out_of_scope = 0
    88→    resolved_detectors: set[str] = set()
    89→
    90→    for issue_id, previous in existing.items():
    91→        previous_status = previous.get("status")
    92→        if issue_id in current_ids or previous_status not in (
    93→            "open",
    94→            "wontfix",
    95→            "fixed",
    96→            "false_positive",
    97→        ):
    98→            continue
    99→
   100→        if lang and previous.get("lang") and previous["lang"] != lang:
   101→            skipped_other_lang += 1
   102→            continue
   103→
   104→        # Suspect detectors (e.g. 'review') are import-only and must never
   105→        # be auto-resolved by a scan — check this BEFORE the scope filter
   106→        # so that review issues with file="." aren't wrongly resolved as
   107→        # "out of current scan scope".
   108→        if previous.get("detector", "unknown") in suspect_detectors:
   109→            continue
   110→
   111→        if scan_path and scan_path != ".":
   112→            prefix = scan_path.rstrip("/") + "/"
   113→            if (
   114→                not previous["file"].startswith(prefix)
   115→                and previous["file"] != scan_path
   116→            ):
   117→                if previous_status != "open":
   118→                    scope_note = f"Still absent in current scan scope ({scan_path})"
   119→                    _mark_scan_verified(
   120→                        previous,
   121→                        now,
   122→                        note=scope_note,
   123→                        attestation_text=scope_note,
   124→                    )
   125→                    resolved_detectors.add(previous.get("detector", "unknown"))
   126→                    resolved_out_of_scope += 1
   127→                continue
   128→
   129→        if exclude and any(matches_exclusion(previous["file"], ex) for ex in exclude):
   130→            continue
   131→
   132→        if previous_status == "open":
   133→            continue
   134→
   135→        verification_note = (
   136→            "Still absent from scan after manual wontfix"
   137→            if previous_status == "wontfix"
   138→            else "Still absent from scan after manual resolution"
   139→        )
   140→        _mark_scan_verified(
   141→            previous,
   142→            now,
   143→            note=verification_note,
   144→            attestation_text="Absent from detector output in latest scan",
   145→        )
   146→        resolved_detectors.add(previous.get("detector", "unknown"))
   147→        resolved += 1
   148→
   149→    return resolved, skipped_other_lang, resolved_out_of_scope, resolved_detectors
   150→
   151→
   152→def upsert_issues(
   153→    existing: dict,
   154→    current_issues: list[dict],
   155→    ignore: list[str],
   156→    now: str,
   157→    *,
   158→    lang: str | None,
   159→) -> tuple[set[str], int, int, dict[str, int], int, set[str]]:
   160→    """Insert new issues and update existing ones.
   161→
   162→    Returns (current_ids, new_count, reopened_count, by_detector, ignored_count, changed_detectors).
   163→    """
   164→    current_ids: set[str] = set()
   165→    new_count = reopened_count = ignored_count = 0
   166→    by_detector: dict[str, int] = {}
   167→    changed_detectors: set[str] = set()
   168→
   169→    for issue in current_issues:
   170→        issue_id = issue["id"]
   171→        detector = issue.get("detector", "unknown")
   172→        current_ids.add(issue_id)
   173→        by_detector[detector] = by_detector.get(detector, 0) + 1
   174→        matched_ignore = matched_ignore_pattern(issue_id, issue["file"], ignore)
   175→        if matched_ignore:
   176→            ignored_count += 1
   177→
   178→        if lang:
   179→            issue["lang"] = lang
   180→
   181→        if issue_id not in existing:
   182→            existing[issue_id] = dict(issue)
   183→            if matched_ignore:
   184→                existing[issue_id]["suppressed"] = True
   185→                existing[issue_id]["suppressed_at"] = now
   186→                existing[issue_id]["suppression_pattern"] = matched_ignore
   187→                continue
   188→            new_count += 1
   189→            changed_detectors.add(detector)
   190→            continue
   191→
   192→        previous = existing[issue_id]
   193→        previous.update(
   194→            last_seen=now,
   195→            tier=issue["tier"],
   196→            confidence=issue["confidence"],
   197→            summary=issue["summary"],
   198→            detail=issue.get("detail", {}),
   199→        )
   200→        if "zone" in issue:
   201→            previous["zone"] = issue["zone"]
   202→        if lang and not previous.get("lang"):
   203→            previous["lang"] = lang
   204→
   205→        if matched_ignore:
   206→            previous["suppressed"] = True
   207→            previous["suppressed_at"] = now
   208→            previous["suppression_pattern"] = matched_ignore
   209→            continue
   210→
   211→        previous["suppressed"] = False
   212→        previous["suppressed_at"] = None
   213→        previous["suppression_pattern"] = None
   214→
   215→        if previous["status"] in ("fixed", "auto_resolved", "false_positive"):
   216→            # subjective_review issues are condition-based.  When just
   217→            # completed by an agent import, skip reopening to avoid a
   218→            # resolve-then-reopen loop on the same scan cycle.
   219→            if (
   220→                detector == "subjective_review"
   221→                and previous["status"] in {"fixed", "auto_resolved"}
   222→                and (previous.get("resolution_attestation") or {}).get("kind") == "agent_import"
   223→            ):
   224→                continue
   225→            previous_status = previous["status"]
   226→            previous["reopen_count"] = previous.get("reopen_count", 0) + 1
   227→            previous.pop("resolution_attestation", None)
   228→            previous.update(
   229→                status="open",
   230→                resolved_at=None,
   231→                note=(
   232→                    f"Reopened (×{previous['reopen_count']}) "
   233→                    f"— reappeared in scan (was {previous_status})"
   234→                ),
   235→            )
   236→            reopened_count += 1
   237→            changed_detectors.add(detector)
   238→
   239→    return current_ids, new_count, reopened_count, by_detector, ignored_count, changed_detectors
   240→
   241→
   242→__all__ = [
   243→    "verify_disappeared",
   244→    "find_suspect_detectors",
   245→    "upsert_issues",
   246→]
   247→

1→"""Post-scan plan reconciliation — handle issue churn."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     6→from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.annotations import get_issue_note
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES
    10→from desloppify.engine._plan.operations.meta import append_log_entry
    11→from desloppify.engine._plan.operations.lifecycle import clear_focus_if_cluster_empty
    12→from desloppify.engine._plan.operations.skip import resurface_stale_skips
    13→from desloppify.engine._plan.promoted_ids import prune_promoted_ids
    14→from desloppify.engine._plan.reconcile_review_import import (
    15→    ReviewImportSyncResult,
    16→    sync_plan_after_review_import,
    17→)
    18→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
    19→    EPIC_PREFIX,
    20→    PlanModel,
    21→    SupersededEntry,
    22→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    23→)
    24→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
    25→
    26→SUPERSEDED_TTL_DAYS = 90
    27→
    28→
    29→@dataclass
    30→class ReconcileResult:
    31→    """Summary of changes made during reconciliation."""
    32→
    33→    superseded: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    34→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    35→    resurfaced: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    36→    clusters_completed: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    37→    changes: int = 0
    38→
    39→
    40→def _find_candidates(
    41→    state: StateModel, detector: str, file: str
    42→) -> list[str]:
    43→    """Find open issues that could be remaps for a disappeared issue."""
    44→    candidates: list[str] = []
    45→    for fid, issue in state.get("issues", {}).items():
    46→        if issue.get("status") != "open":
    47→            continue
    48→        if issue.get("detector") == detector and issue.get("file") == file:
    49→            candidates.append(fid)
    50→    return candidates
    51→
    52→
    53→def _is_issue_alive(state: StateModel, issue_id: str) -> bool:
    54→    """Return True if the issue exists and is open."""
    55→    issue = state.get("issues", {}).get(issue_id)
    56→    if issue is None:
    57→        return False
    58→    return issue.get("status") == "open"
    59→
    60→
    61→def _supersede_id(
    62→    plan: PlanModel,
    63→    state: StateModel,
    64→    issue_id: str,
    65→    now: str,
    66→) -> bool:
    67→    """Move a disappeared issue to superseded. Returns True if changed."""
    68→    issue = state.get("issues", {}).get(issue_id)
    69→    detector = ""
    70→    file = ""
    71→    summary = ""
    72→    if issue:
    73→        detector = issue.get("detector", "")
    74→        file = issue.get("file", "")
    75→        summary = issue.get("summary", "")
    76→
    77→    candidates = _find_candidates(state, detector, file) if detector else []
    78→    # Don't include the original in candidates
    79→    candidates = [c for c in candidates if c != issue_id]
    80→
    81→    entry: SupersededEntry = {
    82→        "original_id": issue_id,
    83→        "original_detector": detector,
    84→        "original_file": file,
    85→        "original_summary": summary,
    86→        "status": "superseded",
    87→        "superseded_at": now,
    88→        "remapped_to": None,
    89→        "candidates": candidates[:5],
    90→    }
    91→
    92→    # Preserve any existing override note
    93→    override_note = get_issue_note(plan, issue_id)
    94→    if override_note:
    95→        entry["note"] = override_note
    96→
    97→    plan["superseded"][issue_id] = entry
    98→
    99→    # Remove from queue_order, skipped, promoted_ids, cluster issue_ids
   100→    order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   101→    skipped: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
   102→    if issue_id in order:
   103→        order.remove(issue_id)
   104→    skipped.pop(issue_id, None)
   105→    prune_promoted_ids(plan, {issue_id})
   106→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   107→        ids = cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   108→        if issue_id in ids:
   109→            ids.remove(issue_id)
   110→
   111→    # Clear stale cluster reference from override
   112→    override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(issue_id)
   113→    if override and override.get("cluster"):
   114→        override["cluster"] = None
   115→        override["updated_at"] = now
   116→
   117→    return True
   118→
   119→
   120→def _prune_old_superseded(plan: PlanModel, now_dt: datetime) -> list[str]:
   121→    """Remove superseded entries older than TTL. Returns pruned IDs."""
   122→    superseded = plan.get("superseded", {})
   123→    cutoff = now_dt - timedelta(days=SUPERSEDED_TTL_DAYS)
   124→    to_prune: list[str] = []
   125→
   126→    for fid, entry in superseded.items():
   127→        ts = entry.get("superseded_at", "")
   128→        try:
   129→            entry_dt = datetime.fromisoformat(ts)
   130→            if entry_dt.tzinfo is None:
   131→                entry_dt = entry_dt.replace(tzinfo=UTC)
   132→            if entry_dt < cutoff:
   133→                to_prune.append(fid)
   134→        except (ValueError, TypeError):
   135→            to_prune.append(fid)
   136→
   137→    for fid in to_prune:
   138→        superseded.pop(fid, None)
   139→        # Also clean up stale overrides
   140→        plan.get("overrides", {}).pop(fid, None)
   141→
   142→    return to_prune
   143→
   144→
   145→def _referenced_plan_issue_ids(plan: PlanModel) -> set[str]:
   146→    referenced_ids: set[str] = set()
   147→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   148→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   149→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("overrides", {}).keys())
   150→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   151→        referenced_ids.update(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   152→    already_superseded = set(plan.get("superseded", {}).keys())
   153→    return {
   154→        fid for fid in referenced_ids - already_superseded
   155→        if not any(fid.startswith(prefix) for prefix in SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES)
   156→    }
   157→
   158→
   159→def _supersede_dead_references(
   160→    plan: PlanModel,
   161→    state: StateModel,
   162→    *,
   163→    referenced_ids: set[str],
   164→    now: str,
   165→    result: ReconcileResult,
   166→) -> None:
   167→    for fid in sorted(referenced_ids):
   168→        if _is_issue_alive(state, fid):
   169→            continue
   170→        if _supersede_id(plan, state, fid, now):
   171→            result.superseded.append(fid)
   172→            result.changes += 1
   173→
   174→
   175→def _complete_empty_manual_clusters(
   176→    plan: PlanModel,
   177→    *,
   178→    pre_sizes: dict[str, int],
   179→    result: ReconcileResult,
   180→) -> None:
   181→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   182→    for name, prev_size in pre_sizes.items():
   183→        if prev_size == 0:
   184→            continue
   185→        cluster = clusters.get(name)
   186→        if cluster is None or len(cluster.get("issue_ids", [])) != 0:
   187→            continue
   188→        result.clusters_completed.append(name)
   189→        append_log_entry(
   190→            plan,
   191→            "cluster_done",
   192→            issue_ids=[],
   193→            cluster_name=name,
   194→            actor="system",
   195→            detail={"reason": "cluster members no longer actionable in state"},
   196→        )
   197→        result.changes += 1
   198→
   199→
   200→def _reconcile_epic_clusters(
   201→    plan: PlanModel,
   202→    state: StateModel,
   203→    *,
   204→    result: ReconcileResult,
   205→) -> None:
   206→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   207→    epic_names_to_delete: list[str] = []
   208→    for name, cluster in list(clusters.items()):
   209→        if not name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX):
   210→            continue
   211→        issue_ids = cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   212→        alive_ids = [fid for fid in issue_ids if _is_issue_alive(state, fid)]
   213→        if alive_ids != issue_ids:
   214→            cluster["issue_ids"] = alive_ids
   215→            result.changes += 1
   216→        if not alive_ids:
   217→            epic_names_to_delete.append(name)
   218→    for name in epic_names_to_delete:
   219→        clusters.pop(name, None)
   220→        result.changes += 1
   221→
   222→
   223→def reconcile_plan_after_scan(
   224→    plan: PlanModel,
   225→    state: StateModel,
   226→) -> ReconcileResult:
   227→    """Reconcile plan against current state after a scan.
   228→
   229→    Finds IDs referenced in the plan that no longer exist or are no longer
   230→    open, moves them to superseded, and prunes old superseded entries.
   231→    """
   232→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   233→    result = ReconcileResult()
   234→    now = utc_now()
   235→    now_dt = datetime.now(UTC)
   236→
   237→    referenced_ids = _referenced_plan_issue_ids(plan)
   238→
   239→    # Snapshot non-epic cluster sizes before superseding so we can detect
   240→    # clusters that become empty after state reconciliation.
   241→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   242→    pre_sizes: dict[str, int] = {
   243→        name: len(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   244→        for name, cluster in clusters.items()
   245→        if not name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX)
   246→    }
   247→
   248→    _supersede_dead_references(
   249→        plan,
   250→        state,
   251→        referenced_ids=referenced_ids,
   252→        now=now,
   253→        result=result,
   254→    )
   255→    _complete_empty_manual_clusters(plan, pre_sizes=pre_sizes, result=result)
   256→    _reconcile_epic_clusters(plan, state, result=result)
   257→
   258→    clear_focus_if_cluster_empty(plan)
   259→
   260→    # Resurface stale temporary skips
   261→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count", 0)
   262→
   263→    resurfaced = resurface_stale_skips(plan, scan_count)
   264→    if resurfaced:
   265→        result.resurfaced = resurfaced
   266→        result.changes += len(resurfaced)
   267→
   268→    # Prune old superseded entries
   269→    pruned = _prune_old_superseded(plan, now_dt)
   270→    result.pruned = pruned
   271→    result.changes += len(pruned)
   272→
   273→    # Log reconciliation if any changes were made
   274→    if result.changes > 0:
   275→        append_log_entry(
   276→            plan,
   277→            "reconcile",
   278→            issue_ids=result.superseded,
   279→            actor="system",
   280→            detail={
   281→                "superseded_count": len(result.superseded),
   282→                "pruned_count": len(result.pruned),
   283→                "resurfaced_count": len(result.resurfaced),
   284→                "clusters_completed_count": len(result.clusters_completed),
   285→            },
   286→        )
   287→
   288→    return result
   289→
   290→__all__ = [
   291→    "ReconcileResult",
   292→    "ReviewImportSyncResult",
   293→    "reconcile_plan_after_scan",
   294→    "sync_plan_after_review_import",
   295→]
   296→

    28→def write_show_output_file(output_file: str, payload: dict, surfaced_count: int) -> bool:
    29→    """Write serialized show payload to file."""
    30→    try:
    31→        safe_write_text(output_file, json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + "\n")

    40→def group_matches_by_file(matches: list[dict]) -> list[tuple[str, list]]:
    41→    """Group issues by file and sort by descending count."""
    42→    by_file: dict[str, list] = defaultdict(list)
    43→    for issue in matches:
    44→        by_file[issue["file"]].append(issue)
    45→    return sorted(by_file.items(), key=lambda item: -len(item[1]))

    48→def _print_single_issue(issue: dict, *, show_code: bool) -> None:
    49→    """Render a single issue to terminal."""
    50→    normalized_status = canonical_issue_status(issue.get("status"))
    51→    status_icon = {
    52→        "open": "○",
    53→        "fixed": "✓",
    54→        "wontfix": "—",
    55→        "false_positive": "✗",
    56→        "auto_resolved": "◌",

    58→    zone = issue.get("zone", "production")

    60→    print(
    61→        f"    {status_icon} T{issue['tier']} [{issue['confidence']}] {issue['summary']}{zone_tag}"
    62→    )
    63→
    64→    detail_parts = format_detail(issue.get("detail", {}))
    65→    if detail_parts:
    66→        print(colorize(f"      {' · '.join(detail_parts)}", "dim"))
    67→    if show_code:
    68→        detail_raw = issue.get("detail", {})

    70→        target_line = (

    72→        )
    73→        if target_line and issue["file"] not in (".", ""):
    74→            snippet = read_code_snippet(issue["file"], target_line)
    75→            if snippet:

   211→def show_agent_plan(
   212→    narrative: dict, matches: list[dict], *, plan: dict | None = None
   213→) -> None:
   214→    """Render a compact plan from current issues and narrative actions.

   216→    When a living *plan* is active, renders plan focus/progress instead.
   217→    """

   219→        render_plan_with_living_plan(

   236→    if actions:

   238→        print(

   241→                "cyan",

   243→        )
   244→    elif matches:
   245→        first = matches[0]
   246→        print(
   247→            colorize(

   249→                f"inspect and resolve `{first.get('id', '')}`",
   250→                "cyan",
   251→            )
   252→        )
   253→
   254→    if print_ranked_actions(actions):
   255→        print()
   256→
   257→
   258→def show_subjective_followup(
   259→    state: dict, target_strict_score: float, *, objective_backlog: int = 0,
   260→) -> None:
   261→    """Show subjective follow-up guidance for the current state."""
   262→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   263→    if not dim_scores:
   264→        return
   265→
   266→    subjective = reporting_dimensions_mod.scorecard_subjective_entries(
   267→        state,
   268→        dim_scores=dim_scores,

   270→    if not subjective:

   272→
   273→    followup = reporting_dimensions_mod.build_subjective_followup(
   274→        state,
   275→        subjective,
   276→        threshold=target_strict_score,
   277→        max_quality_items=3,
   278→        max_integrity_items=5,
   279→    )
   280→    if print_subjective_followup(followup, objective_backlog=objective_backlog):
   281→        print()

1→"""Section render helpers for markdown planning output."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections import defaultdict
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine.plan_ops import (
     8→    SKIP_KIND_SECTION_LABELS,
     9→    USER_SKIP_KINDS,
    10→    get_issue_description,
    11→    get_issue_note,
    12→)
    13→
    14→
    15→def summary_lines(stats: dict) -> list[str]:
    16→    open_count = stats.get("open", 0)
    17→    total_issues = sum(
    18→        stats.get(key, 0) for key in ("open", "fixed", "wontfix", "auto_resolved")
    19→    )
    20→    addressed = total_issues - open_count
    21→    pct = round(addressed / total_issues * 100) if total_issues else 100
    22→    return [
    23→        f"- **{open_count} open** / {total_issues} total ({pct}% addressed)",
    24→        "",
    25→    ]
    26→
    27→
    28→def addressed_section(issues: dict) -> list[str]:
    29→    addressed = [issue for issue in issues.values() if issue["status"] != "open"]
    30→    if not addressed:
    31→        return []
    32→
    33→    lines: list[str] = ["---", "## Addressed", ""]
    34→    by_status: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
    35→    for issue in addressed:
    36→        by_status[issue["status"]] += 1
    37→    for status, count in sorted(by_status.items()):
    38→        lines.append(f"- **{status}**: {count}")
    39→
    40→    wontfix = [
    41→        issue
    42→        for issue in addressed
    43→        if issue["status"] == "wontfix" and issue.get("note")
    44→    ]
    45→    if wontfix:
    46→        lines.extend(["", "### Wontfix (with explanations)", ""])
    47→        for issue in wontfix[:30]:
    48→            lines.append(f"- `{issue['id']}` — {issue['note']}")
    49→    lines.append("")
    50→    return lines
    51→
    52→
    53→def render_plan_item(item: dict, plan: dict) -> list[str]:
    54→    """Render a single plan item as markdown lines."""
    55→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "medium")
    56→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
    57→    item_id = item.get("id", "")
    58→
    59→    lines = [f"- [ ] [{confidence}] {summary}"]
    60→    description = get_issue_description(plan, item_id)
    61→    if description:
    62→        lines.append(f"      → {description}")
    63→    lines.append(f"      `{item_id}`")
    64→    note = get_issue_note(plan, item_id)
    65→    if note:
    66→        lines.append(f"      Note: {note}")
    67→    return lines
    68→
    69→
    70→def plan_user_ordered_section(
    71→    items: list[dict],
    72→    plan: dict,
    73→) -> list[str]:
    74→    """Render the user-ordered queue section, grouped by cluster."""
    75→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    76→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    77→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
    78→
    79→    ordered_ids = set(queue_order) - skipped_ids
    80→    if not ordered_ids:
    81→        return []
    82→
    83→    by_id = {item.get("id"): item for item in items}
    84→    lines: list[str] = [
    85→        "---",
    86→        f"## User-Ordered Queue ({len(ordered_ids)} items)",
    87→        "",
    88→    ]
    89→
    90→    # Build override-based cluster lookup: override cluster wins over issue_ids membership
    91→    overrides: dict = plan.get("overrides", {})
    92→    effective_cluster: dict[str, str] = {}
    93→    for issue_id, ov in overrides.items():
    94→        ov_cluster = ov.get("cluster")
    95→        if ov_cluster and ov_cluster in clusters:
    96→            effective_cluster[issue_id] = ov_cluster
    97→
    98→    # queue_order is the single source of truth for item ordering
    99→    queue_pos = {qid: i for i, qid in enumerate(queue_order)}
   100→
   101→    # Pre-compute members per cluster, sorted by queue_order position
   102→    cluster_members: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
   103→    cluster_member_sets: dict[str, set[str]] = {}
   104→    for cluster_name, cluster in clusters.items():
   105→        members = [
   106→            issue_id
   107→            for issue_id in cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   108→            if issue_id in ordered_ids
   109→            and issue_id in by_id
   110→            and effective_cluster.get(issue_id, cluster_name) == cluster_name
   111→        ]
   112→        members.sort(key=lambda x: queue_pos.get(x, float("inf")))
   113→        if members:
   114→            cluster_members[cluster_name] = members
   115→            cluster_member_sets[cluster_name] = set(members)
   116→
   117→    # Sort clusters by the queue_order position of their first member
   118→    sorted_cluster_names = sorted(
   119→        cluster_members,
   120→        key=lambda cn: queue_pos.get(cluster_members[cn][0], float("inf")),
   121→    )
   122→
   123→    emitted: set[str] = set()
   124→    for cluster_name in sorted_cluster_names:
   125→        member_ids = [mid for mid in cluster_members[cluster_name] if mid not in emitted]
   126→        if not member_ids:
   127→            continue
   128→        desc = clusters[cluster_name].get("description") or ""
   129→        lines.append(f"### Cluster: {cluster_name}")
   130→        if desc:
   131→            lines.append(f"> {desc}")
   132→        lines.append("")
   133→        for issue_id in member_ids:
   134→            item = by_id.get(issue_id)
   135→            if item:
   136→                lines.extend(render_plan_item(item, plan))
   137→                emitted.add(issue_id)
   138→        lines.append("")
   139→
   140→    unclustered = [
   141→        issue_id
   142→        for issue_id in queue_order
   143→        if issue_id in ordered_ids and issue_id not in emitted and issue_id in by_id
   144→    ]
   145→    if unclustered:
   146→        if any(cluster.get("issue_ids") for cluster in clusters.values()):
   147→            lines.append("### (unclustered ordered items)")
   148→            lines.append("")
   149→        for issue_id in unclustered:
   150→            item = by_id.get(issue_id)
   151→            if item:
   152→                lines.extend(render_plan_item(item, plan))
   153→        lines.append("")
   154→    return lines
   155→
   156→
   157→def plan_skipped_section(items: list[dict], plan: dict) -> list[str]:
   158→    """Render the skipped items section, grouped by kind."""
   159→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   160→    if not skipped:
   161→        return []
   162→
   163→    by_id = {item.get("id"): item for item in items}
   164→
   165→    by_kind: dict[str, list[str]] = {kind: [] for kind in USER_SKIP_KINDS}
   166→    for issue_id, entry in skipped.items():
   167→        kind = entry.get("kind", "temporary")
   168→        by_kind.setdefault(kind, []).append(issue_id)
   169→
   170→    lines: list[str] = [
   171→        "---",
   172→        f"## Skipped ({len(skipped)} items)",
   173→        "",
   174→    ]
   175→
   176→    for kind in USER_SKIP_KINDS:
   177→        ids = by_kind.get(kind, [])
   178→        if not ids:
   179→            continue
   180→        lines.append(f"### {SKIP_KIND_SECTION_LABELS[kind]} ({len(ids)})")
   181→        lines.append("")
   182→        for issue_id in ids:
   183→            entry = skipped.get(issue_id, {})
   184→            item = by_id.get(issue_id)
   185→            if item:
   186→                lines.extend(render_plan_item(item, plan))
   187→            else:
   188→                lines.append(f"- ~~{issue_id}~~ (not in current queue)")
   189→            reason = entry.get("reason")
   190→            if reason:
   191→                lines.append(f"      Reason: {reason}")
   192→            note = entry.get("note")
   193→            if note and not get_issue_note(plan, issue_id):
   194→                lines.append(f"      Note: {note}")
   195→            review_after = entry.get("review_after")
   196→            if review_after:
   197→                skipped_at = entry.get("skipped_at_scan", 0)
   198→                lines.append(f"      Review after: scan {skipped_at + review_after}")
   199→        lines.append("")
   200→    return lines
   201→
   202→
   203→def plan_superseded_section(plan: dict) -> list[str]:
   204→    """Render the superseded items section."""
   205→    superseded = plan.get("superseded", {})
   206→    if not superseded:
   207→        return []
   208→
   209→    lines: list[str] = [
   210→        "---",
   211→        f"## Scan Drift ({len(superseded)} items — may need remap)",
   212→        "",
   213→    ]
   214→    for issue_id, entry in superseded.items():
   215→        summary = entry.get("original_summary", "")
   216→        summary_str = f" — {summary}" if summary else ""
   217→        lines.append(f"- ~~{issue_id}~~{summary_str}")
   218→        candidates = entry.get("candidates", [])
   219→        if candidates:
   220→            lines.append(f"  Candidates: {', '.join(candidates[:3])}")
   221→        note = entry.get("note")
   222→        if note:
   223→            lines.append(f"  Note: {note}")
   224→    lines.append("")
   225→    return lines
   226→
```

    38→def _plan_header(state: PlanState, stats: dict) -> list[str]:
    39→    """Build the plan header: title, score line, and codebase metrics."""
    40→    scores = score_snapshot(state)
    41→    overall_score = scores.overall
    42→    objective_score = scores.objective
    43→    strict_score = scores.strict
    44→
    45→    if (
    46→        overall_score is not None
    47→        and objective_score is not None
    48→        and strict_score is not None
    49→    ):
    50→        header_score = (
    51→            f"**Health:** overall {overall_score:.1f}/100 | "
    52→            f"objective {objective_score:.1f}/100 | "
    53→            f"strict {strict_score:.1f}/100"
    54→        )
    55→    elif overall_score is not None:
    56→        header_score = f"**Score: {overall_score:.1f}/100**"
    57→    else:
    58→        header_score = "**Scores unavailable**"
    59→
    60→    metrics = state.get("codebase_metrics", {})
    61→    total_files = sum(metric.get("total_files", 0) for metric in metrics.values())
    62→    total_loc = sum(metric.get("total_loc", 0) for metric in metrics.values())
    63→    total_dirs = sum(metric.get("total_directories", 0) for metric in metrics.values())
    64→
    65→    lines = [
    66→        f"# Desloppify Plan — {date.today().isoformat()}",
    67→        "",
    68→        f"{header_score} | "
    69→        f"{stats.get('open', 0)} open | "
    70→        f"{stats.get('fixed', 0)} fixed | "
    71→        f"{stats.get('wontfix', 0)} wontfix | "
    72→        f"{stats.get('auto_resolved', 0)} auto-resolved",
    73→        "",
    74→    ]
    75→
    76→    if total_files:
    77→        loc_str = (
    78→            f"{total_loc:,}"
    79→            if total_loc < LOC_COMPACT_THRESHOLD
    80→            else f"{total_loc // 1000}K"
    81→        )
    82→        lines.append(
    83→            f"\n{total_files} files · {loc_str} LOC · {total_dirs} directories\n"
    84→        )
    85→
    86→    return lines
    87→
    88→
    89→def _plan_dimension_table(state: PlanState) -> list[str]:
    90→    """Build the dimension health table rows (empty list when no data)."""
    91→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
    92→    if not dim_scores:
    93→        return []
    94→
    95→    lines = [
    96→        "## Health by Dimension",
    97→        "",
    98→        "| Dimension | Tier | Checks | Issues | Health | Strict | Action |",
    99→        "|-----------|------|--------|--------|--------|--------|--------|",
   100→    ]
   101→    static_names: set[str] = set()
   102→    rendered_names: set[str] = set()
   103→    subjective_display_names = {
   104→        display.lower() for display in DISPLAY_NAMES.values()
   105→    }
   106→
   107→    def _looks_subjective(name: str, data: dict) -> bool:
   108→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
   109→        if "subjective_assessment" in detectors:
   110→            return True
   111→        lowered = name.strip().lower()
   112→        return lowered in subjective_display_names or lowered.startswith("elegance")
   113→
   114→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   115→        ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
   116→        if not ds:
   117→            continue
   118→        static_names.add(dim.name)
   119→        rendered_names.add(dim.name)
   120→        checks = ds.get("checks", 0)
   121→        issues = ds.get("failing", 0)
   122→        score_val = ds.get("score", 100)
   123→        strict_val = ds.get("strict", score_val)
   124→        bold = "**" if score_val < 93 else ""
   125→        action = dimension_action_type(dim.name)
   126→        lines.append(
   127→            f"| {bold}{dim.name}{bold} | T{dim.tier} | "
   128→            f"{checks:,} | {issues} | {score_val:.1f}% | {strict_val:.1f}% | {action} |"
   129→        )
   130→
   131→    from desloppify.engine.planning.dimension_rows import scorecard_dimension_rows
   132→
   133→    scorecard_rows = scorecard_dimension_rows(state)
   134→    scorecard_subjective_rows = [
   135→        (name, ds) for name, ds in scorecard_rows if _looks_subjective(name, ds)
   136→    ]
   137→    scorecard_subjective_names = {name for name, _ in scorecard_subjective_rows}
   138→
   139→    # Show custom dimensions not present in scorecard.png in the main table.
   140→    custom_non_subjective_rows: list[tuple[str, dict]] = []
   141→    for name, ds in sorted(dim_scores.items(), key=lambda item: str(item[0]).lower()):
   142→        if name in rendered_names or not isinstance(ds, dict):
   143→            continue
   144→        if _looks_subjective(name, ds):
   145→            continue
   146→        custom_non_subjective_rows.append((name, ds))
   147→        rendered_names.add(name)
   148→
   149→    for name, ds in custom_non_subjective_rows:
   150→        checks = ds.get("checks", 0)
   151→        issues = ds.get("failing", 0)
   152→        score_val = ds.get("score", 100)
   153→        strict_val = ds.get("strict", score_val)
   154→        tier = int(ds.get("tier", 3) or 3)
   155→        bold = "**" if score_val < 93 else ""
   156→        action = dimension_action_type(name)
   157→        lines.append(
   158→            f"| {bold}{name}{bold} | T{tier} | "
   159→            f"{checks:,} | {issues} | {score_val:.1f}% | {strict_val:.1f}% | {action} |"
   160→        )
   161→
   162→    extra_subjective_rows = [
   163→        (name, ds)
   164→        for name, ds in sorted(

   166→        )
   167→        if (
   168→            isinstance(ds, dict)
   169→            and name not in scorecard_subjective_names
   170→            and name.strip().lower() not in subjective_display_names
   171→            and name.strip().lower() not in {"elegance", "elegance (combined)"}
   172→            and _looks_subjective(name, ds)
   173→        )
   174→    ]
   175→    subjective_rows = [*scorecard_subjective_rows, *extra_subjective_rows]
   176→
   177→    if subjective_rows:
   178→        lines.append("| **Subjective Measures (matches scorecard.png)** | | | | | | |")
   179→        for name, ds in subjective_rows:
   180→            issues = ds.get("failing", 0)
   181→            score_val = ds.get("score", 100)
   182→            strict_val = ds.get("strict", score_val)
   183→            tier = ds.get("tier", 4)
   184→            bold = "**" if score_val < 93 else ""
   185→            lines.append(
   186→                f"| {bold}{name}{bold} | T{tier} | "
   187→                f"— | {issues} | {score_val:.1f}% | {strict_val:.1f}% | review |"
   188→            )
   189→
   190→    lines.append("")
   191→    return lines
   192→
   193→
   194→def _plan_item_sections(
   195→    issues: dict,
   196→    *,
   197→    state: PlanState | None = None,
   198→    plan: dict | None = None,
   199→) -> list[str]:
   200→    """Build per-file sections from the shared work-queue backend."""
   201→    return _plan_item_sections_core(issues, state=state, plan=plan)
   202→
   203→
   204→def _backlog_item_section(items: list[dict]) -> list[str]:
   205→    """Render backlog items as a dedicated markdown section."""
   206→    if not items:
   207→        return []
   208→    lines = ["---", f"## Backlog ({len(items)})", ""]
   209→    lines.extend(_render_queue_item_sections(items, include_header=False))
   210→    return lines
   211→
   212→
   213→def generate_plan_md(state: PlanState, plan: dict | None = None) -> str:
   214→    """Generate a prioritized markdown plan from state.
   215→
   216→    When *plan* is provided (or auto-loaded from disk), user-ordered
   217→    items, clusters, skipped, and superseded sections are rendered.
   218→    When no plan exists, output is identical to the previous behavior.
   219→    """
   220→    issues = state["issues"]
   221→    stats = state.get("stats", {})
   222→
   223→    # Auto-load plan if not provided
   224→    if plan is None:
   225→        try:
   226→            from desloppify.engine.plan_state import load_plan
   227→            plan = load_plan()
   228→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   229→            plan = {}
   230→    if not isinstance(plan, dict):
   231→        plan = {}
   232→    plan.setdefault("queue_order", [])
   233→    plan.setdefault("skipped", {})
   234→    plan.setdefault("clusters", {})
   235→
   236→    has_plan = bool(
   237→        plan
   238→        and (
   239→            plan.get("queue_order")
   240→            or plan.get("skipped")
   241→            or plan.get("clusters")
   242→        )
   243→    )
   244→
   245→    lines = _plan_header(state, stats)
   246→    lines.extend(_plan_dimension_table(state))
   247→    lines.extend(_summary_lines(stats))
   248→
   249→    if has_plan:
   250→        execution_queue = build_execution_queue(
   251→            state,
   252→            options=QueueBuildOptions(
   253→                count=None,
   254→                status="open",
   255→                include_subjective=True,
   256→                plan=plan,
   257→            ),
   258→        )
   259→        execution_items = execution_queue.get("items", [])
   260→        lines.extend(_plan_user_ordered_section(execution_items, plan))
   261→        lines.extend(_plan_item_sections(issues, state=state, plan=plan))
   262→
   263→        backlog_queue = build_backlog_queue(
   264→            state,
   265→            options=QueueBuildOptions(
   266→                count=None,
   267→                status="open",
   268→                include_subjective=True,
   269→                plan=plan,
   270→            ),
   271→        )
   272→        backlog_items = backlog_queue.get("items", [])
   273→        lines.extend(_backlog_item_section(backlog_items))
   274→
   275→        all_items = [*execution_items, *backlog_items]
   276→        lines.extend(_plan_skipped_section(all_items, plan))
   277→        lines.extend(_plan_superseded_section(plan))
   278→    else:
   279→        lines.extend(_plan_item_sections(issues, state=state))
   280→
   281→    lines.extend(_addressed_section(issues))
   282→
   283→    return "\n".join(lines)
   284→
