```
    66→@dataclass
    67→class ReviewPrepareOptions:
    68→    """Configuration bundle for per-file review preparation."""
    69→
    70→    max_files: int | None = None
    71→    max_age_days: int = 30
    72→    force_refresh: bool = True
    73→    dimensions: list[str] | None = None
    74→    config_dimensions: list[str] | None = None
    75→    files: list[str] | None = None
    76→
    77→
    78→@dataclass
    79→class HolisticReviewPrepareOptions:
    80→    """Configuration bundle for holistic review preparation."""
    81→
    82→    dimensions: list[str] | None = None
    83→    files: list[str] | None = None
    84→    include_full_sweep: bool = False
    85→    max_files_per_batch: int | None = None
    86→    include_issue_history: bool = False
    87→    issue_history_max_issues: int = 30
    88→    issue_history_max_batch_items: int = 20
    89→
    90→def _rel_list(s: set[str] | list[str]) -> list[str]:
    91→    """Normalize a set or list of paths to sorted relative paths (max 10)."""
    92→    if isinstance(s, set):
    93→        return sorted(rel(x) for x in s)[:10]
    94→    return [rel(x) for x in list(s)[:10]]
    95→
    96→
    97→def _normalize_max_files(value: Any) -> int | None:
    98→    """Normalize max_files input: None/<=0 means unlimited."""
    99→    if value in (None, ""):
   100→        return None
   101→    parsed = int(value)
   102→    return parsed if parsed > 0 else None
   103→
   104→
   105→def prepare_review(
   106→    path: Path,
   107→    lang: object,
   108→    state: dict,
   109→    options: ReviewPrepareOptions | None = None,
   110→) -> dict[str, object]:
   111→    """Prepare review data for agent consumption. Returns structured dict.
   112→
   113→    If *files* is provided, skip file_finder (avoids redundant filesystem walks
   114→    when the caller already has the file list, e.g. from _setup_lang).
   115→    """
   116→    resolved_options = options or ReviewPrepareOptions()
   117→    resolved_options.max_files = _normalize_max_files(resolved_options.max_files)
   118→    all_files = (
   119→        resolved_options.files
   120→        if resolved_options.files is not None
   121→        else (lang.file_finder(path) if lang.file_finder else [])
   122→    )
   123→
   124→    # Enable file cache for entire prepare operation — context building,
   125→    # file selection, and content extraction all read the same files.
   126→    already_cached = is_file_cache_enabled()
   127→    if not already_cached:
   128→        enable_file_cache()
   129→    try:
   130→        context = build_review_context(path, lang, state, files=all_files)
   131→        selected = select_files_for_review(
   132→            lang,
   133→            path,
   134→            state,
   135→            options=ReviewSelectionOptions(
   136→                max_files=resolved_options.max_files,
   137→                max_age_days=resolved_options.max_age_days,
   138→                force_refresh=resolved_options.force_refresh,
   139→                files=all_files,
   140→            ),
   141→        )
   142→        file_requests = _build_file_requests(selected, lang, state)
   143→    finally:
   144→        if not already_cached:
   145→            disable_file_cache()
   146→
   147→    default_dims, dimension_prompts, system_prompt = load_dimensions_for_lang(lang.name)
   148→    dims = resolve_dimensions(
   149→        cli_dimensions=resolved_options.dimensions,
   150→        config_dimensions=resolved_options.config_dimensions,
   151→        default_dimensions=default_dims,
   152→    )
   153→    lang_guide = get_lang_guidance(lang.name)
   154→    valid_dims = set(dimension_prompts)
   155→    invalid_requested = [
   156→        dim for dim in (resolved_options.dimensions or []) if dim not in valid_dims
   157→    ]
   158→    invalid_config = [
   159→        dim
   160→        for dim in (resolved_options.config_dimensions or [])
   161→        if dim not in valid_dims
   162→    ]
   163→
   164→    return {
   165→        "command": "review",
   166→        "language": lang.name,
   167→        "dimensions": dims,
   168→        "dimension_prompts": {
   169→            d: dimension_prompts[d] for d in dims if d in dimension_prompts
   170→        },
   171→        "lang_guidance": lang_guide,
   172→        "context": serialize_context(context),
   173→        "system_prompt": system_prompt,
   174→        "files": file_requests,
   175→        "total_candidates": len(file_requests),
   176→        "cache_status": {
   177→            "fresh": count_fresh(state, resolved_options.max_age_days),
   178→            "stale": count_stale(state, resolved_options.max_age_days),
   179→            "new": len(file_requests),
   180→        },
   181→        "invalid_dimensions": {
   182→            "requested": invalid_requested,
   183→            "config": invalid_config,
   184→        },
   185→    }
   186→
   187→
   188→def _build_file_requests(files: list[str], lang: object, state: dict) -> list[dict]:
   189→    """Build per-file review request dicts."""
   190→    file_requests = []
   191→    for filepath in files:
   192→        content = read_file_text(abs_path(filepath))
   193→        if content is None:
   194→            continue
   195→
   196→        rpath = rel(filepath)
   197→        zone = "production"
   198→        if lang.zone_map is not None:
   199→            zone = lang.zone_map.get(filepath).value
   200→
   201→        neighbors: dict
   202→        if lang.dep_graph:
   203→            entry = dep_graph_lookup(lang.dep_graph, filepath)
   204→            imports_raw = entry.get("imports", set())
   205→            importers_raw = entry.get("importers", set())
   206→            importer_count_value = importer_count(entry)
   207→            neighbors = {
   208→                "imports": _rel_list(imports_raw),
   209→                "importers": _rel_list(importers_raw),
   210→                "importer_count": importer_count_value,
   211→            }
   212→        else:
   213→            neighbors = {}
   214→
   215→        file_requests.append(
   216→            {
   217→                "file": rpath,
   218→                "content": content,
   219→                "zone": zone,
   220→                "loc": len(content.splitlines()),
   221→                "neighbors": neighbors,
   222→                "existing_issues": get_file_issues(state, filepath),
   223→            }
   224→        )
   225→    return file_requests
   226→
   227→
   228→def prepare_holistic_review(
   229→    path: Path,
   230→    lang: object,
   231→    state: dict,
   232→    options: HolisticReviewPrepareOptions | None = None,
   233→) -> dict[str, object]:
   234→    """Prepare holistic review data for agent consumption. Returns structured dict."""
   235→    resolved_options = options or HolisticReviewPrepareOptions()
   236→    deps = HolisticPrepareDependencies(
   237→        is_file_cache_enabled_fn=is_file_cache_enabled,
   238→        enable_file_cache_fn=enable_file_cache,
   239→        disable_file_cache_fn=disable_file_cache,
   240→        build_holistic_context_fn=build_holistic_context,
   241→        build_review_context_fn=build_review_context,
   242→        load_dimensions_for_lang_fn=load_dimensions_for_lang,
   243→        resolve_dimensions_fn=resolve_dimensions,
   244→        get_lang_guidance_fn=get_lang_guidance,
   245→        assemble_holistic_batches_fn=assemble_holistic_batches,
   246→        holistic_batch_deps=HolisticBatchAssemblyDependencies(
   247→            build_investigation_batches_fn=_build_investigation_batches,
   248→            batch_concerns_fn=_batch_concerns,
   249→            filter_batches_to_dimensions_fn=_filter_batches_to_dimensions,
   250→            append_full_sweep_batch_fn=append_full_sweep_batch,
   251→            log_best_effort_failure_fn=log_best_effort_failure,
   252→            logger=logger,
   253→        ),
   254→        serialize_context_fn=serialize_context,
   255→    )
   256→    return prepare_holistic_review_payload(
   257→        path,
   258→        lang,
   259→        state,
   260→        resolved_options,
   261→        deps=deps,
   262→    )
   263→
```

```
    23→def _resolve_review_files(
    24→    path: Path,
    25→    lang: object,
    26→    options: object,
    27→) -> tuple[list[str], set[str]]:
    28→    """Resolve scoped review files and the allowed-review-file set."""
    29→    discovered_files = (
    30→        options.files
    31→        if options.files is not None
    32→        else (lang.file_finder(path) if lang.file_finder else [])
    33→    )
    34→    allowed = collect_allowed_review_files(discovered_files, lang, base_path=path)
    35→    scoped_files = [
    36→        filepath
    37→        for filepath in discovered_files
    38→        if file_in_allowed_scope(filepath, allowed)
    39→    ]
    40→    return scoped_files, allowed

    43→def _build_review_contexts(
    44→    path: Path,
    45→    lang: object,
    46→    state: dict,
    47→    review_files: list[str],
    48→    *,
    49→    is_file_cache_enabled_fn,
    50→    enable_file_cache_fn,
    51→    disable_file_cache_fn,
    52→    build_holistic_context_fn,
    53→    build_review_context_fn,
    54→) -> tuple[HolisticContext, object]:
    55→    """Build holistic and review contexts, managing the file cache lifecycle."""

    57→    if not already_cached:
    58→        enable_file_cache_fn()
    59→    try:
    60→        context = HolisticContext.from_raw(
    61→            build_holistic_context_fn(path, lang, state, files=review_files)
    62→        )
    63→        review_ctx = build_review_context_fn(path, lang, state, files=review_files)
    64→    finally:
    65→        if not already_cached:
    66→            disable_file_cache_fn()
    67→    return context, review_ctx

   117→    invalid_requested = [

   121→    return _DimensionContext(

   132→def prepare_holistic_review_payload(
   133→    path: Path,
   134→    lang: object,
   135→    state: dict,
   136→    options,

   138→    deps: HolisticPrepareDependencies,
   139→) -> dict[str, object]:

   203→    batches = _attach_issue_history_context(

   211→    # Attach accumulated dimension contexts to each batch

   213→    if isinstance(dim_contexts, dict) and dim_contexts:
```

```
1→"""Dismissal helpers for cluster triage plan mutations."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from .prompt import TriageResult
     6→
     7→
     8→def _triaged_out_payload(
     9→    *,
    10→    issue_id: str,
    11→    reason: str,
    12→    note: str | None,
    13→    now: str,
    14→    scan_count: int,
    15→) -> dict:
    16→    return {
    17→        "issue_id": issue_id,
    18→        "kind": "triaged_out",
    19→        "reason": reason,
    20→        "note": note,
    21→        "attestation": None,
    22→        "created_at": now,
    23→        "review_after": None,
    24→        "skipped_at_scan": scan_count,
    25→    }
    26→
    27→
    28→def dismiss_triage_issues(
    29→    *,
    30→    triage: TriageResult,
    31→    order: list[str],
    32→    skipped: dict,
    33→    now: str,
    34→    version: int,
    35→    scan_count: int,
    36→) -> tuple[list[str], int]:
    37→    """Move triage-dismissed issues out of queue and into skipped metadata."""
    38→    dismissed_ids: list[str] = []
    39→    dismiss_count = 0
    40→    for dismissed in triage.dismissed_issues:
    41→        issue_id = dismissed.issue_id
    42→        dismissed_ids.append(issue_id)
    43→        if issue_id in order:
    44→            order.remove(issue_id)
    45→        skipped[issue_id] = _triaged_out_payload(
    46→            issue_id=issue_id,
    47→            reason=dismissed.reason,
    48→            note=f"Dismissed by epic triage v{version}",
    49→            now=now,
    50→            scan_count=scan_count,
    51→        )
    52→        dismiss_count += 1
    53→
    54→    for epic_data in triage.clusters:
    55→        for issue_id in epic_data.get("dismissed", []):
    56→            if issue_id in dismissed_ids or issue_id not in order:
    57→                continue
    58→            order.remove(issue_id)
    59→            dismissed_ids.append(issue_id)
    60→            skipped[issue_id] = _triaged_out_payload(
    61→                issue_id=issue_id,
    62→                reason=f"Dismissed by epic triage v{version}",
    63→                note=None,
    64→                now=now,
    65→                scan_count=scan_count,
    66→            )
    67→            dismiss_count += 1
    68→
    69→    return dismissed_ids, dismiss_count
    70→
    71→
    72→__all__ = ["dismiss_triage_issues"]
    73→
```

```
    63→
    64→
    65→def _render_context_update_example() -> str:
    66→    """Render a concrete context_updates example from the schema file."""

    68→        schema = _load_context_schema()
    69→        example = schema.get("example")

    73→    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError, KeyError):

    91→        '      "sub_axes": {"abstraction_leverage": 0-100, "indirection_cost": 0-100, "interface_honesty": 0-100, "delegation_density": 0-100, "definition_directness": 0-100, "type_discipline": 0-100}  // required for abstraction_fitness when evidence supports it; all one decimal place\n'
    92→        "    }\n"
    93→        "  },\n"
    94→        '  "dimension_judgment": {\n'
    95→        '    "<dimension>": {\n'
    96→        '      "strengths": ["0-5 specific things the codebase does well from this dimension\'s perspective"],\n'
    97→        '      "issue_character": "one sentence characterizing the nature/pattern of issues from this dimension\'s perspective",\n'
    98→        '      "score_rationale": "2-3 sentences explaining the score from this dimension\'s perspective, referencing global anchors"\n'
    99→        "    }  // required for every assessed dimension; do not omit\n"
   100→        "  },\n"
   101→        '  "issues": [{\n'
   102→        '    "dimension": "<dimension>",\n'
   103→        '    "identifier": "short_id",\n'
   104→        '    "summary": "one-line defect summary",\n'
   105→        '    "related_files": ["relative/path.py"],\n'
   106→        '    "evidence": ["specific code observation"],\n'
   107→        '    "suggestion": "concrete fix recommendation",\n'
   108→        '    "confidence": "high|medium|low",\n'
   109→        '    "impact_scope": "local|module|subsystem|codebase",\n'
   110→        '    "fix_scope": "single_edit|multi_file_refactor|architectural_change",\n'
   111→        '    "root_cause_cluster": "optional_cluster_name_when_supported_by_history",\n'
   112→        '    "concern_verdict": "confirmed|dismissed  // for concern signals only",\n'
   113→        '    "concern_fingerprint": "abc123  // required when dismissed; copy from signal fingerprint",\n'
   114→        '    "reasoning": "why dismissed  // optional, for dismissed only"\n'
   115→        "  }],\n"
   116→        '  "retrospective": {\n'
   117→        '    "root_causes": ["optional: concise root-cause hypotheses"],\n'
   118→        '    "likely_symptoms": ["optional: identifiers that look symptom-level"],\n'
   119→        '    "possible_false_positives": ["optional: prior concept keys likely mis-scoped"]\n'
   120→        "  },\n"
   121→        '  "context_updates": {\n'
   122→        '    "<dimension>": {\n'
   123→        '      "add": [{"header": "short label", "description": "why this is the way it is", "settled": true|false}],\n'
   124→        '      "remove": ["header of insight to remove"],\n'
   125→        '      "settle": ["header of insight to mark as settled"],\n'
   126→        '      "unsettle": ["header of insight to unsettle"]\n'
   127→        "    }  // omit context_updates entirely if no changes\n"
   128→        "  }\n"
   129→        "}\n"
   130→        + _render_context_update_example()
   131→    )
   132→
   133→def render_batch_prompt(
   134→    *,
   135→    repo_root: Path,
   136→    packet_path: Path,
   137→    batch_index: int,
   138→    batch: dict[str, object],
   139→    policy_block: str = "",
   140→) -> str:
   141→    """Render one subagent prompt for a holistic investigation batch."""
   142→    context = build_batch_context(batch, batch_index)
   143→    dim_prompts = context.dimension_prompts or batch_dimension_prompts(batch)
   144→    dimension_contexts = batch.get("dimension_contexts") if isinstance(batch, dict) else None
   145→    return join_non_empty_sections(
   146→        _render_metadata_block(
   147→            repo_root=repo_root,
   148→            packet_path=packet_path,
   149→            batch_index=batch_index,
   150→            context=context,
   151→        ),
   152→        render_dimension_prompts_block(context.dimensions, dim_prompts),
   153→        policy_block,
   154→        render_scoring_frame(),
   155→        render_dimension_context_block(
   156→            context.dimensions,
   157→            dimension_contexts if isinstance(dimension_contexts, dict) else {},
   158→        ),
   159→        render_scan_evidence_note(),
   160→        render_seed_files_block(context),
   161→        render_historical_focus(batch),
   162→        render_mechanical_concern_signals(batch),
   163→        render_judgment_findings_section(batch),
   164→        render_task_requirements(issues_cap=context.issues_cap, dim_set=context.dimension_set),
   165→        render_scope_enums(),
   166→        _render_output_schema(context, batch_index),
   167→    )
   168→
   169→
   170→__all__ = ["render_batch_prompt"]
```

```
    65→def _utc_now() -> datetime:
    66→    return datetime.now(UTC)
    67→
    68→
    69→def _iso_seconds(dt: datetime) -> str:
    70→    return dt.isoformat(timespec="seconds")
    71→
    72→
    73→def _parse_iso(raw: object) -> datetime | None:
    74→    if not isinstance(raw, str) or not raw.strip():
    75→        return None
    76→    try:
    77→        dt = datetime.fromisoformat(raw)
    78→    except ValueError:
    79→        return None
    80→    if dt.tzinfo is None:
    81→        return dt.replace(tzinfo=UTC)
    82→    return dt.astimezone(UTC)
    83→
    84→
    85→def _session_id() -> str:

    93→def _session_file(session_id: str) -> Path:
    94→    return _session_dir(session_id) / "session.json"

    96→
    97→def _validate_session_id(session_id: str) -> None:
    98→    if not session_id.strip():
    99→        raise CommandError("Error: --session-id is required.", exit_code=2)
   100→    invalid_chars = {"/", "\\", ".."}
   101→    if any(part in session_id for part in invalid_chars):
   102→        raise CommandError("Error: invalid --session-id value.", exit_code=2)
   103→
   104→
   105→def _load_json_object(path: Path, *, label: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
   106→    if not path.exists():
   107→        raise CommandError(f"Error: {label} not found: {path}")
   108→    try:
   109→        payload = json.loads(path.read_text())
   110→    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
   111→        raise CommandError(f"Error: failed reading {label}: {exc}") from exc
   112→    if not isinstance(payload, dict):
   113→        raise CommandError(f"Error: {label} must contain a JSON object.")
   114→    return payload
   115→
   116→
   117→def _session_payload(session_id: str) -> tuple[Path, dict[str, Any]]:
   118→    _validate_session_id(session_id)
   119→    path = _session_file(session_id)
   120→    payload = _load_json_object(path, label="session")
   121→    payload_id = str(payload.get("session_id", "")).strip()
   122→    if payload_id != session_id:
   123→        raise CommandError(
   124→            f"Error: session id mismatch in {path} (expected {session_id}, found {payload_id or '<missing>'}).",
   125→        )
   126→    return path, payload
   127→
   128→
   129→def _prepare_packet_snapshot(
   130→    args,
   131→    state: dict,
   132→    lang,
   133→    *,
   134→    config: dict[str, Any],
   135→) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], Path, Path]:
   136→    """Prepare holistic review packet and persist immutable+blind snapshots."""
   137→    context = resolve_review_packet_context(args)
   138→    next_command = build_external_submit_next_command(context)
   139→    try:
   140→        packet = build_review_packet_payload(
   141→            state=state,
   142→            lang=lang,
   143→            config=config,
   144→            context=context,
   145→            next_command=next_command,
   146→            setup_lang_fn=setup_lang_concrete,
   147→        )
   148→    except ValueError as exc:
   149→        msg = str(exc).strip()
   150→        if not msg:
   151→            msg = f"no files found at path '{context.path}'. Nothing to review."
   152→        raise CommandError(msg, exit_code=1) from exc
   153→    write_query(packet)
   154→
   155→    stamp = run_stamp()
   156→    blind_packet_path = _blind_packet_path()
   157→    packet_path, blind_path = write_review_packet_snapshot(
   158→        packet,
   159→        stamp=stamp,

   161→        blind_path_override=blind_packet_path,
   162→        safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,

   164→    return packet, packet_path, blind_path

   166→
   167→def _build_template_payload(packet: dict[str, Any], *, session_id: str, token: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
   168→    dimensions = [

   171→        if isinstance(dim, str) and dim.strip()

   173→    return {

   175→            "id": session_id,
   176→            "token": token,
   177→        },

   184→def _build_claude_launch_prompt(
   185→    *,

   190→    output_path: Path,
   191→    packet: dict[str, Any],
   192→) -> str:
   193→    """Build a copy/paste-ready prompt for a Claude blind reviewer subagent."""
   194→    header = (
   195→        "# Claude Blind Reviewer Launch Prompt\n\n"
   196→        "You are an isolated blind reviewer. Do not use prior chat context, "
   197→        "prior score history, or target-score anchoring.\n\n"
   198→        f"Session id: {session_id}\n"
   199→        f"Session token: {token}\n"

   201→        f"Template JSON: {template_path}\n"
   202→        f"Output JSON path: {output_path}\n\n"
   203→    )

   206→    if not isinstance(raw_batches, list):
   207→        raw_batches = []
   208→    raw_dim_prompts = packet.get("dimension_prompts")
   209→    dim_prompts: dict[str, dict[str, object]] = (
   210→        raw_dim_prompts if isinstance(raw_dim_prompts, dict) else {}
   211→    )
   212→    batches = explode_to_single_dimension(
   213→        [b for b in raw_batches if isinstance(b, dict)],
   214→        dimension_prompts=dim_prompts or None,
   215→    )
   216→
   217→    all_dims: set[str] = set()
   218→    combined_cap = 0
   219→    batch_sections: list[str] = []
   220→    for i, batch in enumerate(batches):
   221→        ctx = build_batch_context(batch, i)
   222→        all_dims.update(ctx.dimension_set)
   223→        combined_cap += ctx.issues_cap
   224→
   225→        section = (
   226→            f"--- Batch {i + 1}: {ctx.name} ---\n"
   227→            f"Rationale: {ctx.rationale}\n"
   228→        )
   229→        section += render_dimension_prompts_block(
   230→            ctx.dimensions,
   231→            ctx.dimension_prompts or dim_prompts,
   232→        )
   233→        section += render_seed_files_block(ctx)
   234→        section += render_historical_focus(batch)
   235→        section += render_mechanical_concern_signals(batch)
   236→        section += render_judgment_findings_section(batch)
   237→        batch_sections.append(section)
   238→
   239→    if not combined_cap:
   240→        combined_cap = 10
   241→
   242→    output_schema = (
   243→        "Output schema:\n"
   244→        "{\n"
   245→        '  "session": {"id": "<preserve from template>", "token": "<preserve from template>"},\n'
   246→        '  "assessments": {"<dimension>": <0-100 with one decimal place>},\n'
   247→        '  "dimension_notes": {\n'

   268→        '    "reasoning": "why dismissed  // optional, for dismissed only"\n'

   271→    )
   272→
   273→    session_requirements = (

   276→        f"2. Keep `session.token` exactly `{token}`.\n"

   278→    )
   279→

   426→
   427→def _canonical_external_payload(
   428→    raw_payload: dict[str, Any],
   429→    *,
   430→    session: dict[str, Any],
   431→) -> dict[str, Any]:
   432→    """Return import payload with canonical provenance and required session token."""
   433→    session_meta = raw_payload.get("session")
   434→    if not isinstance(session_meta, dict):
   435→        raise CommandError(
   436→            "Error: external reviewer payload must include top-level `session` object."
   437→            ' Expected: {"session":{"id":"...","token":"..."},"assessments":{...},"issues":[...]}',
   438→        )
   439→
   440→    payload_id = str(session_meta.get("id", "")).strip()
   441→    payload_token = str(session_meta.get("token", "")).strip()
   442→    expected_id = str(session.get("session_id", "")).strip()
   443→    expected_token = str(session.get("token", "")).strip()
   444→    if payload_id != expected_id or payload_token != expected_token:
   445→        raise CommandError(
   446→            "Error: session id/token mismatch in external reviewer payload."
   447→            " Regenerate output using the session template/instructions.",
   448→        )
   449→
   450→    payload = {
   451→        key: value
   452→        for key, value in raw_payload.items()
   453→        if key not in {"session", "provenance"}
   454→    }
   455→    payload["provenance"] = {
   456→        "kind": "blind_review_batch_import",
   457→        "blind": True,
   458→        "runner": str(session.get("runner", "claude")),
   459→        "session_id": str(session.get("session_id", "")),
   460→        "created_at": _iso_seconds(_utc_now()),
   461→        "packet_path": str(session.get("blind_packet_path", "")),
   462→        "packet_sha256": str(session.get("packet_sha256", "")),
   463→    }
   464→    return payload
   465→
   466→
   467→def _ensure_session_open(session: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   468→    status = str(session.get("status", "open")).strip().lower()
   469→    if status == "open":
   470→        return
   471→    raise CommandError(
   472→        f"Error: session is not open (status={status or 'unknown'}). Start a new session with --external-start.",
   473→    )
   474→
   475→
   476→def _ensure_session_not_expired(session: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   477→    expires_at = _parse_iso(session.get("expires_at"))
   478→    if expires_at is None:
   479→        raise CommandError(
   480→            "Error: session metadata is missing/invalid expires_at.",
   481→        )
   482→    now = _utc_now()
   483→    if now <= expires_at:
   484→        return
   485→    raise CommandError(
   486→        f"Error: session expired at {session.get('expires_at')}. Start a new session with --external-start.",
   487→    )
   488→
   489→
   490→def do_external_submit(
   491→    *,
   492→    import_file: str,
   493→    session_id: str,
   494→    state: dict,
   495→    lang,
   496→    state_file,
   497→    config: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
   498→    allow_partial: bool = False,
   499→    scan_after_import: bool = False,
   500→    scan_path: str = ".",
   501→    dry_run: bool = False,
   502→) -> None:
   503→    """Submit external reviewer output via session, adding canonical provenance."""
   504→    config = config or {}
   505→    session_path, session = _session_payload(session_id)
   506→    _ensure_session_open(session)
   507→    _ensure_session_not_expired(session)
   508→
   509→    if str(session.get("runner", "")).strip().lower() not in _EXTERNAL_SUPPORTED_RUNNERS:
   510→        raise CommandError(
   511→            "Error: only Claude external sessions currently support durable score submit.",
   512→        )
   513→
   514→    issues_path = Path(import_file)
   515→    raw_payload = _load_json_object(issues_path, label="external issues")
   516→    canonical_payload = _canonical_external_payload(raw_payload, session=session)
   517→
   518→    stamp = run_stamp()
   519→    session_dir = session_path.parent
   520→    canonical_path = session_dir / f"canonical_import_{stamp}.json"
   521→    safe_write_text(canonical_path, json.dumps(canonical_payload, indent=2) + "\n")
   522→
   523→    _import_config = ReviewImportConfig(
   524→        config=config,
   525→        allow_partial=allow_partial,
   526→        attested_external=True,
   527→        manual_attest=str(session.get("attest", EXTERNAL_ATTEST_TEXT)),
   528→    )
   529→
   530→    if dry_run:
   531→        do_validate_import(
   532→            str(canonical_path),
   533→            lang,
   534→            import_config=_import_config,
   535→        )
   536→        return
   537→
   538→    do_import(
   539→        str(canonical_path),
   540→        state,
   541→        lang,
   542→        state_file,
   543→        import_config=_import_config,
   544→    )
   545→
   546→    submitted_at = _iso_seconds(_utc_now())
   547→    session["status"] = "submitted"
   548→    session["submitted_at"] = submitted_at
   549→    session["submitted_input_file"] = str(issues_path)
   550→    session["submitted_canonical_file"] = str(canonical_path)
   551→    safe_write_text(session_path, json.dumps(session, indent=2) + "\n")
   552→
   553→    if scan_after_import:
   554→        code = run_followup_scan(
   555→            lang_name=lang.name,
   556→            scan_path=scan_path,
   557→            deps=FollowupScanDeps(
   558→                project_root=_runtime_project_root(),
   559→                timeout_seconds=FOLLOWUP_SCAN_TIMEOUT_SECONDS,
   560→                python_executable=sys.executable,
   561→                subprocess_run=subprocess.run,
   562→                timeout_error=subprocess.TimeoutExpired,
   563→                colorize_fn=colorize,
   564→            ),
   565→        )
   566→        if code != 0:
   567→            raise CommandError(f"External review exited with code {code}", exit_code=code)
   568→
   569→
   570→__all__ = ["do_external_start", "do_external_submit", "EXTERNAL_ATTEST_TEXT"]
   571→
```

```
   166→def require_non_empty_packet(packet: dict[str, Any], *, path: Path) -> int:
   167→    """Return packet total_files, raising ValueError when no reviewable files exist."""
   168→    total = packet.get("total_files", 0)
   169→    if isinstance(total, bool) or not isinstance(total, int):
   170→        raise ValueError(
   171→            f"invalid review packet shape for path '{path}': total_files must be an integer"
   172→        )
   173→    if total <= 0:
   174→        raise ValueError(f"no files found at path '{path}'. Nothing to review.")
   175→    return total

   197→    packet["config"] = redacted_review_config(config)
   198→    packet["next_command"] = next_command

   205→    *,
   206→    stamp: str,
   207→    project_root_override: Path | None = None,
   208→    review_packet_dir_override: Path | None = None,
   209→    blind_path_override: Path | None = None,
   210→    safe_write_text_fn,
   211→) -> tuple[Path, Path]:
   212→    """Persist immutable + blind packet snapshots and return their paths."""
   213→    runtime_root = runtime_project_root(project_root_override=project_root_override)
   214→    blind_path = blind_path_override or blind_packet_path(
   215→        project_root_override=runtime_root,
   216→        stamp=stamp,
   217→    )
   218→    packet_dir = review_packet_dir(
   219→        project_root_override=runtime_root,
   220→        review_packet_dir_override=review_packet_dir_override,
   221→    )
   222→    return runner_packets_mod.write_packet_snapshot(
   223→        packet,
   224→        stamp=stamp,
   225→        review_packet_dir=packet_dir,
   226→        blind_path=blind_path,
   227→        safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text_fn,
   228→    )
   229→
   230→
   231→__all__ = [
   232→    "ReviewPacketContext",
   233→    "build_external_submit_next_command",
   234→    "build_holistic_packet",
```

```
1→"""Prepare flow for review command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.query import write_query
     6→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import CommandError
     7→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     8→
     9→from .packet.build import (
    10→    build_review_packet_payload,
    11→    build_run_batches_next_command,
    12→    resolve_review_packet_context,
    13→)
    14→from .runtime.setup import setup_lang_concrete
    15→
    16→
    17→def do_prepare(
    18→    args,
    19→    state,
    20→    lang,
    21→    _state_path,
    22→    *,
    23→    config: dict,
    24→) -> None:
    25→    """Prepare mode: holistic-only review packet in query.json."""
    26→    context = resolve_review_packet_context(args)
    27→    next_command = build_run_batches_next_command(context)
    28→
    29→    # Keep prepare and external-start on one packet-construction contract.
    30→    try:
    31→        data = build_review_packet_payload(
    32→            state=state,
    33→            lang=lang,
    34→            config=config,
    35→            context=context,
    36→            next_command=next_command,
    37→            setup_lang_fn=setup_lang_concrete,
    38→        )
    39→    except ValueError as exc:
    40→        msg = str(exc).strip()
    41→        if not msg:
    42→            msg = f"no files found at path '{context.path}'. Nothing to review."
    43→        scan_path = state.get("scan_path") if isinstance(state, dict) else None
    44→        if scan_path:
    45→            msg += (
    46→                f"\nHint: your last scan used --path {scan_path}. "
    47→                f"Try: desloppify review --prepare --path {scan_path}"
    48→            )
    49→        else:
    50→            msg += "\nHint: pass --path <dir> matching the path used during scan."
    51→        raise CommandError(msg, exit_code=1) from exc
    52→
    53→    write_query(data)
    54→    _print_prepare_summary(
    55→        data,
    56→        next_command=next_command,
    57→        retrospective=context.retrospective,
    58→    )
    59→
    60→
    61→def _print_prepare_summary(
    62→    data: dict, *, next_command: str, retrospective: bool,
    63→) -> None:
    64→    """Print the prepare summary to the terminal."""
    65→    total = data.get("total_files", 0)
    66→    batches = data.get("investigation_batches", [])
    67→    print(colorize(f"\n  Holistic review prepared: {total} files in codebase", "bold"))
    68→    if retrospective:
    69→        print(
    70→            colorize(
    71→                "  Retrospective context enabled: historical review issues injected into packet.",
    72→                "dim",
    73→            )
    74→        )
    75→    if batches:
    76→        print(
    77→            colorize(
    78→                "\n  Investigation batches (independent — can run in parallel):", "bold"
    79→            )
    80→        )
    81→        for i, batch in enumerate(batches, 1):
    82→            n_files = len(batch["files_to_read"])
    83→            print(
    84→                colorize(
    85→                    f"    {i}. {batch['name']} ({n_files} files) — {batch['why']}",
    86→                    "dim",
    87→                )
    88→            )
    89→    print(colorize("\n  Workflow:", "bold"))
    90→    for step_i, step in enumerate(data.get("workflow", []), 1):
    91→        print(colorize(f"    {step_i}. {step}", "dim"))
    92→    n_batches = len(data.get("investigation_batches", []))
    93→    print(colorize("\n  AGENT PLAN — pick the path matching your runner:", "yellow"))
    94→    print(
    95→        colorize(
    96→            "  1. Codex: `desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import`",
    97→            "dim",
    98→        )
    99→    )

   104→            f" Launch {n_batches} subagents in parallel (one per prompt),"

   107→            "dim",

   110→    print(
   111→        colorize(
   112→            "  3. Cloud/external: `desloppify review --external-start --external-runner claude` → follow template → `--external-submit`",
   113→            "dim",
   114→        )
   115→    )
   116→    print(
   117→        colorize(
   118→            "  4. Issues-only fallback: `desloppify review --import issues.json`",
```

```
1→"""Payload assembly helpers for holistic review orchestration."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any
     6→
     7→from desloppify.intelligence.review._prepare.issue_history import (
     8→    ReviewHistoryOptions,
     9→    build_batch_issue_focus,
    10→    build_issue_history_context,
    11→)
    12→
    13→from .prepare_holistic_scope import filter_batches_to_file_scope
    14→
    15→
    16→def _build_selected_prompts(
    17→    dims: list[str],
    18→    holistic_prompts: dict[str, Any],
    19→    per_file_prompts: dict[str, Any],
    20→) -> dict[str, dict[str, object]]:
    21→    """Build the dimension-to-prompt mapping, preferring holistic prompts."""
    22→    selected: dict[str, dict[str, object]] = {}
    23→    for dim in dims:
    24→        prompt = holistic_prompts.get(dim)
    25→        if prompt is None:
    26→            prompt = per_file_prompts.get(dim)
    27→        if prompt is None:
    28→            continue
    29→        selected[dim] = prompt
    30→    return selected
    31→
    32→
    33→def _attach_issue_history_context(
    34→    payload: dict[str, Any],
    35→    batches: list[dict[str, Any]],
    36→    state: dict,
    37→    options: object,
    38→    allowed_review_files: set[str],
    39→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    40→    """Attach issue history to payload and per-batch focus; re-scope batches."""
    41→    if not options.include_issue_history:
    42→        return batches
    43→    history_payload = build_issue_history_context(
    44→        state,
    45→        options=ReviewHistoryOptions(
    46→            max_issues=options.issue_history_max_issues,
    47→        ),
    48→    )
    49→    payload["historical_review_issues"] = history_payload
    50→    for batch in batches:
    51→        if not isinstance(batch, dict):
    52→            continue
    53→        batch_dims = batch.get("dimensions", [])
    54→        batch["historical_issue_focus"] = build_batch_issue_focus(
    55→            history_payload,
    56→            dimensions=batch_dims,
    57→            max_items=options.issue_history_max_batch_items,
    58→        )
    59→    return filter_batches_to_file_scope(
    60→        batches,
    61→        allowed_files=allowed_review_files,
    62→    )
    63→
    64→
    65→__all__ = ["_attach_issue_history_context", "_build_selected_prompts"]
    66→
```

```
1→"""Orchestration helpers for holistic review payload preparation."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→from typing import Any
     8→
     9→from desloppify.intelligence.review._context.models import HolisticContext
    10→from desloppify.intelligence.review._prepare.helpers import HOLISTIC_WORKFLOW
    11→
    12→from .prepare_holistic_batches import HolisticBatchAssemblyDependencies
    13→from .prepare_holistic_payload_parts import (
    14→    _attach_issue_history_context,
    15→    _build_selected_prompts,
    16→)
    17→from .prepare_holistic_scope import (
    18→    collect_allowed_review_files,
    19→    file_in_allowed_scope,
    20→)
    21→
    22→
    23→def _resolve_review_files(
    24→    path: Path,
    25→    lang: object,
    26→    options: object,
    27→) -> tuple[list[str], set[str]]:
    28→    """Resolve scoped review files and the allowed-review-file set."""
    29→    discovered_files = (
    30→        options.files
    31→        if options.files is not None
    32→        else (lang.file_finder(path) if lang.file_finder else [])
    33→    )
    34→    allowed = collect_allowed_review_files(discovered_files, lang, base_path=path)
    35→    scoped_files = [
    36→        filepath
    37→        for filepath in discovered_files
    38→        if file_in_allowed_scope(filepath, allowed)
    39→    ]
    40→    return scoped_files, allowed
    41→
    42→
    43→def _build_review_contexts(
    44→    path: Path,
    45→    lang: object,
    46→    state: dict,
    47→    review_files: list[str],
    48→    *,
    49→    is_file_cache_enabled_fn,
    50→    enable_file_cache_fn,
    51→    disable_file_cache_fn,
    52→    build_holistic_context_fn,
    53→    build_review_context_fn,
    54→) -> tuple[HolisticContext, object]:
    55→    """Build holistic and review contexts, managing the file cache lifecycle."""
    56→    already_cached = is_file_cache_enabled_fn()
    57→    if not already_cached:
    58→        enable_file_cache_fn()
    59→    try:
    60→        context = HolisticContext.from_raw(
    61→            build_holistic_context_fn(path, lang, state, files=review_files)
    62→        )
    63→        review_ctx = build_review_context_fn(path, lang, state, files=review_files)
    64→    finally:
    65→        if not already_cached:
    66→            disable_file_cache_fn()
    67→    return context, review_ctx
    68→
    69→
    70→@dataclass
    71→class _DimensionContext:
    72→    """Resolved dimension configuration for holistic review."""
    73→
    74→    dims: list[str]
    75→    holistic_prompts: dict[str, Any]
    76→    per_file_prompts: dict[str, Any]
    77→    system_prompt: str
    78→    lang_guide: str
    79→    invalid_requested: list[str]
    80→    invalid_default: list[str]
    81→
    82→
    83→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    84→class HolisticPrepareDependencies:
    85→    """Injected collaborators used by holistic payload orchestration."""
    86→
    87→    is_file_cache_enabled_fn: object
    88→    enable_file_cache_fn: object
    89→    disable_file_cache_fn: object
    90→    build_holistic_context_fn: object
    91→    build_review_context_fn: object
    92→    load_dimensions_for_lang_fn: object
    93→    resolve_dimensions_fn: object
    94→    get_lang_guidance_fn: object
    95→    assemble_holistic_batches_fn: object
    96→    holistic_batch_deps: HolisticBatchAssemblyDependencies
    97→    serialize_context_fn: object
    98→
    99→
   100→def _resolve_dimension_context(
   101→    lang_name: str,
   102→    options: object,
   103→    *,
   104→    load_dimensions_for_lang_fn,
   105→    resolve_dimensions_fn,
   106→    get_lang_guidance_fn,
   107→) -> _DimensionContext:
   108→    """Load, resolve, and validate dimensions for the review."""
   109→    default_dims, holistic_prompts, system_prompt = load_dimensions_for_lang_fn(lang_name)
   110→    _, per_file_prompts, _ = load_dimensions_for_lang_fn(lang_name)
   111→    dims = resolve_dimensions_fn(
   112→        cli_dimensions=options.dimensions,
   113→        default_dimensions=default_dims,
   114→    )
   115→    lang_guide = get_lang_guidance_fn(lang_name)
   116→    valid_dims = set(holistic_prompts) | set(per_file_prompts)
   117→    invalid_requested = [
   118→        dim for dim in (options.dimensions or []) if dim not in valid_dims
   119→    ]
   120→    invalid_default = [dim for dim in default_dims if dim not in valid_dims]
   121→    return _DimensionContext(
   122→        dims=dims,
   123→        holistic_prompts=holistic_prompts,
   124→        per_file_prompts=per_file_prompts,
   125→        system_prompt=system_prompt,
   126→        lang_guide=lang_guide,
   127→        invalid_requested=invalid_requested,
   128→        invalid_default=invalid_default,
   129→    )
   130→
   131→
   132→def prepare_holistic_review_payload(
   133→    path: Path,
   134→    lang: object,
   135→    state: dict,
   136→    options,
   137→    *,
   138→    deps: HolisticPrepareDependencies,
   139→) -> dict[str, object]:
   140→    """Prepare holistic review payload with injected dependencies for patchability."""
   141→    scoped_files, allowed_review_files = _resolve_review_files(path, lang, options)
   142→
   143→    context, review_ctx = _build_review_contexts(
   144→        path,
   145→        lang,
   146→        state,
   147→        scoped_files,
   148→        is_file_cache_enabled_fn=deps.is_file_cache_enabled_fn,
   149→        enable_file_cache_fn=deps.enable_file_cache_fn,
   150→        disable_file_cache_fn=deps.disable_file_cache_fn,
   151→        build_holistic_context_fn=deps.build_holistic_context_fn,
   152→        build_review_context_fn=deps.build_review_context_fn,
   153→    )
   154→
   155→    dim_ctx = _resolve_dimension_context(
   156→        lang.name,
   157→        options,
   158→        load_dimensions_for_lang_fn=deps.load_dimensions_for_lang_fn,
   159→        resolve_dimensions_fn=deps.resolve_dimensions_fn,
   160→        get_lang_guidance_fn=deps.get_lang_guidance_fn,
   161→    )
   162→
   163→    include_full_sweep = bool(options.include_full_sweep)
   164→    if options.dimensions:
   165→        include_full_sweep = False
   166→    batches = deps.assemble_holistic_batches_fn(
   167→        context,
   168→        lang=lang,
   169→        repo_root=path,
   170→        state=state,
   171→        dims=dim_ctx.dims,
   172→        all_files=scoped_files,
   173→        allowed_review_files=allowed_review_files,
   174→        include_full_sweep=include_full_sweep,
   175→        max_files_per_batch=options.max_files_per_batch,
   176→        deps=deps.holistic_batch_deps,
   177→    )
   178→
   179→    selected_prompts = _build_selected_prompts(
   180→        dim_ctx.dims,
   181→        dim_ctx.holistic_prompts,
   182→        dim_ctx.per_file_prompts,
   183→    )
   184→
   185→    payload: dict[str, Any] = {
   186→        "command": "review",
   187→        "mode": "holistic",
   188→        "language": lang.name,
   189→        "dimensions": dim_ctx.dims,
   190→        "dimension_prompts": selected_prompts,
   191→        "lang_guidance": dim_ctx.lang_guide,
   192→        "holistic_context": context.to_dict(),
   193→        "review_context": deps.serialize_context_fn(review_ctx),
   194→        "system_prompt": dim_ctx.system_prompt,
   195→        "total_files": context.codebase_stats.get("total_files", 0),
   196→        "workflow": HOLISTIC_WORKFLOW,
   197→        "invalid_dimensions": {
   198→            "requested": dim_ctx.invalid_requested,
   199→            "default": dim_ctx.invalid_default,
   200→        },
   201→    }
   202→
   203→    batches = _attach_issue_history_context(
   204→        payload,
   205→        batches,
   206→        state,
   207→        options,
   208→        allowed_review_files,
   209→    )
   210→
   211→    # Attach accumulated dimension contexts to each batch
   212→    dim_contexts = state.get("dimension_contexts", {})
   213→    if isinstance(dim_contexts, dict) and dim_contexts:
   214→        payload["dimension_contexts"] = dim_contexts
   215→        for batch_item in batches:
   216→            if not isinstance(batch_item, dict):
   217→                continue
   218→            batch_dims = batch_item.get("dimensions", [])
   219→            if isinstance(batch_dims, list):
   220→                batch_item["dimension_contexts"] = {
   221→                    d: dim_contexts[d] for d in batch_dims if d in dim_contexts
   222→                }
   223→
   224→    payload["investigation_batches"] = batches
   225→    return payload
   226→
   227→
   228→__all__ = ["HolisticPrepareDependencies", "prepare_holistic_review_payload"]
   229→
```

```
   104→        "status": epic_data.get("status", "pending"),
   105→        "triage_version": version,
   106→    }
   107→
   108→
   109→def _upsert_triage_clusters(
   110→    *,
   111→    clusters: dict[str, Cluster],
   112→    triage: TriageResult,
   113→    now: str,
   114→    version: int,
   115→) -> tuple[int, int]:
   116→    created = 0
   117→    updated = 0
   118→    for epic_data in sorted(triage.clusters, key=_epic_sort_key):
   119→        raw_name = epic_data["name"]
   120→        epic_name = _normalized_epic_name(raw_name)
   121→        existing = clusters.get(epic_name)
   122→        if existing and existing.get("thesis"):
   123→            _update_existing_epic_cluster(existing, epic_data, now=now, version=version)
   124→            updated += 1
   125→            continue
   126→        clusters[epic_name] = _create_epic_cluster(
   127→            epic_name=epic_name,
   128→            epic_data=epic_data,
   129→            now=now,
   130→            version=version,
   131→        )
   132→        created += 1
   133→    return created, updated
   134→
   135→
   136→def _reorder_queue_by_dependency(
   137→    *,
   138→    order: list[str],
   139→    triage: TriageResult,
   140→    dismissed_ids: list[str],
   141→) -> None:
   142→    epic_issue_ids: set[str] = set()
   143→    epic_ordered_ids: list[str] = []
   144→    dismissed_set = set(dismissed_ids)
   145→    for epic_data in sorted(triage.clusters, key=_epic_sort_key):
   146→        for fid in epic_data["issue_ids"]:
   147→            if fid in epic_issue_ids or fid in dismissed_set:
   148→                continue
   149→            epic_issue_ids.add(fid)
   150→            epic_ordered_ids.append(fid)
   151→
   152→    non_epic_items = [fid for fid in order if fid not in epic_issue_ids]
   153→    order.clear()
   154→    order.extend(epic_ordered_ids)
   155→    order.extend(non_epic_items)
   156→
   157→
   158→def _set_triage_meta(
   159→    *,
   160→    plan: PlanModel,
   161→    state: StateModel,
   162→    triage: TriageResult,
   163→    now: str,
   164→    version: int,
   165→    dismissed_ids: list[str],
   166→    trigger: str,
   167→) -> None:
   168→    current_hash = review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)
   169→    open_review_ids = sorted(
   170→        fid
   171→        for fid, issue in state.get("issues", {}).items()
   172→        if issue.get("status") == "open"
   173→        and issue.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   174→    )
   175→
   176→    plan["epic_triage_meta"] = {
   177→        "triaged_ids": open_review_ids,
   178→        "last_run": now,
   179→        "version": version,
   180→        "dismissed_ids": dismissed_ids,
   181→        "issue_snapshot_hash": current_hash,
   182→        "strategy_summary": triage.strategy_summary,
   183→        "trigger": trigger,
   184→    }
   185→
   186→
   187→def apply_triage_to_plan(
   188→    plan: PlanModel,
   189→    state: StateModel,
   190→    triage: TriageResult,
   191→    *,
   192→    trigger: str = "manual",
   193→) -> TriageMutationResult:
   194→    """Apply parsed triage result to the living plan.
   195→
   196→    1. Creates/updates triage clusters in plan["clusters"]
   197→    2. Marks dismissed issues as triaged_out skips
   198→    3. Reorders queue_order to group cluster members by dependency_order
   199→    4. Updates epic_triage_meta with snapshot hash
   200→    """
   201→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   202→    now = utc_now()
   203→    result = TriageMutationResult()
   204→    result.strategy_summary = triage.strategy_summary
   205→
   206→    clusters = plan["clusters"]
   207→    skipped: dict = plan["skipped"]
   208→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   209→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   210→    version = int(meta.get("version", 0)) + 1
   211→    result.triage_version = version
   212→
   213→    created, updated = _upsert_triage_clusters(
   214→        clusters=clusters,
   215→        triage=triage,
   216→        now=now,
   217→        version=version,
   218→    )
   219→    result.epics_created += created
   220→    result.epics_updated += updated
   221→
   222→    dismissed_ids, dismiss_count = dismiss_triage_issues(
   223→        triage=triage,
   224→        order=order,
   225→        skipped=skipped,
   226→        now=now,
   227→        version=version,
   228→        scan_count=int(state.get("scan_count", 0)),
   229→    )
   230→    result.issues_dismissed += dismiss_count
   231→
   232→    _reorder_queue_by_dependency(
   233→        order=order,
   234→        triage=triage,
   235→        dismissed_ids=dismissed_ids,
   236→    )
   237→
   238→    _set_triage_meta(
   239→        plan=plan,
   240→        state=state,
   241→        triage=triage,
   242→        now=now,
   243→        version=version,
   244→        dismissed_ids=dismissed_ids,
   245→        trigger=trigger,
   246→    )
   247→    plan["updated"] = now
   248→
   249→    return result
   250→
   251→__all__ = ["TriageMutationResult", "apply_triage_to_plan"]
   252→
```

```
    66→@dataclass
    67→class ReviewPrepareOptions:
    68→    """Configuration bundle for per-file review preparation."""
    69→

    89→
    90→def _rel_list(s: set[str] | list[str]) -> list[str]:
    91→    """Normalize a set or list of paths to sorted relative paths (max 10)."""
    92→    if isinstance(s, set):
    93→        return sorted(rel(x) for x in s)[:10]
    94→    return [rel(x) for x in list(s)[:10]]
    95→
    96→
    97→def _normalize_max_files(value: Any) -> int | None:
    98→    """Normalize max_files input: None/<=0 means unlimited."""
    99→    if value in (None, ""):
   100→        return None
   101→    parsed = int(value)
   102→    return parsed if parsed > 0 else None
   103→
   104→
   105→def prepare_review(
   106→    path: Path,
   107→    lang: object,
   108→    state: dict,
   109→    options: ReviewPrepareOptions | None = None,
   110→) -> dict[str, object]:
   111→    """Prepare review data for agent consumption. Returns structured dict.
   112→
   113→    If *files* is provided, skip file_finder (avoids redundant filesystem walks
   114→    when the caller already has the file list, e.g. from _setup_lang).
   115→    """
   116→    resolved_options = options or ReviewPrepareOptions()
   117→    resolved_options.max_files = _normalize_max_files(resolved_options.max_files)
   118→    all_files = (
   119→        resolved_options.files
   120→        if resolved_options.files is not None
   121→        else (lang.file_finder(path) if lang.file_finder else [])
   122→    )
   123→
   124→    # Enable file cache for entire prepare operation — context building,
   125→    # file selection, and content extraction all read the same files.
   126→    already_cached = is_file_cache_enabled()
   127→    if not already_cached:
   128→        enable_file_cache()
   129→    try:
   130→        context = build_review_context(path, lang, state, files=all_files)
   131→        selected = select_files_for_review(
   132→            lang,
   133→            path,
   134→            state,
   135→            options=ReviewSelectionOptions(
   136→                max_files=resolved_options.max_files,
   137→                max_age_days=resolved_options.max_age_days,
   138→                force_refresh=resolved_options.force_refresh,
   139→                files=all_files,
   140→            ),
   141→        )
   142→        file_requests = _build_file_requests(selected, lang, state)
   143→    finally:
   144→        if not already_cached:
   145→            disable_file_cache()
   146→
   147→    default_dims, dimension_prompts, system_prompt = load_dimensions_for_lang(lang.name)
   148→    dims = resolve_dimensions(
   149→        cli_dimensions=resolved_options.dimensions,
   150→        config_dimensions=resolved_options.config_dimensions,
   151→        default_dimensions=default_dims,
   152→    )
   153→    lang_guide = get_lang_guidance(lang.name)
   154→    valid_dims = set(dimension_prompts)
   155→    invalid_requested = [
   156→        dim for dim in (resolved_options.dimensions or []) if dim not in valid_dims
   157→    ]
   158→    invalid_config = [
   159→        dim
   160→        for dim in (resolved_options.config_dimensions or [])
   161→        if dim not in valid_dims
   162→    ]
   163→
   164→    return {
   165→        "command": "review",
   166→        "language": lang.name,
   167→        "dimensions": dims,
   168→        "dimension_prompts": {
   169→            d: dimension_prompts[d] for d in dims if d in dimension_prompts
   170→        },
   171→        "lang_guidance": lang_guide,
   172→        "context": serialize_context(context),
   173→        "system_prompt": system_prompt,
   174→        "files": file_requests,
   175→        "total_candidates": len(file_requests),
   176→        "cache_status": {
   177→            "fresh": count_fresh(state, resolved_options.max_age_days),
   178→            "stale": count_stale(state, resolved_options.max_age_days),
   179→            "new": len(file_requests),
   180→        },
   181→        "invalid_dimensions": {
   182→            "requested": invalid_requested,
   183→            "config": invalid_config,
   184→        },
   185→    }
   186→
   187→
   188→def _build_file_requests(files: list[str], lang: object, state: dict) -> list[dict]:
   189→    """Build per-file review request dicts."""
   190→    file_requests = []
   191→    for filepath in files:
   192→        content = read_file_text(abs_path(filepath))
   193→        if content is None:
   194→            continue
   195→
   196→        rpath = rel(filepath)
   197→        zone = "production"
   198→        if lang.zone_map is not None:
   199→            zone = lang.zone_map.get(filepath).value
   200→
   201→        neighbors: dict
   202→        if lang.dep_graph:
   203→            entry = dep_graph_lookup(lang.dep_graph, filepath)
   204→            imports_raw = entry.get("imports", set())
   205→            importers_raw = entry.get("importers", set())
   206→            importer_count_value = importer_count(entry)
   207→            neighbors = {
   208→                "imports": _rel_list(imports_raw),
   209→                "importers": _rel_list(importers_raw),
   210→                "importer_count": importer_count_value,
   211→            }
   212→        else:
   213→            neighbors = {}
   214→
   215→        file_requests.append(
   216→            {
   217→                "file": rpath,
   218→                "content": content,
   219→                "zone": zone,
   220→                "loc": len(content.splitlines()),
   221→                "neighbors": neighbors,
   222→                "existing_issues": get_file_issues(state, filepath),
   223→            }
   224→        )
   225→    return file_requests
   226→
   227→
   228→def prepare_holistic_review(
   229→    path: Path,
   230→    lang: object,
   231→    state: dict,
   232→    options: HolisticReviewPrepareOptions | None = None,
   233→) -> dict[str, object]:
   234→    """Prepare holistic review data for agent consumption. Returns structured dict."""
   235→    resolved_options = options or HolisticReviewPrepareOptions()
   236→    deps = HolisticPrepareDependencies(
   237→        is_file_cache_enabled_fn=is_file_cache_enabled,
   238→        enable_file_cache_fn=enable_file_cache,
   239→        disable_file_cache_fn=disable_file_cache,
   240→        build_holistic_context_fn=build_holistic_context,
   241→        build_review_context_fn=build_review_context,
   242→        load_dimensions_for_lang_fn=load_dimensions_for_lang,
   243→        resolve_dimensions_fn=resolve_dimensions,
   244→        get_lang_guidance_fn=get_lang_guidance,
   245→        assemble_holistic_batches_fn=assemble_holistic_batches,
   246→        holistic_batch_deps=HolisticBatchAssemblyDependencies(
   247→            build_investigation_batches_fn=_build_investigation_batches,
   248→            batch_concerns_fn=_batch_concerns,
   249→            filter_batches_to_dimensions_fn=_filter_batches_to_dimensions,
   250→            append_full_sweep_batch_fn=append_full_sweep_batch,
   251→            log_best_effort_failure_fn=log_best_effort_failure,
   252→            logger=logger,
   253→        ),
   254→        serialize_context_fn=serialize_context,
   255→    )
   256→    return prepare_holistic_review_payload(
   257→        path,
   258→        lang,
   259→        state,
   260→        resolved_options,
   261→        deps=deps,
   262→    )
   263→
```

```json
{
```

```json
  "old_string": "def cmd_plan_backlog(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Move deferred items to backlog — remove from plan tracking entirely.\"\"\"\n    runtime = command_runtime(args)\n    state = runtime.state\n    if not require_issue_inventory(state):\n        return\n\n    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, \"patterns\", [])\n\n    state_file = runtime.state_path\n    plan_file = _plan_file_for_state(state_file)\n    plan = load_plan(plan_file)\n    issue_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan, status_filter=\"all\")\n    if not issue_ids:\n        print(colorize(\"  No matching issues found.\", \"yellow\"))\n        return\n\n    removed = backlog_items(plan, issue_ids)\n    if not removed:\n        print(colorize(\"  No matching deferred items found.\", \"yellow\"))\n        return\n\n    append_log_entry(\n        plan,\n        \"backlog\",\n        issue_ids=removed,\n        actor=\"user\",\n    )\n    clear_postflight_scan_completion(plan, issue_ids=removed)\n    save_plan(plan, plan_file)\n\n    print(colorize(f\"  Moved {len(removed)} item(s) to backlog.\", \"green\"))",
  "new_string": "def cmd_plan_backlog(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Move deferred items to backlog — remove from plan tracking entirely.\"\"\"\n    runtime = command_runtime(args)\n    state = runtime.state\n    if not require_issue_inventory(state):\n        return\n\n    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, \"patterns\", [])\n\n    state_file = runtime.state_path\n    plan_file = _plan_file_for_state(state_file)\n    plan = load_plan(plan_file)\n    issue_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan, status_filter=\"all\")\n    if not issue_ids:\n        print(colorize(\"  No matching issues found.\", \"yellow\"))\n        return\n\n    removed = backlog_items(plan, issue_ids)\n    if not removed:\n        print(colorize(\"  No matching deferred items found.\", \"yellow\"))\n        return\n\n    # Reopen issues whose state status was set by the skip (deferred/triaged_out)\n    # so they don't get stranded with a non-open status while untracked by plan.\n    _BACKLOG_REOPEN_STATUSES = {\"deferred\", \"triaged_out\"}\n    state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)\n    issues = state_data.get(\"issues\", {})\n    reopen_ids = [\n        fid for fid in removed\n        if issues.get(fid, {}).get(\"status\") in _BACKLOG_REOPEN_STATUSES\n    ]\n\n    if reopen_ids:\n        for fid in reopen_ids:\n            state_mod.resolve_issues(state_data, fid, \"open\")\n\n    append_log_entry(\n        plan,\n        \"backlog\",\n        issue_ids=removed,\n        actor=\"user\",\n    )\n    clear_postflight_scan_completion(plan, issue_ids=removed)\n\n    if reopen_ids:\n        save_plan_state_transactional(\n            plan=plan,\n            plan_path=plan_file,\n            state_data=state_data,\n            state_path_value=state_file,\n        )\n    else:\n        save_plan(plan, plan_file)\n\n    print(colorize(f\"  Moved {len(removed)} item(s) to backlog.\", \"green\"))\n    if reopen_ids:\n        print(colorize(f\"  Reopened {len(reopen_ids)} deferred/triaged-out issue(s) in state.\", \"dim\"))"
}
```

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/planning/render_sections.py",
  "old_string": "def summary_lines(stats: dict) -> list[str]:\n    open_count = stats.get(\"open\", 0)\n    total_issues = sum(\n        stats.get(key, 0) for key in (\"open\", \"fixed\", \"wontfix\", \"auto_resolved\")\n    )\n    addressed = total_issues - open_count\n    pct = round(addressed / total_issues * 100) if total_issues else 100\n    return [\n        f\"- **{open_count} open** / {total_issues} total ({pct}% addressed)\",\n        \"\",\n    ]",
  "new_string": "def summary_lines(stats: dict) -> list[str]:\n    open_count = stats.get(\"open\", 0)\n    deferred_count = stats.get(\"deferred\", 0) + stats.get(\"triaged_out\", 0)\n    total_issues = sum(\n        stats.get(key, 0)\n        for key in (\"open\", \"fixed\", \"wontfix\", \"auto_resolved\", \"deferred\", \"triaged_out\")\n    )\n    addressed = total_issues - open_count - deferred_count\n    pct = round(addressed / total_issues * 100) if total_issues else 100\n    deferred_note = f\" · {deferred_count} deferred\" if deferred_count else \"\"\n    return [\n        f\"- **{open_count} open** / {total_issues} total ({pct}% addressed{deferred_note})\",\n        \"\",\n    ]"
}
```

```json
}
```

> AGENT

The default in `getattr` is `False`, but now the CLI default is `True`. The `getattr` default only applies when `args` doesn't have the attribute at all (e.g., programmatic callers). The CLI will now set it to `True` by default. This is fine — programmatic callers that don't pass `retrospective` will still get `False` as a safe default. > TOOL

tool_use Edit
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/review/prompt_sections.py",
  "old_string": "def render_historical_focus(batch: PromptBatchPayload) -> str:\n    focus = batch.get(\"historical_issue_focus\")\n    if not isinstance(focus, dict):\n        return \"\"\n\n    selected_raw = focus.get(\"selected_count\", 0)\n    try:\n        selected_count = max(0, int(selected_raw))\n    except (TypeError, ValueError):\n        selected_count = 0\n\n    issues = focus.get(\"issues\", [])\n    if not isinstance(issues, list):\n        issues = []\n\n    if selected_count <= 0 or not issues:\n        return \"\"\n\n    lines: list[str] = []\n    lines.append(\n        \"Previously flagged issues — navigation aid, not scoring evidence:\"\n    )\n    lines.append(\n        \"Check whether each issue still exists in the current code. Do not re-report\"\n        \" issues that have been fixed or marked wontfix — focus on what remains or\"\n        \" what is new. If several past issues share a root cause, call that out.\"\n    )\n\n    for entry in issues:\n        if not isinstance(entry, dict):\n            continue\n        status = str(entry.get(\"status\", \"\")).strip()\n        summary = str(entry.get(\"summary\", \"\")).strip()\n        note = str(entry.get(\"note\", \"\")).strip()\n\n        line = f\"  - [{status}] {summary}\"\n        if note:\n            line += f\" (note: {note})\"\n        lines.append(line)\n    return \"\\n\".join(lines) + \"\\n\\n\"",
  "new_string": "_HISTORICAL_STATUS_GROUPS = (\n    (\"open\", \"Still open\"),\n    (\"deferred\", \"Deferred\"),\n    (\"triaged_out\", \"Triaged out\"),\n)\n_HISTORICAL_RESOLVED_GROUP = \"Resolved\"\n_HISTORICAL_RESOLVED_STATUSES = {\"fixed\", \"wontfix\", \"false_positive\", \"auto_resolved\"}\n\n\ndef render_historical_focus(batch: PromptBatchPayload) -> str:\n    focus = batch.get(\"historical_issue_focus\")\n    if not isinstance(focus, dict):\n        return \"\"\n\n    selected_raw = focus.get(\"selected_count\", 0)\n    try:\n        selected_count = max(0, int(selected_raw))\n    except (TypeError, ValueError):\n        selected_count = 0\n\n    issues = focus.get(\"issues\", [])\n    if not isinstance(issues, list):\n        issues = []\n\n    if selected_count <= 0 or not issues:\n        return \"\"\n\n    lines: list[str] = [\n        \"Previously flagged issues — navigation aid, not scoring evidence:\",\n        \"Check whether open issues still exist. Do not re-report resolved or deferred items.\",\n        \"If several past issues share a root cause, call that out.\",\n    ]\n\n    # Group issues by status category\n    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}\n    for entry in issues:\n        if not isinstance(entry, dict):\n            continue\n        status = str(entry.get(\"status\", \"open\")).strip()\n        grouped.setdefault(status, []).append(entry)\n\n    def _render_entry(entry: dict) -> str:\n        status = str(entry.get(\"status\", \"\")).strip()\n        summary = str(entry.get(\"summary\", \"\")).strip()\n        note = str(entry.get(\"note\", \"\")).strip()\n        prefix = f\"[{status}] \" if status else \"\"\n        line = f\"    - {prefix}{summary}\"\n        if note:\n            line += f\" (note: {note})\"\n        return line\n\n    # Render active groups first (open, deferred, triaged_out)\n    for status_key, group_label in _HISTORICAL_STATUS_GROUPS:\n        group = grouped.pop(status_key, [])\n        if group:\n            lines.append(f\"\\n  {group_label} ({len(group)}):\")\n            lines.extend(_render_entry(e) for e in group)\n\n    # Render resolved group (all remaining resolved statuses)\n    resolved: list[dict] = []\n    for status_key in list(grouped):\n        if status_key in _HISTORICAL_RESOLVED_STATUSES:\n            resolved.extend(grouped.pop(status_key))\n    if resolved:\n        lines.append(f\"\\n  {_HISTORICAL_RESOLVED_GROUP} ({len(resolved)}):\")\n        lines.extend(_render_entry(e) for e in resolved)\n\n    # Any unknown statuses\n    for status_key, group in grouped.items():\n        if group:\n            lines.append(f\"\\n  {status_key} ({len(group)}):\")\n            lines.extend(_render_entry(e) for e in group)\n\n    lines.append(\"\")\n    lines.append(\"Explore past review issues:\")\n    lines.append(\"  desloppify show review --no-budget              # all open review issues\")\n    lines.append(\"  desloppify show review --status deferred         # deferred issues\")\n\n    return \"\\n\".join(lines) + \"\\n\\n\""
}
```

```json
  "new_string": "def render_dimension_deferral_context(batch: PromptBatchPayload) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Render deferral context for dimensions that were deferred for multiple cycles.\"\"\"\n    defer_meta = batch.get(\"subjective_defer_meta\")\n    if not isinstance(defer_meta, dict) or not defer_meta:\n        return \"\"\n\n    lines: list[str] = []\n    for dim, meta in defer_meta.items():\n        if not isinstance(meta, dict):\n            continue\n        cycles = meta.get(\"deferred_cycles\", 0)\n        if not isinstance(cycles, int) or cycles < 1:\n            continue\n        lines.append(\n            f\"Note: {dim} was deferred for {cycles} scan cycle(s) while objective issues took priority.\"\n        )\n        lines.append(\n            \"Previous assessment may be stale — calibrate accordingly.\"\n        )\n    if not lines:\n        return \"\"\n    return \"\\n\".join(lines) + \"\\n\\n\"\n\n\ndef _concern_signal_lines(entry: dict[str, object]) -> list[str]:"
}
```

tool_result Read
```
64→def build_holistic_packet(
    65→    *,
    66→    state: StateModel,
    67→    lang: Any,
    68→    config: dict[str, Any],
    69→    context: ReviewPacketContext,
    70→    setup_lang_fn,
    71→    prepare_holistic_review_fn=None,
    72→) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], str]:
    73→    """Build the canonical holistic review packet payload and lang name."""
    74→    lang_run, found_files = setup_lang_fn(lang, context.path, config)
    75→    lang_name = lang_run.name
    76→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
    77→        state,
    78→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="review"),
    79→    )
    80→    prepare_fn = prepare_holistic_review_fn or prepare_holistic_review
    81→    packet = prepare_fn(
    82→        context.path,
    83→        lang_run,
    84→        state,
    85→        options=HolisticReviewPrepareOptions(
    86→            dimensions=context.dimensions,
    87→            files=found_files or None,
    88→            max_files_per_batch=coerce_review_batch_file_limit(config),
    89→            include_issue_history=context.retrospective,
    90→            issue_history_max_issues=context.retrospective_max_issues,
    91→            issue_history_max_batch_items=context.retrospective_max_batch_items,
    92→        ),
    93→    )
    94→    packet["narrative"] = narrative
    95→    return packet, lang_name
```

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/review/packet/build.py",
  "old_string": "    packet[\"narrative\"] = narrative\n    return packet, lang_name",
  "new_string": "    packet[\"narrative\"] = narrative\n    _attach_plan_deferral_context(packet)\n    return packet, lang_name\n\n\ndef _attach_plan_deferral_context(packet: dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Attach subjective_defer_meta from plan to investigation batches.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        from desloppify.engine.plan_state import load_plan\n        plan = load_plan()\n    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:\n        return\n    defer_meta = plan.get(\"subjective_defer_meta\") if isinstance(plan, dict) else None\n    if not isinstance(defer_meta, dict) or not defer_meta:\n        return\n    defer_count = defer_meta.get(\"defer_count\", 0)\n    if not isinstance(defer_count, int) or defer_count < 1:\n        return\n    deferred_ids = set(defer_meta.get(\"deferred_review_ids\", []))\n    batches = packet.get(\"investigation_batches\")\n    if not isinstance(batches, list):\n        return\n    for batch in batches:\n        if not isinstance(batch, dict):\n            continue\n        dims = batch.get(\"dimensions\", [])\n        batch_defer: dict[str, dict] = {}\n        for dim in dims:\n            dim_id = f\"subjective::{dim}\"\n            if dim_id in deferred_ids or dim in deferred_ids:\n                batch_defer[dim] = {\"deferred_cycles\": defer_count}\n        if batch_defer:\n            batch[\"subjective_defer_meta\"] = batch_defer"
}
```

```
    28→def _plan_with_queue(*ids: str) -> dict:
    29→    plan = empty_plan()
    30→    plan["queue_order"] = list(ids)
    31→    return plan

    33→
    34→def _state_with_issues(*ids: str, status: str = "open") -> dict:
    35→    issues = {}
    36→    for fid in ids:
    37→        issues[fid] = {
    38→            "id": fid,
    39→            "status": status,
    40→            "detector": "test",
    41→            "file": "test.py",
    42→            "tier": 1,
    43→            "confidence": "high",
    44→            "summary": f"Issue {fid}",
    45→        }
    46→    return {"issues": issues, "scan_count": 5}

    53→def test_skip_temporary():
    54→    plan = _plan_with_queue("a", "b", "c")
    55→    count = skip_items(plan, ["b"], kind="temporary")
    56→    assert count == 1
    57→    assert "b" in plan["skipped"]
    58→    assert plan["skipped"]["b"]["kind"] == "temporary"
```

> AGENT

Now I understand the test failures:

1. **test_unskip_temporary** (line 127-135): expects `need_reopen == []` but now temporary skips need state reopen. Fix: update expectation.

2. **test_reconcile_resurfaced** (line 331-340): The reconcile now runs `_sync_skipped_issue_statuses` first, which sets the issue status from "open" to "deferred". Then `_is_issue_alive` checks `status == "open"`, which is now false for the deferred issue, so it gets superseded instead of resurfaced. I need to update `_is_issue_alive` to also consider deferred/triaged_out as alive.

3. **test_skip_and_unskip_roundtrip** (line 347-381): expects `need_reopen == {"c"}` but now includes "a" too.

Let me fix these:

All implementation is complete. Here's a summary of all changes made:

## Part A: Unify Status Enum

**A1** `base/enums.py` — Added `DEFERRED = "deferred"` and `TRIAGED_OUT = "triaged_out"` to `Status` enum and `_CANONICAL_ISSUE_STATUSES`. NOT added to `_RESOLVED_STATUSES`.

**A2** `engine/_scoring/policy/core.py` — Added `deferred` and `triaged_out` to all 3 modes in `FAILURE_STATUSES_BY_MODE`.

**A3** `engine/_plan/skip_policy.py` — `skip_kind_state_status()` now maps `temporary→deferred`, `triaged_out→triaged_out`. `skip_kind_needs_state_reopen()` now includes all 4 kinds.

**A4** `app/commands/plan/override_skip.py` — `cmd_plan_backlog()` now reopens issues with `deferred`/`triaged_out` status back to `open` so they don't get stranded.

**A5** `engine/_plan/triage/apply.py` — `apply_triage_to_plan()` now sets `state.issues[id].status = "triaged_out"` for dismissed issues.

**A6** `engine/_state/merge_issues.py` — Verified: `upsert_issues` only reopens `fixed/auto_resolved/false_positive`, so deferred/triaged_out are NOT auto-reopened. No change needed.

**A7** `app/commands/show/render.py` — Added `⏸` for deferred and `△` for triaged_out status icons.

**A8** `engine/planning/render_sections.py` + `render.py` — `summary_lines()` includes deferred/triaged_out in total; shows deferred count. Plan header shows deferred segment.

**A9** `engine/_plan/reconcile.py` — Added `_sync_skipped_issue_statuses()` that runs on every reconcile, syncing `open` issues in `plan.skipped` to their correct status. Also updated `_is_issue_alive()` and `_find_candidates()` to treat deferred/triaged_out as alive. Resurfaced issues get state reopened from deferred→open.

## Part B: Surface History to Reviewers

**B1** `app/cli_support/parser_groups_admin_review_options_core.py` — `--retrospective` now defaults to `True` with `--no-retrospective` opt-out via `BooleanOptionalAction`. Updated `packet/build.py` default and next-command builders accordingly.

**B2** `app/commands/review/prompt_sections.py` — Rewrote `render_historical_focus()` to group issues by status (open, deferred, triaged_out, resolved) with counts and CLI exploration hints.

**B3** `app/commands/review/prompt_sections.py` — Added `render_dimension_deferral_context()` that surfaces defer cycle count to reviewers. Wired into `batch/prompt_template.py` and `external.py`.

**B4** `app/commands/review/packet/build.py` — Added `_attach_plan_deferral_context()` that loads the plan, extracts `subjective_defer_meta`, and attaches per-dimension deferral info to relevant batches.

## Tests
- Updated 4 test files to match new behavior (5194 passed, 3 skipped, 0 failures)
