Now let me generate a comprehensive report:

## Summary: `show --status` Filter Path and `stats` Dict Computation

I've traced both aspects of your question through the codebase. Here are the findings:

---

## 1. **The `show --status` Filter Path**

### 1.1: Argument Parsing
- **File**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/cli_support/parser_groups.py` (lines 142-196)
- The `--status` argument is defined in `_add_show_parser()` with choices validated by `_STATUS_CHOICES`
- `_STATUS_CHOICES` is computed from `issue_status_tokens(include_all=True)` (line 22)
- Valid values: `"open"`, `"fixed"`, `"wontfix"`, `"false_positive"`, `"auto_resolved"`, `"deferred"`, `"triaged_out"`, `"all"`
- Default: `"open"` (line 164)

### 1.2: Where `--status` is Resolved
- **File**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/show/scope.py` (line 173-190)
- Function: `resolve_show_scope(args)` 
- Extracts `status_filter = getattr(args, "status", "open")` (line 177)
- Returns `(ok, pattern, status_filter, scope)` tuple

### 1.3: Status Validation via `issue_status_tokens()`
- **File**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/base/enums.py` (lines 71-75)
- **Function**: `issue_status_tokens(include_all: bool = False)`
- Returns a frozenset of canonical statuses + optional `"all"` keyword
- **Gate**: The argparse `choices` list directly gates which values are accepted at CLI parse time (parser_groups.py line 163)
- No additional validation happens in the filtering logic itself—argparse enforces it upfront

### 1.4: How the Status Filter is Applied
The `status_filter` flows through this chain:

1. **Entry point** (`cmd_show` in `/desloppify/app/commands/show/cmd.py`, line 133):
   - Calls `resolve_show_scope(args)` → gets `status_filter`

2. **Matching logic** (`scope.py`, lines 193-211, function `load_matches()`):
   - Calls `build_work_queue(state, options=QueueBuildOptions(status=status_filter, ...))`

3. **Queue building** (`engine/_work_queue/selection.py`, lines 14-46, function `select_queue_items()`):
   - If `status == "open"` and not chronic → uses snapshot-based (plan-aware) queue
   - Otherwise → calls `build_issue_items()` with `status_filter`

4. **Issue-level filtering** (`engine/_work_queue/ranking.py`, lines 124-182, function `build_issue_items()`):
   - Iterates through all issues
   - Line 139: **`if not status_matches(issue.get("status", "open"), status_filter): continue`**
   - This is the actual filtering gate

5. **Status comparison** (`engine/_work_queue/helpers.py`, lines 28-29, function `status_matches()`):
   ```python
   def status_matches(item_status: str, status_filter: str) -> bool:
       return status_filter == "all" or item_status == status_filter
   ```
   - **Simple equality check**: accepts exact status match OR the magic `"all"` keyword
   - No regex, no aliases, no partial matching

### Key Points
- `deferred` and `triaged_out` are fully supported status values (defined in `Status` enum)
- Both are accepted by `issue_status_tokens()` and `argparse` 
- Both will be included when `--status deferred` or `--status triaged_out` is used
- `--status all` works as a bypass filter (returns everything except suppressed)

---

## 2. **The `stats` Dict Computation**

### 2.1: Where `stats` is Computed
- **File**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_scoring/state_integration.py` (lines 200-221)
- **Function**: `recompute_stats(state, scan_path, subjective_integrity_target)`
- This is called after scanning and before state is written

### 2.2: Counting Logic
- **Helper function** (`state_integration.py`, lines 48-62): `_count_issues(issues)`
  - Initializes: `counters = dict.fromkeys(_EMPTY_COUNTERS, 0)` where `_EMPTY_COUNTERS = tuple(sorted(issue_status_tokens()))`
  - This expands to: `['auto_resolved', 'deferred', 'false_positive', 'fixed', 'open', 'triaged_out', 'wontfix']`
  
- **Loop** (lines 53-60):
  ```python
  for issue in issues.values():
      if issue.get("suppressed"):
          continue
      status = issue["status"]
      tier = issue.get("tier", 3)
      counters[status] = counters.get(status, 0) + 1
      # ... also updates tier_stats dict
  ```

### 2.3: Stats Dict Structure
- **File**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_state/schema_types_issues.py` (lines 67-74)
- **TypedDict**: `StateStats`
  ```python
  class StateStats(TypedDict, total=False):
      total: int
      open: int
      fixed: int
      auto_resolved: int
      wontfix: int
      false_positive: int
      by_tier: dict[str, TierStats]
  ```
- **Notice**: `deferred` and `triaged_out` are **NOT in the official StateStats schema**

### 2.4: Assignment to State
- **state_integration.py**, lines 210-216:
  ```python
  state["stats"] = {
      "total": sum(counters.values()),
      **counters,  # Spreads all counted statuses
      "by_tier": {
          str(tier): tier_counts for tier, tier_counts in sorted(tier_stats.items())
      },
  }
  ```
- The `**counters` spread will **include** `deferred` and `triaged_out` counts if any issues have those statuses
- They're just not declared in the TypedDict (which uses `total=False` so extra keys are allowed)

### 2.5: How Stats are Used
Two main places use `state["stats"]`:

1. **Plan header** (`engine/planning/render.py`, lines 38-89, function `_plan_header()`):
   - Lines 65-75:
     ```python
     deferred_total = stats.get("deferred", 0) + stats.get("triaged_out", 0)
     deferred_segment = f" | {deferred_total} deferred" if deferred_total else ""
     lines = [
         ...
         f"... | {stats.get('open', 0)} open | "
         f"{stats.get('fixed', 0)} fixed | "
         f"{stats.get('wontfix', 0)} wontfix | "
         f"{stats.get('auto_resolved', 0)} auto-resolved"
         f"{deferred_segment}",
         ...
     ]
     ```
   - **Already handles** `deferred` and `triaged_out` explicitly ✓

2. **Summary lines** (`engine/planning/render_sections.py`, lines 15-28, function `summary_lines()`):
   - Lines 17-23:
     ```python
     deferred_count = stats.get("deferred", 0) + stats.get("triaged_out", 0)
     total_issues = sum(
         stats.get(key, 0)
         for key in ("open", "fixed", "wontfix", "auto_resolved", "deferred", "triaged_out")
     )
     addressed = total_issues - open_count - deferred_count
     ```
   - **Already handles** `deferred` and `triaged_out` in the total and deferred calculations ✓

### Key Points
- **Deferred and triaged_out ARE counted** in stats computation (lines 50-60 of state_integration.py)
- Both are included in `_EMPTY_COUNTERS` initialization (line 28)
- Both appear in plan header (render.py) and summary lines (render_sections.py)
- They're not in the TypedDict schema but that's OK—TypedDict with `total=False` allows extra keys
- The `by_tier` breakdown also includes these statuses (TierStats also lacks them in TypedDict but again `total=False`)

Perfect! Now I have enough information to provide a comprehensive report. Let me compile my findings.

## Summary: How Desloppify Handles Issue Statuses in Narrative and Coaching

Based on my thorough search of the codebase, here are my key findings:

### 1. **Narrative System (`intelligence/narrative/`) - Status Handling**

**In `signals.py` - `count_open_by_detector()` function (lines 95-116):**
- **Critical finding**: This function ONLY counts issues with `status == "open"` that are **not suppressed**
- It explicitly filters: `if issue["status"] != "open" or issue.get("suppressed"): continue`
- **Deferred and triaged_out issues are COMPLETELY INVISIBLE** to the narrative system
- These excluded issues will not appear in:
  - Open counts by detector
  - Strategy guidance
  - Actions generation
  - Headline construction

**In `core.py` - `compute_narrative()` (lines 68-145):**
- Calls `_count_open_by_detector(issues)` which produces `by_detector` counts (line 88)
- Passes these counts to strategy, actions, and headline computation
- Since the count function filters out deferred/triaged_out, these don't reach coaching output

**In `headline.py` - Headline Generation:**
- Only references `stats.get("open", 0)` for issue counts (lines 84, 156, 168)
- Never mentions deferred or triaged_out counts in any headlines
- Security and review counts exclude deferred/triaged_out

### 2. **Stats Computation (`engine/_scoring/state_integration.py`) - Status Counts**

**In `_count_issues()` function (lines 48-62):**
- Computes per-status counters using `issue_status_tokens()` from `base/enums.py`
- **Returns ALL status types**: open, fixed, wontfix, false_positive, auto_resolved, deferred, triaged_out
- Creates: `counters[status]` for each issue not suppressed
- Also creates: `by_tier[tier][status]` breakdown

**In `recompute_stats()` (lines 200-221):**
- Calls `_count_issues()` which creates full status breakdown
- **Stats dict includes**: 
  ```python
  {
    "total": sum(counters),
    "open": N,
    "fixed": N,
    "wontfix": N,
    "false_positive": N,
    "auto_resolved": N,
    "deferred": N,
    "triaged_out": N,
    "by_tier": {
      "1": {status breakdown},
      "2": {status breakdown},
      ...
    }
  }
  ```

**Key insight**: `stats` dict in state DOES include deferred and triaged_out counts, but the narrative coaching system doesn't read these fields.

### 3. **Show Command Output (`app/commands/show/`)**

**In `render.py` - `_print_single_issue()` (lines 48-89):**
- Properly renders all status icons including:
  - `"deferred": "⏸"`
  - `"triaged_out": "△"`
- Status filtering happens in the work queue builder via `status_filter` parameter

**In `cmd.py` - `resolve_show_scope()` (lines 173-190):**
- Default status filter is "open" (line 177)
- User can pass `--status` flag to filter by any status
- The work queue builder receives this status filter

**In `scope.py` - `load_matches()` (lines 193-211):**
- Calls `build_work_queue()` with `status=status_filter`
- The queue builder filters by status, so deferred/triaged_out CAN be shown via `--status deferred` or `--status triaged_out`

**Show output summary (lines 165-210 in render.py):**
- Shows "By tier" and "By detector" breakdowns
- **Does NOT show a status breakdown** (e.g., no "N open, N deferred, N fixed" summary)

### 4. **Scan Command Output (`app/commands/scan/reporting/`)**

**In `summary.py` - `show_score_delta()`:**
- Accesses `stats = state["stats"]` (line 318)
- Uses only `stats.get("wontfix", 0)` for gap calculation (line 340)
- **No display of deferred/triaged_out counts in scan output**

**In `text.py` - Workflow guide and score explanation:**
- No mention of status breakdown or deferred/triaged_out visibility
- Focuses on overall/objective/strict/verified score tiers

### 5. **Review Command - Issue History Grouping (`app/commands/review/prompt_sections.py`)**

**In `render_historical_focus()` (lines 133-202):**
- **Groups historical issues by status explicitly**:
  ```python
  _HISTORICAL_STATUS_GROUPS = (
      ("open", "Still open"),
      ("deferred", "Deferred"),
      ("triaged_out", "Triaged out"),
  )
  _HISTORICAL_RESOLVED_STATUSES = {"fixed", "wontfix", "false_positive", "auto_resolved"}
  ```
- Groups issues into sections: "Still open", "Deferred", "Triaged out", "Resolved"
- **Review agents WILL see deferred and triaged_out issues** when reviewing historical issue context
- Instructions tell reviewers: "Do not re-report resolved or deferred items" (line 153)

### 6. **Plan Command - Deferred/Triaged_out Handling**

**In `app/commands/plan/override_skip.py`:**
- Defines `_BACKLOG_REOPEN_STATUSES = {"deferred", "triaged_out"}` 
- When moving deferred items to backlog, reopens them from these statuses
- Mentions "deferred" and "triaged_out" explicitly in messages

### 7. **Key Gaps & Inconsistencies**

| System | Deferred | Triaged_out | Visibility |
|--------|----------|-------------|------------|
| **Narrative coaching** | Invisible | Invisible | NOT in counts, headlines, actions |
| **Stats computation** | Counted | Counted | In `state["stats"]` dict but not displayed |
| **Show command** | Filterable | Filterable | Can view with `--status deferred` |
| **Show summary** | Missing | Missing | No status breakdown in output |
| **Scan output** | Missing | Missing | Not shown in scan report |
| **Review context** | Visible | Visible | Explicitly grouped in historical issues |
| **Plan/backlog** | Handled | Handled | Can reopen/move to backlog |

### 8. **Root Cause**

The narrative coaching pipeline uses `count_open_by_detector()` which **deliberately filters to only "open" status** (line 99 in signals.py). This is the single point where deferred/triaged_out are excluded from coaching output. The stats are computed correctly, but the coaching layer doesn't read the deferred/triaged_out counts from stats.

1→"""Scope and queue helpers for show command selection."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     9→from desloppify.base import registry as registry_mod
    10→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    11→from desloppify.engine._scoring.policy.core import DIMENSIONS
    12→from desloppify.engine._scoring.subjective.core import DISPLAY_NAMES
    13→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    14→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.core import (
    15→    QueueBuildOptions,
    16→    build_work_queue,
    17→)
    18→
    19→
    20→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    21→class ResolvedEntity:
    22→    """Classifies what the user's show pattern refers to."""
    23→
    24→    kind: str  # "dimension", "special_view", "file_or_pattern"
    25→    pattern: str  # Original user pattern
    26→    display_name: str  # Human-readable label
    27→    detectors: tuple[str, ...] = ()  # Detector names (for dimension kind)
    28→    is_subjective: bool = False  # Whether this is a subjective dimension
    29→
    30→
    31→def _resolve_special_view(pattern: str, lowered: str) -> ResolvedEntity | None:
    32→    if lowered not in ("concerns", "subjective", "subjective_review"):
    33→        return None
    34→    return ResolvedEntity(
    35→        kind="special_view",
    36→        pattern=pattern,
    37→        display_name=pattern,
    38→    )
    39→
    40→
    41→def _resolve_mechanical_dimension(pattern: str, lowered: str) -> ResolvedEntity | None:
    42→    lookup = _build_dimension_lookup()
    43→    detectors = lookup.get(lowered) or lookup.get(pattern.lower())
    44→    if not detectors:
    45→        return None
    46→    dim_display = pattern
    47→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
    48→        dim_lowered = dim.name.lower().replace(" ", "_")
    49→        if dim.name.lower() == lowered or dim_lowered == lowered:
    50→            dim_display = dim.name
    51→            break
    52→    return ResolvedEntity(
    53→        kind="dimension",
    54→        pattern=pattern,
    55→        display_name=dim_display,
    56→        detectors=tuple(detectors),
    57→        is_subjective=False,
    58→    )
    59→
    60→
    61→def _resolve_subjective_dimension(
    62→    pattern: str,
    63→    lowered: str,
    64→    state: StateModel,
    65→) -> ResolvedEntity | None:
    66→    display_name = DISPLAY_NAMES.get(lowered)
    67→    if not display_name:
    68→        for key in state.get("dimension_scores") or {}:
    69→            if key.lower().replace(" ", "_") == lowered:
    70→                display_name = key
    71→                break
    72→    if not display_name:
    73→        return None
    74→    dim_data, display_name = _lookup_dimension_score(state, display_name)
    75→    is_subj = "subjective_assessment" in (
    76→        dim_data.get("detectors", {}) if isinstance(dim_data, dict) else {}
    77→    )
    78→    return ResolvedEntity(
    79→        kind="dimension",
    80→        pattern=pattern,
    81→        display_name=display_name,
    82→        detectors=(),
    83→        is_subjective=is_subj,
    84→    )
    85→
    86→
    87→def resolve_entity(pattern: str, state: StateModel) -> ResolvedEntity:
    88→    """Classify a user pattern as a dimension, special view, or passthrough.
    89→
    90→    Resolution priority:
    91→    1. Special views: "concerns", "subjective", "subjective_review"
    92→    2. Mechanical dimension name (from DIMENSIONS)
    93→    3. Subjective dimension name (from DISPLAY_NAMES / dimension_scores)
    94→    4. Everything else: file_or_pattern passthrough
    95→    """
    96→    lowered = pattern.strip().lower().replace(" ", "_")
    97→
    98→    # 1. Special views
    99→    special_view = _resolve_special_view(pattern, lowered)
   100→    if special_view is not None:
   101→        return special_view
   102→
   103→    # 2. Mechanical dimension (via DIMENSIONS list)
   104→    mechanical = _resolve_mechanical_dimension(pattern, lowered)
   105→    if mechanical is not None:
   106→        return mechanical
   107→
   108→    # 3. Subjective dimension (via DISPLAY_NAMES or dimension_scores)
   109→    subjective = _resolve_subjective_dimension(pattern, lowered, state)
   110→    if subjective is not None:
   111→        return subjective
   112→
   113→    # 4. Everything else
   114→    return ResolvedEntity(
   115→        kind="file_or_pattern",
   116→        pattern=pattern,
   117→        display_name=pattern,
   118→    )
   119→
   120→
   121→def _build_dimension_lookup() -> dict[str, list[str]]:
   122→    """Build a map from dimension name/key (lowered) to detector names."""
   123→    lookup: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
   124→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   125→        detectors = list(dim.detectors) if hasattr(dim, "detectors") else []
   126→        lookup[dim.name.lower()] = detectors
   127→        # Also index by underscore key: "file_health" -> detectors
   128→        key = dim.name.lower().replace(" ", "_")
   129→        if key not in lookup:
   130→            lookup[key] = detectors
   131→    # Also add DISPLAY_NAMES reverse lookup (e.g. "abstraction_fit" -> "Abstraction Fit")
   132→    for key, display in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
   133→        normalized_key = key.lower().replace(" ", "_")
   134→        normalized_display = display.lower()
   135→        # Find which dimension this belongs to via get_dimension_for_detector or direct name match
   136→        for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   137→            dim_lower = dim.name.lower()
   138→            if normalized_display == dim_lower or normalized_key == dim_lower.replace(" ", "_"):
   139→                detectors = list(dim.detectors) if hasattr(dim, "detectors") else []
   140→                if normalized_key not in lookup:
   141→                    lookup[normalized_key] = detectors
   142→                if normalized_display not in lookup:
   143→                    lookup[normalized_display] = detectors
   144→    return lookup
   145→
   146→
   147→def _lookup_dimension_score(
   148→    state: StateModel, display_name: str,
   149→) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], str]:
   150→    """Find dimension_scores entry with case-insensitive fallback.
   151→
   152→    Returns (dim_data_dict, resolved_display_name).
   153→    """
   154→    lowered = display_name.lower().replace(" ", "_")
   155→    dim_data = (state.get("dimension_scores") or {}).get(display_name, {})
   156→    if not dim_data:
   157→        for ds_key, ds_val in (state.get("dimension_scores") or {}).items():
   158→            if ds_key.lower().replace(" ", "_") == lowered:
   159→                dim_data = ds_val
   160→                display_name = ds_key
   161→                break
   162→    return dim_data, display_name
   163→
   164→
   165→def _detector_names_hint() -> str:
   166→    """Return a compact list of detector names for the help message."""
   167→    names = tuple(getattr(registry_mod, "_DISPLAY_ORDER", ()))
   168→    if names:
   169→        return ", ".join(names[:10]) + (", ..." if len(names) > 10 else "")
   170→    return "smells, structural, security, review, ..."
   171→
   172→
   173→def resolve_show_scope(args: object) -> tuple[bool, str | None, str, str | None]:
   174→    """Resolve scope/pattern/status for a show invocation."""
   175→    chronic = getattr(args, "chronic", False)
   176→    pattern = args.pattern
   177→    status_filter = "open" if chronic else getattr(args, "status", "open")
   178→    if chronic:
   179→        scope = pattern
   180→        pattern = pattern or "<chronic>"
   181→        return True, pattern, status_filter, scope
   182→    if not pattern:
   183→        print(
   184→            colorize(
   185→                "Pattern required (or use --chronic). Try: desloppify show --help",
   186→                "yellow",
   187→            )
   188→        )
   189→        return False, None, status_filter, ""
   190→    return True, pattern, status_filter, pattern
   191→
   192→
   193→def load_matches(
   194→    state: StateModel,
   195→    *,
   196→    scope: str | None,
   197→    status_filter: str,
   198→    chronic: bool,
   199→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   200→    """Load matching issues from the ranked queue."""
   201→    queue = build_work_queue(
   202→        state,
   203→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   204→            count=None,
   205→            scope=scope,
   206→            status=status_filter,
   207→            include_subjective=False,
   208→            chronic=chronic,
   209→        ),
   210→    )
   211→    return [item for item in queue.get("items", []) if item.get("kind") == "issue"]
   212→
   213→
   214→def resolve_noise(
   215→    config: dict[str, Any],
   216→    matches: list[dict[str, Any]],
   217→    *,
   218→    no_budget: bool = False,
   219→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], dict[str, int], int, int, str | None]:
   220→    """Apply detector/global noise budget to show matches.
   221→
   222→    When *no_budget* is True, all matches are surfaced (nothing hidden).
   223→    """
   224→    if no_budget:
   225→        return (
   226→            matches,
   227→            {},
   228→            0,
   229→            0,
   230→            None,
   231→        )
   232→    noise_budget, global_noise_budget, budget_warning = (
   233→        state_mod.resolve_issue_noise_settings(config)
   234→    )
   235→    surfaced_matches, hidden_by_detector = state_mod.apply_issue_noise_budget(
   236→        matches,
   237→        budget=noise_budget,
   238→        global_budget=global_noise_budget,
   239→    )
   240→    return (
   241→        surfaced_matches,
   242→        hidden_by_detector,
   243→        noise_budget,
   244→        global_noise_budget,
   245→        budget_warning,
   246→    )
   247→
   248→
   249→__all__ = [
   250→    "ResolvedEntity",
   251→    "_detector_names_hint",
   252→    "_lookup_dimension_score",
   253→    "load_matches",
   254→    "resolve_entity",
   255→    "resolve_noise",
   256→    "resolve_show_scope",
   257→]
   258→

1→"""Canonical enums for issue attributes.
     2→
     3→StrEnum values compare equal to their string values (Confidence.HIGH == "high"),
     4→so existing code using raw strings continues to work during gradual migration.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→import enum
    10→
    11→
    12→class Confidence(enum.StrEnum):
    13→    HIGH = "high"
    14→    MEDIUM = "medium"
    15→    LOW = "low"
    16→
    17→
    18→class Status(enum.StrEnum):
    19→    OPEN = "open"
    20→    FIXED = "fixed"
    21→    WONTFIX = "wontfix"
    22→    FALSE_POSITIVE = "false_positive"
    23→    AUTO_RESOLVED = "auto_resolved"
    24→    DEFERRED = "deferred"
    25→    TRIAGED_OUT = "triaged_out"
    26→    RESOLVED = "resolved"  # Legacy on-disk value; migrated to FIXED on load.
    27→
    28→
    29→_CANONICAL_ISSUE_STATUSES = frozenset(
    30→    {
    31→        Status.OPEN.value,
    32→        Status.FIXED.value,
    33→        Status.WONTFIX.value,
    34→        Status.FALSE_POSITIVE.value,
    35→        Status.AUTO_RESOLVED.value,
    36→        Status.DEFERRED.value,
    37→        Status.TRIAGED_OUT.value,
    38→    }
    39→)
    40→_RESOLVED_STATUSES = frozenset(
    41→    {
    42→        Status.FIXED.value,
    43→        Status.WONTFIX.value,
    44→        Status.FALSE_POSITIVE.value,
    45→        Status.AUTO_RESOLVED.value,
    46→    }
    47→)
    48→_LEGACY_STATUS_ALIASES = {
    49→    Status.RESOLVED.value: Status.FIXED.value,
    50→}
    51→
    52→
    53→class Tier(enum.IntEnum):
    54→    AUTO_FIX = 1
    55→    QUICK_FIX = 2
    56→    JUDGMENT = 3
    57→    MAJOR_REFACTOR = 4
    58→
    59→
    60→def canonical_issue_status(
    61→    value: object, *, default: Status = Status.OPEN
    62→) -> Status:
    63→    """Normalize legacy/unknown issue status values to a canonical enum."""
    64→    token = str(value).strip().lower()
    65→    [REDACTED](token, token)
    66→    if token in _CANONICAL_ISSUE_STATUSES:
    67→        return Status(token)
    68→    return default
    69→
    70→
    71→def issue_status_tokens(*, include_all: bool = False) -> frozenset[str]:
    72→    """Return canonical issue-status tokens, optionally including `all`."""
    73→    if include_all:
    74→        return frozenset({*_CANONICAL_ISSUE_STATUSES, "all"})
    75→    return _CANONICAL_ISSUE_STATUSES
    76→
    77→
    78→def resolved_statuses() -> frozenset[str]:
    79→    """Return the set of statuses that mean an issue is no longer open."""
    80→    return _RESOLVED_STATUSES
    81→
    82→
    83→__all__ = [
    84→    "Confidence",
    85→    "Status",
    86→    "Tier",
    87→    "canonical_issue_status",
    88→    "issue_status_tokens",
    89→    "resolved_statuses",
    90→]
    91→

    57→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    58→class NarrativeContext:
    59→    """Context inputs for narrative computation."""
    60→

    65→    plan: dict[str, Any] | None = None
    66→
    67→
    68→def compute_narrative(
    69→    state: StateModel,
    70→    context: NarrativeContext | None = None,
    71→) -> NarrativeResult:
    72→    """Compute structured narrative context from state data."""
    73→    resolved_context = context or NarrativeContext(command="")
    74→
    75→    diff = resolved_context.diff
    76→    lang = resolved_context.lang
    77→    command = resolved_context.command
    78→    config = resolved_context.config
    79→    plan = resolved_context.plan
    80→
    81→    raw_history = state.get("scan_history", [])
    82→    history = _history_for_lang(raw_history, lang)
    83→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
    84→    stats = state.get("stats", {})
    85→    strict_score, overall_score = _score_snapshot(state)
    86→    issues = _scoped_issues(state)
    87→
    88→    by_detector = _count_open_by_detector(issues)
    89→    badge = _compute_badge_status()
    90→
    91→    phase = detect_phase(history, strict_score)
    92→    dimensions = _analyze_dimensions(dim_scores, history, state)
    93→    debt = _analyze_debt(dim_scores, issues, history)
    94→    milestone = detect_milestone(state, None, history)
    95→    clusters = plan.get("clusters") if isinstance(plan, dict) else None
    96→    action_context = ActionContext(
    97→        by_detector=by_detector,
    98→        dimension_scores=dim_scores,
    99→        state=state,
   100→        debt=debt,
   101→        lang=lang,
   102→        clusters=clusters,
   103→    )
   104→    actions = [dict(action) for action in compute_actions(action_context)]
   105→    strategy = compute_strategy(issues, by_detector, actions, phase, lang)
   106→    tools = dict(compute_tools(by_detector, state, lang, badge))
   107→    primary_action = _compute_primary_action(actions)
   108→    why_now = _compute_why_now(phase, strategy, primary_action)
   109→    verification_step = _compute_verification_step(command)
   110→    risk_flags = _compute_risk_flags(state, debt)
   111→    strict_target = _compute_strict_target(strict_score, config)
   112→    headline = compute_headline(
   113→        phase,
   114→        dimensions,
   115→        debt,
   116→        milestone,
   117→        diff,
   118→        strict_score,
   119→        overall_score,
   120→        stats,
   121→        history,
   122→        open_by_detector=by_detector,
   123→        scan_source=scan_source(state) or None,
   124→    )
   125→    reminders, updated_reminder_history = compute_reminders(
   126→        state, lang, phase, debt, actions, dimensions, badge, command, config=config
   127→    )
   128→
   129→    return {

     6→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.finalize import finalize_queue
     7→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import ATTEST_EXAMPLE
     8→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.inputs import resolve_queue_inputs

    20→def build_work_queue(
    21→    state: StateModel,
    22→    *,
    23→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    24→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    25→    """Build the active canonical work queue for the current invocation."""
    26→    return _build_work_queue_with_visibility(
    27→        state,
    28→        options=options,
    29→        visibility=QueueVisibility.ALL,
    30→    )
    31→
    32→
    33→def _build_work_queue_with_visibility(
    34→    state: StateModel,
    35→    *,
    36→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    37→    visibility: str = QueueVisibility.ALL,
    38→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    39→    """Build a ranked work queue from canonical queue partitions.
    40→
    41→    Open-status queue views are resolved from the canonical queue snapshot so
    42→    lifecycle phase, execution visibility, and backlog visibility all come
    43→    from one source of truth. Non-open status views remain issue-list queries.
    44→    """
    45→    opts = options or QueueBuildOptions()
    46→    plan, scan_path, status, threshold = resolve_queue_inputs(opts, state)
    47→    items = select_queue_items(
    48→        state,
    49→        opts=opts,
    50→        plan=plan,
    51→        scan_path=scan_path,
    52→        status=status,
    53→        threshold=threshold,
    54→        visibility=visibility,
    55→    )
    56→    return finalize_queue(items, state=state, plan=plan, opts=opts)

    40→def resolve_target_strict_score(config: dict | None) -> tuple[int, str | None]:
    41→    """Resolve strict-score target from config with bounded fallback."""
    42→    raw_target = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
    43→    if isinstance(config, dict):
    44→        raw_target = config.get("target_strict_score", DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE)
    45→    try:
    46→        target = int(raw_target)
    47→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    48→        return (
    49→            DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    50→            (
    51→                f"Invalid config `target_strict_score={raw_target!r}`; using "
    52→                f"{DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE}"
    53→            ),
    54→        )
    55→    if target < MIN_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE or target > MAX_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE:
    56→        return (
    57→            DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    58→            (
    59→                f"Invalid config `target_strict_score={raw_target!r}`; using "
    60→                f"{DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE}"
    61→            ),
    62→        )
    63→    return target, None
    64→
    65→
    66→def compute_strict_target(strict_score: float | None, config: dict | None) -> StrictTarget:
    67→    """Build strict-target context for command rendering and agents."""
    68→    target, warning = resolve_target_strict_score(config)
    69→    if not isinstance(strict_score, int | float):
    70→        return {
    71→            "target": float(target),
    72→            "current": None,
    73→            "gap": None,
    74→            "state": "unavailable",
    75→            "warning": warning,
    76→        }
    77→
    78→    current = round(float(strict_score), 1)
    79→    gap = round(float(target) - current, 1)
    80→    if gap > 0:
    81→        state = "below"
    82→    elif gap < 0:
    83→        state = "above"
    84→    else:
    85→        state = "at"
    86→    return {
    87→        "target": float(target),
    88→        "current": current,
    89→        "gap": gap,
    90→        "state": state,
    91→        "warning": warning,
    92→    }
    93→
    94→
    95→def count_open_by_detector(issues: dict) -> dict[str, int]:
    96→    """Count open issues by detector, merging structural sub-detectors."""
    97→    by_detector: dict[str, int] = {}
    98→    for issue in issues.values():
    99→        if issue["status"] != "open" or issue.get("suppressed"):
   100→            continue
   101→        detector = issue.get("detector", "unknown")
   102→        if detector in STRUCTURAL_MERGE:
   103→            detector = "structural"
   104→        by_detector[detector] = by_detector.get(detector, 0) + 1
   105→        if detector == "review" and issue.get("detail", {}).get("holistic"):
   106→            by_detector["review_holistic"] = by_detector.get("review_holistic", 0) + 1
   107→    if by_detector.get("review", 0) > 0:
   108→        by_detector["review_uninvestigated"] = sum(
   109→            1
   110→            for issue in issues.values()
   111→            if issue.get("status") == "open"
   112→            and not issue.get("suppressed")
   113→            and issue.get("detector") == "review"
   114→            and not issue.get("detail", {}).get("investigation")
   115→        )
   116→    return by_detector
   117→
   118→
   119→def resolve_badge_path(project_root: Path) -> tuple[str, Path]:
   120→    """Resolve badge path from config, defaulting to root-level scorecard.png."""
   121→    default_rel = "scorecard.png"
   122→    config = {}
   123→    try:
   124→        config = _load_config()
   125→    except (AttributeError, OSError):
   126→        config = {}
   127→
   128→    raw_path = default_rel
   129→    if isinstance(config, dict):
   130→        config.setdefault("badge_path", default_rel)
   131→        configured = config.get("badge_path")
   132→        if isinstance(configured, str) and configured.strip():
   133→            raw_path = configured.strip()
   134→
   135→    path = Path(raw_path)
   136→    is_root_anchored = bool(path.root)
   137→    if not path.is_absolute() and not is_root_anchored:
   138→        return raw_path, project_root / path
   139→
   140→    try:
   141→        rel_path = str(path.relative_to(project_root))
   142→    except ValueError:
   143→        rel_path = str(path)
   144→    return rel_path, path
   145→
   146→
   147→def compute_badge_status() -> BadgeStatus:
   148→    """Check configured scorecard path and whether README references it."""
   149→    project_root = get_project_root()
   150→    scorecard_rel, scorecard_path = resolve_badge_path(project_root)
   151→    generated = scorecard_path.exists()
   152→
   153→    in_readme = False
   154→    if generated:
   155→        for readme_name in ("README.md", "readme.md", "README.MD"):
   156→            readme_path = project_root / readme_name
   157→            if readme_path.exists():
   158→                try:
   159→                    in_readme = scorecard_rel in readme_path.read_text(
   160→                        encoding="utf-8", errors="replace"
   161→                    )
   162→                except OSError:
   163→                    in_readme = False
   164→                break
   165→
   166→    recommendation = None
   167→    if generated and not in_readme:
   168→        recommendation = f'Add to README: <img src="{scorecard_rel}" width="100%">'
   169→
   170→    return {
   171→        "generated": generated,
   172→        "in_readme": in_readme,
   173→        "path": scorecard_rel,
   174→        "recommendation": recommendation,
   175→    }
   176→
   177→
   178→def compute_primary_action(actions: list[dict]) -> PrimaryAction | None:
   179→    """Pick the highest-priority action for user-facing guidance."""
   180→    if not actions:
   181→        return None
   182→    top = actions[0]
   183→    command = str(top.get("command", "")).strip()
   184→    if not command:
   185→        return None
   186→    description = str(top.get("description", "")).strip() or "run highest-impact action"
   187→    return {
   188→        "command": command,
   189→        "description": description,
   190→    }
   191→
   192→
   193→def compute_why_now(
   194→    phase: str,
   195→    strategy: dict[str, object],
   196→    primary_action: dict | None,
   197→) -> str:
   198→    """Explain why the recommended action should happen now."""
   199→    hint = str(strategy.get("hint", "")).strip() if isinstance(strategy, dict) else ""
   200→    if hint:
   201→        return hint
   202→    if primary_action and primary_action.get("description"):
   203→        return str(primary_action["description"])
   204→    phase_default = {
   205→        "first_scan": "Start with highest-impact issues to establish a clean baseline.",
   206→        "regression": "Recent regressions should be contained before new work.",
   207→        "stagnation": "Current approach is stalling; tackle a different high-impact lane.",
   208→        "maintenance": "Keep the codebase stable by resolving new risk quickly.",
   209→    }
   210→    return phase_default.get(phase, "Address the highest-impact open issues first.")
   211→
   212→
   213→def compute_verification_step(_command: str | None) -> VerificationStep:
   214→    """Verification step returned with every narrative plan."""
   215→    return {
   216→        "command": "desloppify scan",
   217→        "reason": "revalidate after changes",
   218→    }
   219→
   220→
   221→def compute_risk_flags(state: StateModel, debt: dict) -> list[RiskFlag]:
   222→    """Build ordered risk flags from suppression and wontfix debt signals."""
   223→    flags: list[RiskFlag] = []
   224→
   225→    ignore_integrity = state.get("ignore_integrity", {})
   226→    suppressed_pct = float(ignore_integrity.get("suppressed_pct", 0.0) or 0.0)
   227→    ignored_count = int(ignore_integrity.get("ignored", 0) or 0)
   228→    if suppressed_pct >= _HIGH_IGNORE_SUPPRESSION_THRESHOLD or ignored_count >= 100:
   229→        severity = "high" if suppressed_pct >= 40.0 or ignored_count >= 200 else "medium"
   230→        message = (
   231→            f"{suppressed_pct:.1f}% issues hidden by ignore patterns"
   232→            if suppressed_pct > 0
   233→            else f"{ignored_count} issues hidden by ignore patterns"
   234→        )
   235→        flags.append(
   236→            {
   237→                "type": "high_ignore_suppression",
   238→                "severity": severity,
   239→                "message": message,
   240→            }
   241→        )
   242→
   243→    wontfix_count = int(debt.get("wontfix_count", 0) or 0)
   244→    overall_gap = float(debt.get("overall_gap", 0.0) or 0.0)
   245→    if overall_gap >= _WONTFIX_GAP_THRESHOLD or wontfix_count > 0:
   246→        severity = "high" if overall_gap >= 5.0 or wontfix_count >= 50 else "medium"
   247→        flags.append(
   248→            {
   249→                "type": "wontfix_gap",
   250→                "severity": severity,
   251→                "message": (
   252→                    f"Strict/lenient gap is {overall_gap:.1f} pts with "
   253→                    f"{wontfix_count} wontfix issues"
   254→                ),
   255→            }
   256→        )
   257→
   258→    flags.sort(key=lambda flag: _RISK_SEVERITY_ORDER.get(flag.get("severity"), 99))
   259→    return flags
   260→
   261→
   262→def history_for_lang(raw_history: list[dict], lang: str | None) -> list[dict]:
   263→    if not lang:
   264→        return raw_history
   265→    return [entry for entry in raw_history if entry.get("lang") in (lang, None)]
   266→
   267→
   268→def scoped_issues(state: StateModel) -> dict[str, Issue]:
   269→    return path_scoped_issues(
   270→        state.get("issues", {}), state.get("scan_path")
   271→    )
   272→
   273→
   274→def score_snapshot(state: StateModel) -> tuple[float | None, float | None]:
   275→    scores = state_score_snapshot(state)
   276→    return scores.strict, scores.overall
   277→
   278→
   279→__all__ = [
   280→    "compute_badge_status",
   281→    "compute_primary_action",
   282→    "compute_risk_flags",
   283→    "compute_strict_target",
   284→    "compute_verification_step",
   285→    "compute_why_now",
   286→    "count_open_by_detector",
   287→    "history_for_lang",
   288→    "resolve_target_strict_score",
   289→    "score_snapshot",
   290→    "scoped_issues",
   291→]
   292→

1→"""Queue item selection from canonical snapshot or raw issue lists."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import scope_matches
     6→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.inputs import gather_subjective_items
     7→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.models import QueueBuildOptions, QueueVisibility
     8→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import build_issue_items
     9→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.snapshot import build_queue_snapshot
    10→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.types import WorkQueueItem
    11→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    12→
    13→
    14→def select_queue_items(
    15→    state: StateModel,
    16→    *,
    17→    opts: QueueBuildOptions,
    18→    plan: dict | None,
    19→    scan_path: str | None,
    20→    status: str,
    21→    threshold: float,
    22→    visibility: str,
    23→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
    24→    """Select the raw queue item list before ranking/finalization."""
    25→    if status == "open" and not opts.chronic:
    26→        snapshot = build_queue_snapshot(
    27→            state,
    28→            options=opts,
    29→            plan=plan,
    30→            target_strict=threshold,
    31→        )
    32→        return filter_snapshot_items(
    33→            items_for_visibility(snapshot=snapshot, visibility=visibility),
    34→            opts,
    35→        )
    36→
    37→    items = build_issue_items(
    38→        state,
    39→        scan_path=scan_path,
    40→        status_filter=status,
    41→        scope=opts.scope,
    42→        chronic=opts.chronic,
    43→    )
    44→    if opts.include_subjective and status == "all":
    45→        items += gather_subjective_items(state, opts, threshold)
    46→    return items
    47→
    48→
    49→def items_for_visibility(*, snapshot, visibility: str) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
    50→    """Select the snapshot partition for one queue surface."""
    51→    if visibility == QueueVisibility.BACKLOG:
    52→        return [dict(item) for item in snapshot.backlog_items]
    53→    return [dict(item) for item in snapshot.execution_items]
    54→
    55→
    56→def filter_snapshot_items(
    57→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
    58→    opts: QueueBuildOptions,
    59→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
    60→    """Apply view-local filtering after snapshot partition selection."""
    61→    filtered = items
    62→    if not opts.include_subjective:
    63→        filtered = [
    64→            item for item in filtered
    65→            if item.get("kind") != "subjective_dimension"
    66→        ]
    67→    if opts.scope:
    68→        filtered = [
    69→            item for item in filtered
    70→            if item.get("kind") != "subjective_dimension" or scope_matches(item, opts.scope)
    71→        ]
    72→    return filtered
    73→
    74→
    75→__all__ = [
    76→    "filter_snapshot_items",
    77→    "items_for_visibility",
    78→    "select_queue_items",
    79→]
    80→

    28→def write_show_output_file(output_file: str, payload: dict, surfaced_count: int) -> bool:
    29→    """Write serialized show payload to file."""
    30→    try:
    31→        safe_write_text(output_file, json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + "\n")
    32→        print(colorize(f"Wrote {surfaced_count} issues to {output_file}", "green"))
    33→    except OSError as exc:
    34→        payload["output_error"] = str(exc)
    35→        print_write_error(output_file, exc, label="show output")
    36→        return False
    37→    return True

    39→
    40→def group_matches_by_file(matches: list[dict]) -> list[tuple[str, list]]:
    41→    """Group issues by file and sort by descending count."""
    42→    by_file: dict[str, list] = defaultdict(list)
    43→    for issue in matches:
    44→        by_file[issue["file"]].append(issue)
    45→    return sorted(by_file.items(), key=lambda item: -len(item[1]))
    46→
    47→
    48→def _print_single_issue(issue: dict, *, show_code: bool) -> None:
    49→    """Render a single issue to terminal."""
    50→    normalized_status = canonical_issue_status(issue.get("status"))
    51→    status_icon = {
    52→        "open": "○",
    53→        "fixed": "✓",
    54→        "wontfix": "—",
    55→        "false_positive": "✗",
    56→        "auto_resolved": "◌",
    57→        "deferred": "⏸",
    58→        "triaged_out": "△",
    59→    }.get(normalized_status, "?")
    60→    zone = issue.get("zone", "production")
    61→    zone_tag = colorize(f" [{zone}]", "dim") if zone != "production" else ""
    62→    print(
    63→        f"    {status_icon} T{issue['tier']} [{issue['confidence']}] {issue['summary']}{zone_tag}"
    64→    )
    65→
    66→    detail_parts = format_detail(issue.get("detail", {}))
    67→    if detail_parts:
    68→        print(colorize(f"      {' · '.join(detail_parts)}", "dim"))
    69→    if show_code:
    70→        detail_raw = issue.get("detail", {})
    71→        detail = detail_raw if isinstance(detail_raw, dict) else {}
    72→        target_line = (
    73→            detail.get("line") or (detail.get("lines", [None]) or [None])[0]
    74→        )
    75→        if target_line and issue["file"] not in (".", ""):
    76→            snippet = read_code_snippet(issue["file"], target_line)
    77→            if snippet:
    78→                print(snippet)
    79→    if issue.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2:
    80→        print(
    81→            colorize(
    82→                f"      ⟳ reopened {issue['reopen_count']} times — fix properly or wontfix",
    83→                "red",
    84→            )
    85→        )
    86→    if issue.get("note"):
    87→        print(colorize(f"      note: {issue['note']}", "dim"))
    88→    print(colorize(f"      {issue['id']}", "dim"))
    89→
    90→
    91→def render_issues(
    92→    matches: list[dict],
    93→    *,

    95→    status_filter: str,
    96→    show_code: bool,
    97→    top: int,

   100→    noise_budget: int,
   101→    global_noise_budget: int,
   102→    budget_warning: str | None,
   103→) -> None:
   104→    """Render grouped issues and rollup summary to terminal."""
   105→    sorted_files = group_matches_by_file(matches)
   106→    print(

   109→            "bold",

   111→    )
   112→    if budget_warning:

   114→    if hidden_total:

   117→        )

   135→

   260→def show_subjective_followup(
   261→    state: dict, target_strict_score: float, *, objective_backlog: int = 0,
   262→) -> None:
   263→    """Show subjective follow-up guidance for the current state."""
   264→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   265→    if not dim_scores:
   266→        return
   267→
   268→    subjective = reporting_dimensions_mod.scorecard_subjective_entries(
   269→        state,
   270→        dim_scores=dim_scores,
   271→    )
   272→    if not subjective:
   273→        return
   274→
   275→    followup = reporting_dimensions_mod.build_subjective_followup(
   276→        state,
   277→        subjective,
   278→        threshold=target_strict_score,
   279→        max_quality_items=3,
   280→        max_integrity_items=5,
   281→    )
   282→    if print_subjective_followup(followup, objective_backlog=objective_backlog):
   283→        print()
   284→
   285→
   286→__all__ = [
   287→    "group_matches_by_file",
   288→    "render_issues",
   289→    "show_agent_plan",
   290→    "show_subjective_followup",

    39→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

    41→
    42→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    43→class ShowOptions:
    44→    """All user-facing show command options extracted once from argparse."""
    45→
    46→    pattern_raw: str = ""
    47→    show_code: bool = False
    48→    chronic: bool = False
    49→    no_budget: bool = False
    50→    output_file: str | None = None
    51→    top: int = 20
    52→
    53→    @classmethod
    54→    def from_args(cls, args: argparse.Namespace) -> ShowOptions:
    55→        return cls(
    56→            pattern_raw=str(getattr(args, "pattern", "") or ""),
    57→            show_code=bool(getattr(args, "code", False)),
    58→            chronic=bool(getattr(args, "chronic", False)),
    59→            no_budget=bool(getattr(args, "no_budget", False)),
    60→            output_file=getattr(args, "output", None),
    61→            top=int(getattr(args, "top", 20) or 20),
    62→        )
    63→
    64→
    65→def _handle_special_entity_views(
    66→    *,
    67→    entity,
    68→    state: dict,
    69→    config: dict,
    70→    lang_name: str | None,
    71→    pattern_raw: str,
    72→) -> bool:
    73→    if entity.kind == "special_view" and entity.pattern.strip().lower() == "concerns":
    74→        _show_concerns(state, lang_name)
    75→        return True
    76→    if entity.kind == "dimension" and entity.is_subjective:
    77→        _render_subjective_dimension(state, config, entity, pattern_raw)
    78→        return True
    79→    return False
    80→
    81→
    82→def _load_entity_matches(
    83→    *,
    84→    state: dict,
    85→    entity,
    86→    pattern: str,
    87→    status_filter: str,
    88→    scope: str | None,
    89→    chronic: bool,
    90→) -> tuple[str, list[dict]] | None:
    91→    if entity.kind != "dimension" or entity.is_subjective:
    92→        return pattern, load_matches(
    93→            state, scope=scope, status_filter=status_filter, chronic=chronic
    94→        )
    95→    matches = _load_dimension_issues(state, entity, status_filter)
    96→    if not matches:
    97→        _render_clean_mechanical_dimension(state, entity)
    98→        return None
    99→    return entity.display_name, matches
   100→
   101→
   102→def _active_plan_or_none() -> dict | None:
   103→    try:
   104→        plan = load_plan()
   105→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   106→        logger.debug("Show command skipped active-plan lookup.", exc_info=exc)
   107→        return None
   108→    if plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("clusters"):
   109→        return plan
   110→    return None
   111→
   112→
   113→def cmd_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   114→    """Show all issues for a file, directory, detector, or pattern."""
   115→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   116→    state = runtime.state
   117→    config = runtime.config
   118→
   119→    if not require_issue_inventory(state):
   120→        return
   121→
   122→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
   123→    if skill_warning:
   124→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
   125→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
   126→    if config_warning:
   127→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
   128→
   129→    print_triage_guardrail_info(state=state)
   130→
   131→    opts = ShowOptions.from_args(args)
   132→
   133→    ok, pattern, status_filter, scope = resolve_show_scope(args)
   134→    if not ok or pattern is None:
   135→        return
   136→
   137→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   138→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   139→    narrative = compute_narrative(
   140→        state,
   141→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="show"),
   142→    )
   143→
   144→    entity = resolve_entity(pattern, state)
   145→    if _handle_special_entity_views(
   146→        entity=entity,
   147→        state=state,
   148→        config=config,
   149→        lang_name=lang_name,
   150→        pattern_raw=opts.pattern_raw,
   151→    ):
   152→        return
   153→
   154→    match_result = _load_entity_matches(
   155→        state=state,
   156→        entity=entity,
   157→        pattern=pattern,
   158→        status_filter=status_filter,
   159→        scope=scope,
   160→        chronic=opts.chronic,
   161→    )
   162→    if match_result is None:
   163→        return
   164→    pattern, matches = match_result
   165→
   166→    if not matches:
   167→        _render_no_matches(entity, pattern, status_filter, narrative, state, config)
   168→        return
   169→
   170→    (
   171→        surfaced_matches,
   172→        hidden_by_detector,
   173→        noise_budget,
   174→        global_noise_budget,
   175→        budget_warning,
   176→    ) = resolve_noise(
   177→        config,
   178→        matches,
   179→        no_budget=opts.no_budget,
   180→    )
   181→    hidden_total = sum(hidden_by_detector.values())
   182→
   183→    payload = build_show_payload(
   184→        surfaced_matches,
   185→        pattern,
   186→        status_filter,
   187→        ShowPayloadMeta(
   188→            total_matches=len(matches),
   189→            hidden_by_detector=hidden_by_detector,
   190→            noise_budget=noise_budget,
   191→            global_noise_budget=global_noise_budget,
   192→        ),
   193→    )
   194→    write_query({"command": "show", **payload, "narrative": narrative})
   195→
   196→    if opts.output_file:
   197→        if write_show_output_file(opts.output_file, payload, len(surfaced_matches)):
   198→            return
   199→        raise CommandError("Failed to write output file")
   200→
   201→    render_issues(
   202→        surfaced_matches,
   203→        pattern=pattern,
   204→        status_filter=status_filter,
   205→        show_code=opts.show_code,
   206→        top=opts.top,
   207→        hidden_by_detector=hidden_by_detector,
   208→        hidden_total=hidden_total,
   209→        noise_budget=noise_budget,
   210→        global_noise_budget=global_noise_budget,
   211→        budget_warning=budget_warning,
   212→    )
   213→    plan_active = _active_plan_or_none()
   214→    show_agent_plan(narrative, surfaced_matches, plan=plan_active)
   215→    show_subjective_followup(
   216→        state,
   217→        target_strict_score_from_config(config),
   218→    )
   219→
   220→    _render_subjective_views_guide(entity)
   221→
   222→
   223→__all__ = ["cmd_show"]
   224→

1→"""Score and diff summary output for scan command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score_update import print_strict_target_nudge
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.scan.helpers import format_delta
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.scan.reporting.agent_context import is_agent_environment
    12→from desloppify.app.commands.status.strict_target import (
    13→    format_strict_target_progress,
    14→)
    15→from desloppify.base.output.fallbacks import log_best_effort_failure
    16→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    17→from desloppify.engine._concerns.generators import generate_concerns
    18→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    19→
    20→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    21→
    22→
    23→def _consecutive_subjective_integrity_status(
    24→    state: StateModel, status: str
    25→) -> int:
    26→    """Return consecutive trailing scans with the given subjective-integrity status."""
    27→    history = state.get("scan_history", [])
    28→    if not isinstance(history, list):
    29→        return 0
    30→
    31→    streak = 0
    32→    for entry in reversed(history):
    33→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
    34→            break
    35→        integrity = entry.get("subjective_integrity")
    36→        if not isinstance(integrity, dict):
    37→            break
    38→        if integrity.get("status") != status:
    39→            break
    40→        streak += 1
    41→    return streak
    42→
    43→
    44→def _show_score_reveal(
    45→    state: StateModel,
    46→    new: state_mod.ScoreSnapshot,
    47→    *,
    48→    target_strict: float | None = None,
    49→) -> None:
    50→    """Show before/after score comparison when a queue cycle just completed.
    51→
    52→    This fires when the plan had ``plan_start_scores`` and the queue was empty
    53→    at scan time (i.e. the reconcile block cleared plan_start_scores).  We detect
    54→    this by checking the plan *before* the clear happened — since merge_scan_results
    55→    clears it, we peek at the ``_last_score_reveal`` stash it leaves behind.
    56→    """
    57→    # scan_workflow stashes the old plan_start_scores on state as a transient
    58→    # key when it clears them (queue empty).  Pop it here for the reveal.
    59→    plan_start = state.pop("_plan_start_scores_for_reveal", None)
    60→    if not isinstance(plan_start, dict) or not plan_start.get("strict"):
    61→        return
    62→
    63→    old_strict = float(plan_start["strict"])
    64→    new_strict = float(new.strict or 0)
    65→    delta = round(new_strict - old_strict, 1)
    66→    delta_str = f" ({'+' if delta > 0 else ''}{delta:.1f})" if abs(delta) >= 0.05 else ""
    67→
    68→    bar = "=" * 50
    69→    print(colorize(f"  {bar}", "cyan"))
    70→    print(colorize("  SCORE UPDATE — Queue cycle complete!", "bold"))
    71→    print(colorize(f"  Plan-start: strict {old_strict:.1f}/100", "dim"))
    72→    print(colorize(f"  Updated:    strict {new_strict:.1f}/100{delta_str}", "cyan"))
    73→    if target_strict is not None:
    74→        target_gap = round(target_strict - new_strict, 1)
    75→        if target_gap > 0:
    76→            print(colorize(f"  Target:     {target_strict:.1f} (+{target_gap:.1f} to go)", "dim"))
    77→        else:
    78→            print(colorize(f"  Target:     {target_strict:.1f} — reached!", "green"))
    79→    print(colorize(f"  {bar}", "cyan"))
    80→
    81→
    82→def show_diff_summary(diff: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    83→    """Print the +new / -resolved / reopened one-liner."""
    84→    diff_parts = []
    85→    if diff["new"]:
    86→        diff_parts.append(colorize(f"+{diff['new']} new", "yellow"))
    87→    if diff["auto_resolved"]:
    88→        diff_parts.append(colorize(f"-{diff['auto_resolved']} resolved", "green"))
    89→    if diff["reopened"]:
    90→        diff_parts.append(colorize(f"↻{diff['reopened']} reopened", "red"))
    91→    if diff_parts:
    92→        print(f"  {' · '.join(diff_parts)}")
    93→    else:
    94→        print(colorize("  No changes since last scan", "dim"))
    95→    if diff.get("suspect_detectors"):
    96→        print(
    97→            colorize(
    98→                "  ⚠ Skipped auto-resolve for: "
    99→                f"{', '.join(diff['suspect_detectors'])} (returned 0 — likely transient)",
   100→                "yellow",
   101→            )
   102→        )
   103→
   104→
   105→def _has_complete_scores(new: state_mod.ScoreSnapshot) -> bool:
   106→    return not (
   107→        new.overall is None
   108→        or new.objective is None
   109→        or new.strict is None
   110→        or new.verified is None
   111→    )
   112→
   113→
   114→def _unscored_subjective_callout(state: StateModel) -> None:
   115→    """Print a callout when subjective dimensions are unassessed, clarifying score composition."""
   116→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   117→    if not dim_scores:
   118→        return
   119→
   120→    total_subj, unscored_subj = _subjective_unscored_counts(dim_scores)
   121→    if unscored_subj == 0 or total_subj == 0:
   122→        return
   123→
   124→    print(colorize(_unscored_subjective_message(dim_scores, total_subj, unscored_subj), "yellow"))
   125→
   126→
   127→def _subjective_unscored_counts(dim_scores: dict[str, Any]) -> tuple[int, int]:
   128→    total_subj = 0
   129→    unscored_subj = 0
   130→    for data in dim_scores.values():
   131→        if not isinstance(data, dict):
   132→            continue
   133→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
   134→        if "subjective_assessment" not in detectors:
   135→            continue
   136→        total_subj += 1
   137→        meta = detectors.get("subjective_assessment", {})
   138→        if isinstance(meta, dict) and meta.get("placeholder"):
   139→            unscored_subj += 1
   140→    return total_subj, unscored_subj
   141→
   142→
   143→def _subjective_fraction_percent(dim_scores: dict[str, Any]) -> int:
   144→    try:
   145→        import desloppify.engine._scoring.results.core as scoring_mod
   146→
   147→        breakdown = scoring_mod.compute_health_breakdown(dim_scores)
   148→        return round(float(breakdown.get("subjective_fraction", 0.0) or 0.0) * 100)
   149→    except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError):
   150→        return 0
   151→
   152→
   153→def _unscored_subjective_message(
   154→    dim_scores: dict[str, Any],
   155→    total_subj: int,
   156→    unscored_subj: int,
   157→) -> str:
   158→    if unscored_subj != total_subj:
   159→        return (
   160→            f"  ⚠ {unscored_subj} of {total_subj} subjective dimensions unassessed (scored as 0). "
   161→            f"Strict score is lower than expected until these are reviewed."
   162→        )
   163→
   164→    subject_word = "dimensions" if unscored_subj != 1 else "dimension"
   165→    verb = "are" if unscored_subj != 1 else "is"
   166→    msg = (
   167→        f"  ⚠ {unscored_subj} subjective {subject_word} {verb} unassessed (scored as 0). "
   168→    )
   169→    subj_pct = _subjective_fraction_percent(dim_scores)
   170→    if subj_pct:
   171→        msg += (
   172→            f"The strict score currently reflects only mechanical detectors "
   173→            f"({100 - subj_pct}% of score weight). "
   174→        )
   175→    return msg + "Run `desloppify review --prepare` to assess."
   176→
   177→
   178→def _print_score_guide() -> None:
   179→    print(colorize("  Score guide:", "dim"))
   180→    print(colorize("    overall  = 25% mechanical + 75% subjective (lenient — ignores wontfix)", "dim"))
   181→    print(colorize("    objective = mechanical detectors only (no subjective review)", "dim"))
   182→    print(colorize("    strict   = like overall, but wontfix counts against you  <-- your north star", "dim"))
   183→    print(colorize("    verified = strict, but only credits scan-verified fixes", "dim"))
   184→
   185→
   186→def _subjective_target_label(target: object) -> str:
   187→    if target is None:
   188→        return "target threshold"
   189→    return f"target {target}"
   190→
   191→
   192→def _print_subjective_integrity_warning(
   193→    state: StateModel,
   194→    integrity: dict[str, Any],
   195→) -> None:
   196→    status = integrity.get("status")
   197→    matched_count = int(integrity.get("matched_count", 0) or 0)
   198→    target = integrity.get("target_score")
   199→    target_label = _subjective_target_label(target)
   200→
   201→    if status == "penalized":
   202→        print(
   203→            colorize(
   204→                "  ⚠ Subjective integrity: "
   205→                f"{matched_count} target-matched dimensions were reset to 0.0 "
   206→                f"({target_label}).",
   207→                "red",
   208→            )
   209→        )
   210→        streak = _consecutive_subjective_integrity_status(state, "penalized")
   211→        if streak < 2:
   212→            return
   213→        print(
   214→            colorize(
   215→                "    Repeated penalty across scans. Use a blind, isolated reviewer "
   216→                "on `.desloppify/review_packet_blind.json` and re-import before trusting subjective scores.",
   217→                "yellow",
   218→            )
   219→        )
   220→        return
   221→
   222→    if status != "warn":
   223→        return
   224→    print(
   225→        colorize(
   226→            "  ⚠ Subjective integrity: "
   227→            f"{matched_count} dimension matched the target "
   228→            f"({target_label}). Re-review recommended.",
   229→            "yellow",
   230→        )
   231→    )
   232→    streak = _consecutive_subjective_integrity_status(state, "warn")
   233→    if streak < 2:
   234→        return
   235→    print(
   236→        colorize(
   237→            "    This warning has repeated. Prefer "
   238→            "`desloppify review --prepare` and run a trusted review "
   239→            "(see skill doc for options).",
   240→            "yellow",
   241→        )
   242→    )
   243→
   244→
   245→def _print_score_quartet(
   246→    new: state_mod.ScoreSnapshot,
   247→    prev_overall: float | None,
   248→    prev_objective: float | None,
   249→    prev_strict: float | None,
   250→    prev_verified: float | None,
   251→    non_comparable_reason: str | None,
   252→) -> None:
   253→    """Print the four-score comparison line with deltas."""
   254→    overall_delta_str, overall_color = format_delta(new.overall, prev_overall)
   255→    objective_delta_str, objective_color = format_delta(new.objective, prev_objective)
   256→    strict_delta_str, strict_color = format_delta(new.strict, prev_strict)
   257→    verified_delta_str, verified_color = format_delta(new.verified, prev_verified)
   258→    print(
   259→        "  Scores: "
   260→        + colorize(f"overall {new.overall:.1f}/100{overall_delta_str}", overall_color)
   261→        + colorize(
   262→            f"  objective {new.objective:.1f}/100{objective_delta_str}",
   263→            objective_color,
   264→        )
   265→        + colorize(f"  strict {new.strict:.1f}/100{strict_delta_str}", strict_color)
   266→        + colorize(
   267→            f"  verified {new.verified:.1f}/100{verified_delta_str}",
   268→            verified_color,
   269→        )
   270→    )
   271→    if isinstance(non_comparable_reason, str) and non_comparable_reason.strip():
   272→        print(colorize(f"  Δ non-comparable: {non_comparable_reason.strip()}", "yellow"))
   273→
   274→
   275→def _print_wontfix_gap(
   276→    wontfix: int,

   307→def show_score_delta(
   308→    state: StateModel,
   309→    prev_overall: float | None,
   310→    prev_objective: float | None,
   311→    prev_strict: float | None,
   312→    prev_verified: float | None = None,
   313→    non_comparable_reason: str | None = None,
   314→    *,
   315→    target_strict: float | None = None,
   316→) -> None:
   317→    """Print the canonical score quartet with deltas."""
   318→    stats = state["stats"]
   319→    new = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   320→
   321→    if not _has_complete_scores(new):
   322→        print(
   323→            colorize(
   324→                "  Scores unavailable — run a full scan with language detectors enabled.",
   325→                "yellow",
   326→            )
   327→        )
   328→        return
   329→
   330→    _show_score_reveal(state, new, target_strict=target_strict)
   331→
   332→    _print_score_quartet(
   333→        new, prev_overall, prev_objective, prev_strict, prev_verified,
   334→        non_comparable_reason,
   335→    )
   336→
   337→    _unscored_subjective_callout(state)
   338→
   339→    gap = (new.overall or 0) - (new.strict or 0)
   340→    _print_wontfix_gap(stats.get("wontfix", 0), gap)
   341→    _print_score_legend(state, gap)
   342→
   343→    if target_strict is not None and new.strict is not None:
   344→        print_strict_target_nudge(new.strict, target_strict, show_next=False)
   345→
   346→    _print_integrity_warnings(state)
   347→
   348→
   349→def show_concern_count(state: StateModel, lang_name: str | None = None) -> None:
   350→    """Print concern count if any exist."""
   351→    try:
   352→        concerns = generate_concerns(state)
   353→        if concerns:
   354→            print(
   355→                colorize(
   356→                    f"  {len(concerns)} potential design concern{'s' if len(concerns) != 1 else ''}"
   357→                    " (run `show concerns` to view)",
   358→                    "cyan",
   359→                )
   360→            )
   361→    except (ImportError, AttributeError, TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
   362→        log_best_effort_failure(logger, "generate best-effort concern summary", exc)
   363→
   364→
   365→def show_strict_target_progress(
   366→    strict_target: dict[str, Any] | None,
   367→) -> tuple[float | None, float | None]:
   368→    """Print strict target progress lines and return (target, gap)."""
   369→    lines, target, gap = format_strict_target_progress(strict_target)
   370→    for message, style in lines:
   371→        print(colorize(message, style))
   372→    return target, gap
   373→
   374→
   375→__all__ = ["show_concern_count", "show_diff_summary", "show_score_delta", "show_strict_target_progress"]
   376→

1→"""Text templates for scan reporting output."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from textwrap import dedent
     6→
     7→
     8→def build_workflow_guide(attest_example: str) -> str:
     9→    """Render the scan workflow guide with current attestation text."""
    10→    return dedent(
    11→        f"""
    12→        ## Workflow Guide
    13→
    14→        Work the two loops: **outer** (scan → score → at target? → rescan) and
    15→        **inner** (plan → fix next → update plan → repeat until plan clear).
    16→
    17→        1. **Follow `next`**: `desloppify next` — the single source of truth for what to work on now.
    18→           It is the execution queue from the living plan, surfaces auto-clustered batches,
    19→           and tells you exactly what to do.
    20→           Use `desloppify backlog` only to inspect broader open work outside execution.
    21→        2. **Fix & resolve**: Fix the issue, then:
    22→           `desloppify plan resolve "<id>" --note "<what you did>" --confirm`
    23→           Or with explicit attestation: `--attest "{attest_example}"`
    24→        3. **Plan strategically**: `desloppify plan` / `desloppify plan queue` — reorder,
    25→           cluster related issues, and inspect the execution queue.
    26→           Think about sequencing: what unblocks the most? What cascades? What can be batched?
    27→        4. **Run auto-fixers** (if available): `desloppify autofix <fixer> --dry-run` to preview, then apply.
    28→        5. **Rescan**: `desloppify scan --path <path>` — verify improvements, catch cascading effects.
    29→        6. **Subjective review**: `desloppify review --prepare` then follow your runner's review workflow
    30→           (see skill doc for Codex, Claude, or external paths).
    31→        7. **Triage** (after review): prefer
    32→           `desloppify plan triage --run-stages --runner codex` or
    33→           `desloppify plan triage --run-stages --runner claude`.
    34→           Manual dashboard/fallback: `desloppify plan triage`.
    35→           Complete all stages (observe → reflect → organize → enrich → sense-check → commit).
    36→        8. **Check progress**: `desloppify status` — dimension scores dashboard.
    37→
    38→        ### Decision Guide
    39→        - **Tackle**: T1/T2 (high impact), auto-fixable, security issues
    40→        - **Consider skipping**: T4 low-confidence, test/config zone issues (lower impact)
    41→        - **Wontfix**: Intentional patterns, false positives →
    42→          `desloppify plan skip --permanent "<id>" --note "<why>" --attest "{attest_example}"`
    43→        - **Batch wontfix**: Multiple intentional patterns →
    44→          `desloppify plan skip --permanent "<detector>::*::<category>" --note "<why>" --attest "{attest_example}"`
    45→
    46→        ### Understanding Scores
    47→        - **Overall**: 25% mechanical + 75% subjective. Lenient — wontfix doesn't count against you.
    48→        - **Objective**: Mechanical detectors only (no subjective review component).
    49→        - **Strict**: Same as overall, but wontfix items count as open. THIS IS YOUR NORTH STAR.
    50→        - **Verified**: Like strict, but only credits fixes the scanner has confirmed.
    51→        - Wontfix is not free — every wontfix widens the overall↔strict gap.
    52→        - Re-reviewing subjective dimensions can LOWER scores if the reviewer finds issues.
    53→
    54→        ### Understanding Dimensions
    55→        - **Mechanical** (File health, Code quality, etc.): Fix code → rescan
    56→        - **Subjective** (Naming quality, Logic clarity, etc.): Address review issues → re-review
    57→        """
    58→    ).strip()
    59→
    60→
    61→__all__ = ["build_workflow_guide"]
    62→
