    36→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    37→
    38→# Plan-aware sort tiers (item_sort_key)
    39→_TIER_PLANNED = 0   # Items with explicit plan position
    40→_TIER_EXISTING = 1  # Known items, natural ranking
    41→_TIER_NEW = 2       # Newly discovered items
    42→
    43→# Natural ranking groups (_natural_sort_key)
    44→_RANK_INITIAL_REVIEW = -3  # Unassessed subjective dimensions
    45→_RANK_WORKFLOW = -2         # Score checkpoints, create-plan
    46→_RANK_TRIAGE_STAGE = -1     # Epic triage workflow stages
    47→_RANK_CLUSTER = 0           # Auto-clustered issues
    48→_RANK_ISSUE = 1           # Individual issues + assessed subjective
    49→
    50→def _workflow_stage_index(item: WorkQueueItem) -> int:
    51→    raw_index = item.get("stage_index")
    52→    if raw_index is not None:
    53→        if isinstance(raw_index, int):
    54→            return raw_index
    55→        if isinstance(raw_index, str):
    56→            stripped = raw_index.strip()
    57→            if stripped.lstrip("-").isdigit():
    58→                return int(stripped)
    59→        logger.warning("Invalid workflow stage index %r", raw_index)
    60→    return TRIAGE_STAGE_ORDER.get(workflow_stage_name(item).lower(), 0)
    61→
    62→
    63→def enrich_with_impact(
    64→    items: list[WorkQueueItem], dimension_scores: dict[str, Any]
    65→) -> None:
    66→    """Stamp ``estimated_impact`` on each item based on dimension-level headroom.
    67→
    68→    Impact = ``overall_per_point * headroom`` where headroom = ``100 - score``.
    69→    Items in dimensions with more score headroom sort first.
    70→    """
    71→    if not dimension_scores:
    72→        for item in items:
    73→            item["estimated_impact"] = 0.0
    74→        return
    75→
    76→    breakdown = compute_health_breakdown(dimension_scores, score_key="strict")
    77→    entries = breakdown.get("entries", [])
    78→
    79→    # Build lookup: normalized dimension name -> {per_point, headroom}
    80→    dim_impact: dict[str, dict[str, float]] = {}
    81→    for entry in entries:
    82→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
    83→        if not name:
    84→            continue
    85→        per_point = float(entry.get("overall_per_point", 0.0))
    86→        score = float(entry.get("score", 0.0))
    87→        headroom = 100.0 - score
    88→        dim_impact[name.lower()] = {"per_point": per_point, "headroom": headroom}
    89→
    90→    for item in items:
    91→        impact = _compute_item_impact(item, dim_impact, get_dimension_for_detector)
    92→        item["estimated_impact"] = impact
    93→
    94→
    95→def _compute_item_impact(
    96→    item: WorkQueueItem,
    97→    dim_impact: dict[str, dict[str, float]],
    98→    get_dimension_for_detector,
    99→) -> float:
   100→    """Compute impact value for a single queue item."""
   101→    kind = item.get("kind", "issue")
   102→
   103→    # Subjective items (synthetic dimensions or subjective issues):
   104→    # look up by detail.dimension_name
   105→    if kind == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
   106→        dim_name = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
   107→        entry = dim_impact.get(dim_name.lower())
   108→        if entry:
   109→            return entry["per_point"] * entry["headroom"]
   110→        return 0.0
   111→
   112→    # Mechanical issues: use detector -> dimension mapping
   113→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   114→    if detector:
   115→        dimension = get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
   116→        if dimension:
   117→            entry = dim_impact.get(dimension.name.lower())
   118→            if entry:
   119→                return entry["per_point"] * entry["headroom"]
   120→
   121→    return 0.0
   122→
   123→
   124→def build_issue_items(
   125→    state: StateModel,
   126→    *,
   127→    scan_path: str | None,
   128→    status_filter: str,
   129→    scope: str | None,
   130→    chronic: bool,
   131→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   132→    scoped = path_scoped_issues(state.get("issues", {}), scan_path)
   133→    subjective_scores = subjective_strict_scores(state)
   134→    out: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   135→
   136→    for issue_id, issue in scoped.items():
   137→        if issue.get("suppressed"):
   138→            continue
   139→        if not status_matches(issue.get("status", "open"), status_filter):
   140→            continue
   141→        if chronic and not (
   142→            issue.get("status") == "open" and issue.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2
   143→        ):
   144→            continue
   145→
   146→        # Evidence-only: skip issues below standalone confidence threshold
   147→        detector = issue.get("detector", "")
   148→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   149→        if meta and meta.standalone_threshold:
   150→            threshold_rank = CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(meta.standalone_threshold, 9)
   151→            issue_rank = CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(issue.get("confidence", "low"), 9)
   152→            if issue_rank > threshold_rank:
   153→                continue
   154→
   155→        item = cast(WorkQueueItem, dict(issue))
   156→        item["id"] = issue_id
   157→        item["kind"] = "issue"
   158→        item["is_review"] = is_review_issue(item)
   159→        item["is_subjective"] = is_subjective_issue(item)
   160→        item["review_weight"] = (
   161→            review_issue_weight(item) if item["is_review"] else None
   162→        )
   163→        subjective_score = None
   164→        if item["is_subjective"]:
   165→            detail = detail_dict(issue)
   166→            dim_name = detail.get("dimension_name", "")
   167→            dim_key = detail.get("dimension", "") or slugify(dim_name)
   168→            subjective_score = subjective_scores.get(
   169→                dim_key, subjective_scores.get(dim_name.lower(), 100.0)
   170→            )
   171→        item["subjective_score"] = subjective_score
   172→        supported_fixers = supported_fixers_for_item(state, item)
   173→        item["primary_command"] = primary_command_for_issue(
   174→            item,
   175→            supported_fixers=supported_fixers,
   176→        )
   177→
   178→        if not scope_matches(item, scope):
   179→            continue
   180→        out.append(item)
   181→
   182→    return out
   183→
   184→
   185→def _natural_sort_key(item: WorkQueueItem) -> tuple:
   186→    """Compute natural (non-plan-aware) ranking for queue items."""
   187→    kind = item.get("kind", "issue")
   188→
   189→    # Initial-review subjective items: highest priority
   190→    if kind == "subjective_dimension" and item.get("initial_review"):
   191→        return (_RANK_INITIAL_REVIEW, 0, subjective_score_value(item), item.get("id", ""))
   192→
   193→    # Triage stage items: stage order, blocked after unblocked
   194→    if kind == "workflow_stage":
   195→        blocked_penalty = 1 if item.get("is_blocked") else 0
   196→        stage_index = _workflow_stage_index(item)
   197→        return (_RANK_TRIAGE_STAGE, blocked_penalty, stage_index, item.get("id", ""))
   198→

1→"""Predicates and small utilities for work queue items."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→from fnmatch import fnmatch
     7→from typing import Any
     8→
     9→from desloppify.base.enums import issue_status_tokens
    10→from desloppify.base.registry import DETECTORS
    11→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    12→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.types import WorkQueueItem
    13→
    14→ALL_STATUSES = set(issue_status_tokens(include_all=True))
    15→ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "refactor": 1, "manual_fix": 2, "reorganize": 3}
    16→ATTEST_EXAMPLE = (
    17→    "I have actually [DESCRIBE THE CONCRETE CHANGE YOU MADE] "
    18→    "and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing."
    19→)
    20→
    21→
    22→def detail_dict(item: WorkQueueItem | dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    23→    """Return issue detail as a dict; tolerate legacy/non-dict payloads."""
    24→    detail = item.get("detail")
    25→    return detail if isinstance(detail, dict) else {}
    26→
    27→
    28→def status_matches(item_status: str, status_filter: str) -> bool:
    29→    return status_filter == "all" or item_status == status_filter
    30→
    31→
    32→def is_subjective_issue(item: WorkQueueItem | dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
    33→    detector = item.get("detector")
    34→    return detector in {"subjective_assessment", "holistic_review", "subjective_review"}
    35→
    36→
    37→def is_review_issue(item: WorkQueueItem | dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
    38→    return item.get("detector") == "review"
    39→
    40→
    41→def is_subjective_queue_item(item: WorkQueueItem | dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
    42→    """True for subjective work items, including collapsed subjective clusters."""
    43→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
    44→        return True
    45→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
    46→        members = item.get("members", [])
    47→        return bool(members) and all(
    48→            m.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" for m in members
    49→        )
    50→    return False
    51→
    52→
    53→def review_issue_weight(item: WorkQueueItem | dict[str, Any]) -> float:
    54→    """Return review issue weight aligned with issues list ordering."""
    55→    confidence = str(item.get("confidence", "low")).lower()
    56→    weight_by_confidence = {
    57→        "high": 1.0,
    58→        "medium": 0.7,
    59→        "low": 0.3,
    60→    }
    61→    weight = weight_by_confidence.get(confidence, 0.3)
    62→    if detail_dict(item).get("holistic"):
    63→        weight *= 10.0
    64→    return float(weight)
    65→
    66→
    67→def scope_matches(item: WorkQueueItem | dict[str, Any], scope: str | None) -> bool:
    68→    """Apply show-style pattern matching against a queue item."""
    69→    if not scope:
    70→        return True
    71→
    72→    item_id = item.get("id", "")
    73→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    74→    filepath = item.get("file", "")
    75→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
    76→    dimension = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    77→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
    78→
    79→    if "*" in scope:
    80→        return any(
    81→            fnmatch(candidate, scope)
    82→            for candidate in (item_id, filepath, detector, dimension, summary)
    83→        )
    84→
    85→    if "::" in scope:
    86→        return item_id.startswith(scope)
    87→
    88→    lowered = scope.lower()
    89→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
    90→        return (
    91→            lowered in item_id.lower()
    92→            or lowered in dimension.lower()
    93→            or lowered in summary.lower()
    94→        )
    95→
    96→    # Hash suffix: 8+ hex chars matches the tail segment of a issue ID.
    97→    if len(lowered) >= 8 and re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]+", lowered):
    98→        return item_id.lower().endswith("::" + lowered)
    99→
   100→    return (
   101→        detector == scope
   102→        or filepath == scope
   103→        or filepath.startswith(scope.rstrip("/") + "/")
   104→    )
   105→
   106→
   107→def workflow_stage_name(item: WorkQueueItem | dict[str, Any]) -> str:
   108→    """Resolve the triage stage name from an item, with fallbacks."""
   109→    stage_name = str(item.get("stage_name", "")).strip()
   110→    if stage_name:
   111→        return stage_name
   112→
   113→    stage_name = str(detail_dict(item).get("stage", "")).strip()
   114→    if stage_name:
   115→        return stage_name
   116→
   117→    item_id = str(item.get("id", "")).strip()
   118→    if item_id.startswith("triage::"):
   119→        return item_id.split("::", 1)[1]
   120→    return ""
   121→
   122→
   123→def slugify(text: str) -> str:
   124→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", text.lower()).strip("_")
   125→
   126→
   127→def supported_fixers_for_item(
   128→    state: StateModel, item: WorkQueueItem
   129→) -> set[str] | None:
   130→    """Return supported fixers for an item's language when known."""
   131→    lang = str(item.get("lang", "") or "").strip()
   132→    if not lang:
   133→        return None
   134→
   135→    caps_obj = state.get("lang_capabilities")
   136→    if not isinstance(caps_obj, dict):
   137→        return None
   138→    lang_caps_obj = caps_obj.get(lang)
   139→    if not isinstance(lang_caps_obj, dict):
   140→        return None
   141→
   142→    fixers = lang_caps_obj.get("fixers")
   143→    if not isinstance(fixers, list):
   144→        return None
   145→    return {fixer for fixer in fixers if isinstance(fixer, str)}
   146→
   147→
   148→def primary_command_for_issue(
   149→    item: WorkQueueItem, *, supported_fixers: set[str] | None = None
   150→) -> str:
   151→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   152→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   153→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers:
   154→        available_fixers = [
   155→            fixer
   156→            for fixer in meta.fixers
   157→            if supported_fixers is not None and fixer in supported_fixers
   158→        ]
   159→        if available_fixers:
   160→            return f"desloppify autofix {available_fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   161→    if detector == "subjective_review":
   162→        dim_key = (item.get("detail") or {}).get("dimension", "")
   163→        if dim_key:
   164→            return f"desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {dim_key}"
   165→        return "desloppify review --prepare"
   166→    return f'desloppify plan resolve "{item.get("id", "")}" --note "<what you did>" --confirm'
   167→
   168→
   169→__all__ = [

1→"""Bridge between state persistence and scoring computation.
     2→
     3→This module owns the score-recomputation step that runs before state is written.
     4→The dependency direction is: _scoring/state_integration -> _state (reads state),
     5→_scoring/state_integration -> _scoring (calls scoring functions).
     6→State persistence calls this module, never the reverse.
     7→"""
     8→
     9→from __future__ import annotations
    10→
    11→from desloppify.base.enums import issue_status_tokens
    12→from desloppify.engine._scoring.detection import merge_potentials
    13→from desloppify.engine._scoring.results.core import (
    14→    compute_health_score,
    15→    compute_score_bundle,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.engine._scoring.state_integration_subjective import (
    18→    _apply_subjective_integrity_policy,
    19→    _normalize_integrity_target,
    20→    _subjective_integrity_baseline,
    21→)
    22→from desloppify.engine._scoring.state_coverage import (
    23→    apply_scan_coverage_to_dimension_scores as _apply_scan_coverage_to_dimension_scores,
    24→)
    25→from desloppify.engine._state.scope import path_scoped_issues
    26→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, ensure_state_defaults
    27→
    28→_EMPTY_COUNTERS = tuple(sorted(issue_status_tokens()))
    29→
    30→
    31→def _resolve_lang_from_state(state: StateModel) -> str | None:
    32→    """Best-effort language detection from state (scan_history > lang_capabilities)."""
    33→    history = state.get("scan_history")
    34→    if isinstance(history, list):
    35→        for entry in reversed(history):
    36→            if isinstance(entry, dict):
    37→                lang = entry.get("lang")
    38→                if isinstance(lang, str) and lang.strip():
    39→                    return lang.strip().lower()
    40→    capabilities = state.get("lang_capabilities")
    41→    if isinstance(capabilities, dict) and len(capabilities) == 1:
    42→        only_lang = next(iter(capabilities.keys()))
    43→        if isinstance(only_lang, str) and only_lang.strip():
    44→            return only_lang.strip().lower()
    45→    return None
    46→
    47→
    48→def _count_issues(issues: dict) -> tuple[dict[str, int], dict[int, dict[str, int]]]:
    49→    """Tally per-status counters and per-tier breakdowns."""
    50→    counters = dict.fromkeys(_EMPTY_COUNTERS, 0)
    51→    tier_stats: dict[int, dict[str, int]] = {}
    52→
    53→    for issue in issues.values():
    54→        if issue.get("suppressed"):
    55→            continue
    56→        status = issue["status"]
    57→        tier = issue.get("tier", 3)
    58→        counters[status] = counters.get(status, 0) + 1
    59→        tier_counter = tier_stats.setdefault(tier, dict.fromkeys(_EMPTY_COUNTERS, 0))
    60→        tier_counter[status] = tier_counter.get(status, 0) + 1
    61→
    62→    return counters, tier_stats
    63→
    64→
    65→def _aggregate_scores(dim_scores: dict) -> dict[str, float]:
    66→    """Derive the four aggregate scores from dimension-level data."""
    67→    mechanical = {
    68→        n: d
    69→        for n, d in dim_scores.items()
    70→        if "subjective_assessment" not in d.get("detectors", {})
    71→    }
    72→    return {
    73→        "overall_score": compute_health_score(dim_scores),
    74→        "strict_score": compute_health_score(dim_scores, score_key="strict"),
    75→        "objective_score": compute_health_score(mechanical),
    76→        "verified_strict_score": compute_health_score(
    77→            mechanical,
    78→            [REDACTED],
    79→        ),
    80→    }
    81→
    82→
    83→def _set_perfect_scores(state: StateModel) -> None:
    84→    """Set all score fields to 100 when there are no active checks."""
    85→    state["dimension_scores"] = {}
    86→    state["overall_score"] = 100.0
    87→    state["objective_score"] = 100.0
    88→    state["strict_score"] = 100.0
    89→    state["verified_strict_score"] = 100.0
    90→
    91→
    92→def _resolve_allowed_subjective_dimensions(
    93→    state: StateModel,
    94→) -> set[str] | None:
    95→    """Resolve allowed subjective dimensions from the language config."""
    96→    lang_name = _resolve_lang_from_state(state)
    97→    if not lang_name:
    98→        return None
    99→    try:
   100→        from desloppify.intelligence.review.dimensions.data import (
   101→            load_dimensions_for_lang,
   102→        )
   103→
   104→        dims, _, _ = load_dimensions_for_lang(lang_name)
   105→        if dims:
   106→            return set(dims)
   107→    except (ImportError, AttributeError) as exc:
   108→        _ = exc
   109→    return None
   110→
   111→
   112→def _materialize_dimension_scores(
   113→    state: StateModel,
   114→    bundle: object,
   115→) -> None:
   116→    """Write dimension scores from a score bundle into state, carrying forward old dims."""
   117→    lenient_scores = bundle.dimension_scores
   118→    strict_scores = bundle.strict_dimension_scores
   119→    verified_strict_scores = bundle.verified_strict_dimension_scores
   120→
   121→    prev_dim_scores = dict(state.get("dimension_scores", {}))
   122→
   123→    state["dimension_scores"] = {
   124→        name: dict(
   125→            score=lenient_scores[name]["score"],
   126→            strict=strict_scores[name]["score"],
   127→            verified_strict_score=verified_strict_scores[name]["score"],
   128→            checks=lenient_scores[name]["checks"],
   129→            failing=lenient_scores[name]["failing"],
   130→            tier=lenient_scores[name]["tier"],
   131→            detectors=lenient_scores[name].get("detectors", {}),
   132→        )
   133→        for name in lenient_scores
   134→    }
   135→
   136→    for dim_name, prev_data in prev_dim_scores.items():
   137→        if dim_name in state["dimension_scores"]:
   138→            continue
   139→        if not isinstance(prev_data, dict):
   140→            continue
   141→        if "subjective_assessment" in prev_data.get("detectors", {}):
   142→            continue
   143→        carried = {**prev_data, "carried_forward": True}
   144→        carried.setdefault("score", 0.0)
   145→        carried.setdefault("strict", carried.get("score", 0.0))
   146→        carried.setdefault(
   147→            "verified_strict_score",
   148→            carried.get("strict", carried.get("score", 0.0)),
   149→        )
   150→        state["dimension_scores"][dim_name] = carried
   151→
   152→    _apply_scan_coverage_to_dimension_scores(
   153→        state,
   154→        dimension_scores=state["dimension_scores"],
   155→    )
   156→    state.update(_aggregate_scores(state["dimension_scores"]))
   157→
   158→
   159→def _update_objective_health(
   160→    state: StateModel,
   161→    issues: dict,
   162→    *,
   163→    subjective_integrity_target: float | None = None,
   164→) -> None:
   165→    """Compute canonical score tuple from current detector issues/potentials."""
   166→    pots = state.get("potentials", {})
   167→    if not pots:
   168→        return
   169→
   170→    merged = merge_potentials(pots)
   171→    if not merged:
   172→        return
   173→
   174→    subjective_assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments") or None
   175→    integrity_target = _normalize_integrity_target(subjective_integrity_target)
   176→    integrity_meta = _subjective_integrity_baseline(integrity_target)
   177→    if subjective_assessments and integrity_target is not None:
   178→        subjective_assessments, integrity_meta = _apply_subjective_integrity_policy(
   179→            subjective_assessments,
   180→            target=integrity_target,
   181→        )
   182→    state["subjective_integrity"] = integrity_meta
   183→
   184→    has_active_checks = any((count or 0) > 0 for count in merged.values())
   185→    if not has_active_checks and not subjective_assessments:
   186→        _set_perfect_scores(state)
   187→        return
   188→
   189→    allowed_subjective = _resolve_allowed_subjective_dimensions(state)
   190→
   191→    bundle = compute_score_bundle(
   192→        issues,
   193→        merged,
   194→        subjective_assessments=subjective_assessments,
   195→        allowed_subjective_dimensions=allowed_subjective,
   196→    )
   197→    _materialize_dimension_scores(state, bundle)
   198→
   199→
   200→def recompute_stats(
   201→    state: StateModel,
   202→    scan_path: str | None = None,
   203→    *,
   204→    subjective_integrity_target: float | None = None,
   205→) -> None:
   206→    """Recompute stats and canonical health scores from issues."""
   207→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   208→    issues = path_scoped_issues(state["issues"], scan_path)
   209→    counters, tier_stats = _count_issues(issues)
   210→    state["stats"] = {
   211→        "total": sum(counters.values()),
   212→        **counters,
   213→        "by_tier": {
   214→            str(tier): tier_counts for tier, tier_counts in sorted(tier_stats.items())
   215→        },
   216→    }
   217→    _update_objective_health(
   218→        state,
   219→        issues,
   220→        subjective_integrity_target=subjective_integrity_target,
   221→    )
   222→
   223→
   224→__all__ = [
   225→    "_count_issues",
   226→    "_update_objective_health",
   227→    "recompute_stats",
   228→]
   229→

1→"""Bridge between state persistence and scoring computation.
     2→
     3→This module owns the score-recomputation step that runs before state is written.
     4→The dependency direction is: _scoring/state_integration -> _state (reads state),
     5→_scoring/state_integration -> _scoring (calls scoring functions).
     6→State persistence calls this module, never the reverse.
     7→"""
     8→
     9→from __future__ import annotations
    10→
    11→from desloppify.base.enums import issue_status_tokens
    12→from desloppify.engine._scoring.detection import merge_potentials
    13→from desloppify.engine._scoring.results.core import (
    14→    compute_health_score,
    15→    compute_score_bundle,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.engine._scoring.state_integration_subjective import (
    18→    _apply_subjective_integrity_policy,
    19→    _normalize_integrity_target,
    20→    _subjective_integrity_baseline,
    21→)
    22→from desloppify.engine._scoring.state_coverage import (
    23→    apply_scan_coverage_to_dimension_scores as _apply_scan_coverage_to_dimension_scores,
    24→)
    25→from desloppify.engine._state.scope import path_scoped_issues
    26→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, ensure_state_defaults
    27→
    28→_EMPTY_COUNTERS = tuple(sorted(issue_status_tokens()))
    29→
    30→
    31→def _resolve_lang_from_state(state: StateModel) -> str | None:
    32→    """Best-effort language detection from state (scan_history > lang_capabilities)."""
    33→    history = state.get("scan_history")
    34→    if isinstance(history, list):
    35→        for entry in reversed(history):
    36→            if isinstance(entry, dict):
    37→                lang = entry.get("lang")
    38→                if isinstance(lang, str) and lang.strip():
    39→                    return lang.strip().lower()
    40→    capabilities = state.get("lang_capabilities")
    41→    if isinstance(capabilities, dict) and len(capabilities) == 1:
    42→        only_lang = next(iter(capabilities.keys()))
    43→        if isinstance(only_lang, str) and only_lang.strip():
    44→            return only_lang.strip().lower()
    45→    return None
    46→
    47→
    48→def _count_issues(issues: dict) -> tuple[dict[str, int], dict[int, dict[str, int]]]:
    49→    """Tally per-status counters and per-tier breakdowns."""
    50→    counters = dict.fromkeys(_EMPTY_COUNTERS, 0)
    51→    tier_stats: dict[int, dict[str, int]] = {}
    52→
    53→    for issue in issues.values():
    54→        if issue.get("suppressed"):
    55→            continue
    56→        status = issue["status"]
    57→        tier = issue.get("tier", 3)
    58→        counters[status] = counters.get(status, 0) + 1
    59→        tier_counter = tier_stats.setdefault(tier, dict.fromkeys(_EMPTY_COUNTERS, 0))
    60→        tier_counter[status] = tier_counter.get(status, 0) + 1
    61→
    62→    return counters, tier_stats
    63→
    64→
    65→def _aggregate_scores(dim_scores: dict) -> dict[str, float]:
    66→    """Derive the four aggregate scores from dimension-level data."""
    67→    mechanical = {
    68→        n: d
    69→        for n, d in dim_scores.items()
    70→        if "subjective_assessment" not in d.get("detectors", {})
    71→    }
    72→    return {
    73→        "overall_score": compute_health_score(dim_scores),
    74→        "strict_score": compute_health_score(dim_scores, score_key="strict"),
    75→        "objective_score": compute_health_score(mechanical),
    76→        "verified_strict_score": compute_health_score(
    77→            mechanical,
    78→            [REDACTED],
    79→        ),
    80→    }
    81→
    82→
    83→def _set_perfect_scores(state: StateModel) -> None:
    84→    """Set all score fields to 100 when there are no active checks."""
    85→    state["dimension_scores"] = {}
    86→    state["overall_score"] = 100.0
    87→    state["objective_score"] = 100.0
    88→    state["strict_score"] = 100.0
    89→    state["verified_strict_score"] = 100.0
    90→
    91→
    92→def _resolve_allowed_subjective_dimensions(
    93→    state: StateModel,
    94→) -> set[str] | None:
    95→    """Resolve allowed subjective dimensions from the language config."""
    96→    lang_name = _resolve_lang_from_state(state)
    97→    if not lang_name:
    98→        return None
    99→    try:
   100→        from desloppify.intelligence.review.dimensions.data import (
   101→            load_dimensions_for_lang,
   102→        )
   103→
   104→        dims, _, _ = load_dimensions_for_lang(lang_name)
   105→        if dims:
   106→            return set(dims)
   107→    except (ImportError, AttributeError) as exc:
   108→        _ = exc
   109→    return None
   110→
   111→
   112→def _materialize_dimension_scores(
   113→    state: StateModel,
   114→    bundle: object,
   115→) -> None:
   116→    """Write dimension scores from a score bundle into state, carrying forward old dims."""
   117→    lenient_scores = bundle.dimension_scores
   118→    strict_scores = bundle.strict_dimension_scores
   119→    verified_strict_scores = bundle.verified_strict_dimension_scores
   120→
   121→    prev_dim_scores = dict(state.get("dimension_scores", {}))
   122→
   123→    state["dimension_scores"] = {
   124→        name: dict(
   125→            score=lenient_scores[name]["score"],
   126→            strict=strict_scores[name]["score"],
   127→            verified_strict_score=verified_strict_scores[name]["score"],
   128→            checks=lenient_scores[name]["checks"],
   129→            failing=lenient_scores[name]["failing"],
   130→            tier=lenient_scores[name]["tier"],
   131→            detectors=lenient_scores[name].get("detectors", {}),
   132→        )
   133→        for name in lenient_scores
   134→    }
   135→
   136→    for dim_name, prev_data in prev_dim_scores.items():
   137→        if dim_name in state["dimension_scores"]:
   138→            continue
   139→        if not isinstance(prev_data, dict):
   140→            continue
   141→        if "subjective_assessment" in prev_data.get("detectors", {}):
   142→            continue
   143→        carried = {**prev_data, "carried_forward": True}
   144→        carried.setdefault("score", 0.0)
   145→        carried.setdefault("strict", carried.get("score", 0.0))
   146→        carried.setdefault(
   147→            "verified_strict_score",
   148→            carried.get("strict", carried.get("score", 0.0)),
   149→        )
   150→        state["dimension_scores"][dim_name] = carried
   151→
   152→    _apply_scan_coverage_to_dimension_scores(
   153→        state,
   154→        dimension_scores=state["dimension_scores"],
   155→    )
   156→    state.update(_aggregate_scores(state["dimension_scores"]))
   157→
   158→
   159→def _update_objective_health(
   160→    state: StateModel,
   161→    issues: dict,
   162→    *,
   163→    subjective_integrity_target: float | None = None,
   164→) -> None:
   165→    """Compute canonical score tuple from current detector issues/potentials."""
   166→    pots = state.get("potentials", {})
   167→    if not pots:
   168→        return
   169→
   170→    merged = merge_potentials(pots)
   171→    if not merged:
   172→        return
   173→
   174→    subjective_assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments") or None
   175→    integrity_target = _normalize_integrity_target(subjective_integrity_target)
   176→    integrity_meta = _subjective_integrity_baseline(integrity_target)
   177→    if subjective_assessments and integrity_target is not None:
   178→        subjective_assessments, integrity_meta = _apply_subjective_integrity_policy(
   179→            subjective_assessments,
   180→            target=integrity_target,
   181→        )
   182→    state["subjective_integrity"] = integrity_meta
   183→
   184→    has_active_checks = any((count or 0) > 0 for count in merged.values())
   185→    if not has_active_checks and not subjective_assessments:
   186→        _set_perfect_scores(state)
   187→        return
   188→
   189→    allowed_subjective = _resolve_allowed_subjective_dimensions(state)
   190→
   191→    bundle = compute_score_bundle(
   192→        issues,
   193→        merged,
   194→        subjective_assessments=subjective_assessments,
   195→        allowed_subjective_dimensions=allowed_subjective,
   196→    )
   197→    _materialize_dimension_scores(state, bundle)
   198→
   199→
   200→def recompute_stats(
   201→    state: StateModel,
   202→    scan_path: str | None = None,
   203→    *,
   204→    subjective_integrity_target: float | None = None,
   205→) -> None:
   206→    """Recompute stats and canonical health scores from issues."""
   207→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   208→    issues = path_scoped_issues(state["issues"], scan_path)
   209→    counters, tier_stats = _count_issues(issues)
   210→    state["stats"] = {
   211→        "total": sum(counters.values()),
   212→        **counters,
   213→        "by_tier": {
   214→            str(tier): tier_counts for tier, tier_counts in sorted(tier_stats.items())
   215→        },
   216→    }
   217→    _update_objective_health(
   218→        state,
   219→        issues,
   220→        subjective_integrity_target=subjective_integrity_target,
   221→    )
   222→
   223→
   224→__all__ = [
   225→    "_count_issues",
   226→    "_update_objective_health",

1→"""Canonical enums for issue attributes.
     2→
     3→StrEnum values compare equal to their string values (Confidence.HIGH == "high"),
     4→so existing code using raw strings continues to work during gradual migration.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→import enum
    10→
    11→
    12→class Confidence(enum.StrEnum):
    13→    HIGH = "high"
    14→    MEDIUM = "medium"
    15→    LOW = "low"
    16→
    17→
    18→class Status(enum.StrEnum):
    19→    OPEN = "open"
    20→    FIXED = "fixed"
    21→    WONTFIX = "wontfix"
    22→    FALSE_POSITIVE = "false_positive"
    23→    AUTO_RESOLVED = "auto_resolved"
    24→    DEFERRED = "deferred"
    25→    TRIAGED_OUT = "triaged_out"
    26→    RESOLVED = "resolved"  # Legacy on-disk value; migrated to FIXED on load.
    27→
    28→
    29→_CANONICAL_ISSUE_STATUSES = frozenset(
    30→    {
    31→        Status.OPEN.value,
    32→        Status.FIXED.value,
    33→        Status.WONTFIX.value,
    34→        Status.FALSE_POSITIVE.value,
    35→        Status.AUTO_RESOLVED.value,
    36→        Status.DEFERRED.value,
    37→        Status.TRIAGED_OUT.value,
    38→    }
    39→)
    40→_RESOLVED_STATUSES = frozenset(
    41→    {
    42→        Status.FIXED.value,
    43→        Status.WONTFIX.value,
    44→        Status.FALSE_POSITIVE.value,
    45→        Status.AUTO_RESOLVED.value,
    46→    }
    47→)
    48→_LEGACY_STATUS_ALIASES = {
    49→    Status.RESOLVED.value: Status.FIXED.value,
    50→}
    51→
    52→
    53→class Tier(enum.IntEnum):
    54→    AUTO_FIX = 1
    55→    QUICK_FIX = 2
    56→    JUDGMENT = 3
    57→    MAJOR_REFACTOR = 4
    58→
    59→
    60→def canonical_issue_status(
    61→    value: object, *, default: Status = Status.OPEN
    62→) -> Status:
    63→    """Normalize legacy/unknown issue status values to a canonical enum."""
    64→    token = str(value).strip().lower()
    65→    [REDACTED](token, token)
    66→    if token in _CANONICAL_ISSUE_STATUSES:
    67→        return Status(token)
    68→    return default
    69→
    70→
    71→def issue_status_tokens(*, include_all: bool = False) -> frozenset[str]:
    72→    """Return canonical issue-status tokens, optionally including `all`."""
    73→    if include_all:
    74→        return frozenset({*_CANONICAL_ISSUE_STATUSES, "all"})
    75→    return _CANONICAL_ISSUE_STATUSES
    76→
    77→
    78→def resolved_statuses() -> frozenset[str]:
    79→    """Return the set of statuses that mean an issue is no longer open."""
    80→    return _RESOLVED_STATUSES
    81→

    83→__all__ = [

    85→    "Status",
    86→    "Tier",

1→"""Scope and queue helpers for show command selection."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     9→from desloppify.base import registry as registry_mod
    10→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    11→from desloppify.engine._scoring.policy.core import DIMENSIONS
    12→from desloppify.engine._scoring.subjective.core import DISPLAY_NAMES
    13→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    14→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.core import (
    15→    QueueBuildOptions,
    16→    build_work_queue,
    17→)
    18→
    19→
    20→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    21→class ResolvedEntity:
    22→    """Classifies what the user's show pattern refers to."""
    23→
    24→    kind: str  # "dimension", "special_view", "file_or_pattern"
    25→    pattern: str  # Original user pattern
    26→    display_name: str  # Human-readable label
    27→    detectors: tuple[str, ...] = ()  # Detector names (for dimension kind)
    28→    is_subjective: bool = False  # Whether this is a subjective dimension
    29→
    30→
    31→def _resolve_special_view(pattern: str, lowered: str) -> ResolvedEntity | None:
    32→    if lowered not in ("concerns", "subjective", "subjective_review"):
    33→        return None
    34→    return ResolvedEntity(
    35→        kind="special_view",
    36→        pattern=pattern,
    37→        display_name=pattern,
    38→    )
    39→
    40→
    41→def _resolve_mechanical_dimension(pattern: str, lowered: str) -> ResolvedEntity | None:
    42→    lookup = _build_dimension_lookup()
    43→    detectors = lookup.get(lowered) or lookup.get(pattern.lower())
    44→    if not detectors:
    45→        return None
    46→    dim_display = pattern
    47→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
    48→        dim_lowered = dim.name.lower().replace(" ", "_")
    49→        if dim.name.lower() == lowered or dim_lowered == lowered:
    50→            dim_display = dim.name
    51→            break
    52→    return ResolvedEntity(
    53→        kind="dimension",
    54→        pattern=pattern,
    55→        display_name=dim_display,
    56→        detectors=tuple(detectors),
    57→        is_subjective=False,
    58→    )
    59→
    60→
    61→def _resolve_subjective_dimension(
    62→    pattern: str,
    63→    lowered: str,
    64→    state: StateModel,
    65→) -> ResolvedEntity | None:
    66→    display_name = DISPLAY_NAMES.get(lowered)
    67→    if not display_name:
    68→        for key in state.get("dimension_scores") or {}:
    69→            if key.lower().replace(" ", "_") == lowered:
    70→                display_name = key
    71→                break
    72→    if not display_name:
    73→        return None
    74→    dim_data, display_name = _lookup_dimension_score(state, display_name)
    75→    is_subj = "subjective_assessment" in (
    76→        dim_data.get("detectors", {}) if isinstance(dim_data, dict) else {}
    77→    )
    78→    return ResolvedEntity(
    79→        kind="dimension",
    80→        pattern=pattern,
    81→        display_name=display_name,
    82→        detectors=(),
    83→        is_subjective=is_subj,
    84→    )
    85→
    86→
    87→def resolve_entity(pattern: str, state: StateModel) -> ResolvedEntity:
    88→    """Classify a user pattern as a dimension, special view, or passthrough.
    89→
    90→    Resolution priority:
    91→    1. Special views: "concerns", "subjective", "subjective_review"
    92→    2. Mechanical dimension name (from DIMENSIONS)
    93→    3. Subjective dimension name (from DISPLAY_NAMES / dimension_scores)
    94→    4. Everything else: file_or_pattern passthrough
    95→    """
    96→    lowered = pattern.strip().lower().replace(" ", "_")
    97→
    98→    # 1. Special views
    99→    special_view = _resolve_special_view(pattern, lowered)
   100→    if special_view is not None:
   101→        return special_view
   102→
   103→    # 2. Mechanical dimension (via DIMENSIONS list)
   104→    mechanical = _resolve_mechanical_dimension(pattern, lowered)
   105→    if mechanical is not None:
   106→        return mechanical
   107→
   108→    # 3. Subjective dimension (via DISPLAY_NAMES or dimension_scores)
   109→    subjective = _resolve_subjective_dimension(pattern, lowered, state)
   110→    if subjective is not None:
   111→        return subjective
   112→
   113→    # 4. Everything else
   114→    return ResolvedEntity(
   115→        kind="file_or_pattern",
   116→        pattern=pattern,
   117→        display_name=pattern,
   118→    )
   119→
   120→
   121→def _build_dimension_lookup() -> dict[str, list[str]]:
   122→    """Build a map from dimension name/key (lowered) to detector names."""
   123→    lookup: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
   124→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   125→        detectors = list(dim.detectors) if hasattr(dim, "detectors") else []
   126→        lookup[dim.name.lower()] = detectors
   127→        # Also index by underscore key: "file_health" -> detectors
   128→        key = dim.name.lower().replace(" ", "_")
   129→        if key not in lookup:
   130→            lookup[key] = detectors
   131→    # Also add DISPLAY_NAMES reverse lookup (e.g. "abstraction_fit" -> "Abstraction Fit")
   132→    for key, display in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
   133→        normalized_key = key.lower().replace(" ", "_")
   134→        normalized_display = display.lower()
   135→        # Find which dimension this belongs to via get_dimension_for_detector or direct name match
   136→        for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   137→            dim_lower = dim.name.lower()
   138→            if normalized_display == dim_lower or normalized_key == dim_lower.replace(" ", "_"):
   139→                detectors = list(dim.detectors) if hasattr(dim, "detectors") else []
   140→                if normalized_key not in lookup:
   141→                    lookup[normalized_key] = detectors
   142→                if normalized_display not in lookup:
   143→                    lookup[normalized_display] = detectors
   144→    return lookup
   145→
   146→
   147→def _lookup_dimension_score(
   148→    state: StateModel, display_name: str,
   149→) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], str]:
   150→    """Find dimension_scores entry with case-insensitive fallback.
   151→
   152→    Returns (dim_data_dict, resolved_display_name).
   153→    """
   154→    lowered = display_name.lower().replace(" ", "_")
   155→    dim_data = (state.get("dimension_scores") or {}).get(display_name, {})
   156→    if not dim_data:
   157→        for ds_key, ds_val in (state.get("dimension_scores") or {}).items():
   158→            if ds_key.lower().replace(" ", "_") == lowered:
   159→                dim_data = ds_val
   160→                display_name = ds_key
   161→                break
   162→    return dim_data, display_name
   163→
   164→
   165→def _detector_names_hint() -> str:
   166→    """Return a compact list of detector names for the help message."""
   167→    names = tuple(getattr(registry_mod, "_DISPLAY_ORDER", ()))
   168→    if names:
   169→        return ", ".join(names[:10]) + (", ..." if len(names) > 10 else "")
   170→    return "smells, structural, security, review, ..."
   171→
   172→
   173→def resolve_show_scope(args: object) -> tuple[bool, str | None, str, str | None]:
   174→    """Resolve scope/pattern/status for a show invocation."""
   175→    chronic = getattr(args, "chronic", False)
   176→    pattern = args.pattern
   177→    status_filter = "open" if chronic else getattr(args, "status", "open")
   178→    if chronic:
   179→        scope = pattern
   180→        pattern = pattern or "<chronic>"
   181→        return True, pattern, status_filter, scope
   182→    if not pattern:
   183→        print(
   184→            colorize(
   185→                "Pattern required (or use --chronic). Try: desloppify show --help",
   186→                "yellow",
   187→            )
   188→        )
   189→        return False, None, status_filter, ""
   190→    return True, pattern, status_filter, pattern
   191→
   192→
   193→def load_matches(
   194→    state: StateModel,
   195→    *,
   196→    scope: str | None,
   197→    status_filter: str,
   198→    chronic: bool,
   199→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   200→    """Load matching issues from the ranked queue."""
   201→    queue = build_work_queue(
   202→        state,
   203→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   204→            count=None,
   205→            scope=scope,
   206→            status=status_filter,
   207→            include_subjective=False,
   208→            chronic=chronic,
   209→        ),
   210→    )
   211→    return [item for item in queue.get("items", []) if item.get("kind") == "issue"]
   212→
   213→
   214→def resolve_noise(
   215→    config: dict[str, Any],
   216→    matches: list[dict[str, Any]],
   217→    *,
   218→    no_budget: bool = False,
   219→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], dict[str, int], int, int, str | None]:
   220→    """Apply detector/global noise budget to show matches.
   221→
   222→    When *no_budget* is True, all matches are surfaced (nothing hidden).
   223→    """
   224→    if no_budget:
   225→        return (
   226→            matches,
   227→            {},
   228→            0,
   229→            0,
   230→            None,
   231→        )
   232→    noise_budget, global_noise_budget, budget_warning = (
   233→        state_mod.resolve_issue_noise_settings(config)
   234→    )
   235→    surfaced_matches, hidden_by_detector = state_mod.apply_issue_noise_budget(
   236→        matches,
   237→        budget=noise_budget,
   238→        global_budget=global_noise_budget,
   239→    )
   240→    return (
   241→        surfaced_matches,
   242→        hidden_by_detector,
   243→        noise_budget,
   244→        global_noise_budget,
   245→        budget_warning,
   246→    )
   247→
   248→
   249→__all__ = [
   250→    "ResolvedEntity",
   251→    "_detector_names_hint",
   252→    "_lookup_dimension_score",
   253→    "load_matches",
   254→    "resolve_entity",
   255→    "resolve_noise",
   256→    "resolve_show_scope",
   257→]
   258→

1→"""Section render helpers for markdown planning output."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections import defaultdict
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine.plan_ops import (
     8→    SKIP_KIND_SECTION_LABELS,
     9→    USER_SKIP_KINDS,
    10→    get_issue_description,
    11→    get_issue_note,
    12→)
    13→
    14→
    15→def summary_lines(stats: dict) -> list[str]:
    16→    open_count = stats.get("open", 0)
    17→    deferred_count = stats.get("deferred", 0) + stats.get("triaged_out", 0)
    18→    total_issues = sum(
    19→        stats.get(key, 0)
    20→        for key in ("open", "fixed", "wontfix", "auto_resolved", "deferred", "triaged_out")
    21→    )
    22→    addressed = total_issues - open_count - deferred_count
    23→    pct = round(addressed / total_issues * 100) if total_issues else 100
    24→    deferred_note = f" · {deferred_count} deferred" if deferred_count else ""
    25→    return [
    26→        f"- **{open_count} open** / {total_issues} total ({pct}% addressed{deferred_note})",
    27→        "",
    28→    ]
    29→
    30→
    31→def addressed_section(issues: dict) -> list[str]:
    32→    addressed = [issue for issue in issues.values() if issue["status"] != "open"]
    33→    if not addressed:
    34→        return []
    35→
    36→    lines: list[str] = ["---", "## Addressed", ""]
    37→    by_status: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
    38→    for issue in addressed:
    39→        by_status[issue["status"]] += 1
    40→    for status, count in sorted(by_status.items()):
    41→        lines.append(f"- **{status}**: {count}")
    42→
    43→    wontfix = [
    44→        issue
    45→        for issue in addressed
    46→        if issue["status"] == "wontfix" and issue.get("note")
    47→    ]
    48→    if wontfix:
    49→        lines.extend(["", "### Wontfix (with explanations)", ""])
    50→        for issue in wontfix[:30]:
    51→            lines.append(f"- `{issue['id']}` — {issue['note']}")
    52→    lines.append("")
    53→    return lines
    54→
    55→
    56→def render_plan_item(item: dict, plan: dict) -> list[str]:
    57→    """Render a single plan item as markdown lines."""
    58→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "medium")
    59→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
    60→    item_id = item.get("id", "")
    61→
    62→    lines = [f"- [ ] [{confidence}] {summary}"]
    63→    description = get_issue_description(plan, item_id)
    64→    if description:
    65→        lines.append(f"      → {description}")
    66→    lines.append(f"      `{item_id}`")
    67→    note = get_issue_note(plan, item_id)
    68→    if note:
    69→        lines.append(f"      Note: {note}")
    70→    return lines
    71→
    72→
    73→def plan_user_ordered_section(
    74→    items: list[dict],
    75→    plan: dict,
    76→) -> list[str]:
    77→    """Render the user-ordered queue section, grouped by cluster."""
    78→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    79→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    80→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
    81→
    82→    ordered_ids = set(queue_order) - skipped_ids
    83→    if not ordered_ids:
    84→        return []
    85→
    86→    by_id = {item.get("id"): item for item in items}
    87→    lines: list[str] = [
    88→        "---",
    89→        f"## User-Ordered Queue ({len(ordered_ids)} items)",
    90→        "",
    91→    ]
    92→
    93→    # Build override-based cluster lookup: override cluster wins over issue_ids membership
    94→    overrides: dict = plan.get("overrides", {})
    95→    effective_cluster: dict[str, str] = {}
    96→    for issue_id, ov in overrides.items():
    97→        ov_cluster = ov.get("cluster")
    98→        if ov_cluster and ov_cluster in clusters:
    99→            effective_cluster[issue_id] = ov_cluster
   100→
   101→    # queue_order is the single source of truth for item ordering
   102→    queue_pos = {qid: i for i, qid in enumerate(queue_order)}
   103→
   104→    # Pre-compute members per cluster, sorted by queue_order position
   105→    cluster_members: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
   106→    cluster_member_sets: dict[str, set[str]] = {}
   107→    for cluster_name, cluster in clusters.items():
   108→        members = [
   109→            issue_id
   110→            for issue_id in cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   111→            if issue_id in ordered_ids
   112→            and issue_id in by_id
   113→            and effective_cluster.get(issue_id, cluster_name) == cluster_name
   114→        ]
   115→        members.sort(key=lambda x: queue_pos.get(x, float("inf")))
   116→        if members:
   117→            cluster_members[cluster_name] = members
   118→            cluster_member_sets[cluster_name] = set(members)
   119→
   120→    # Sort clusters by the queue_order position of their first member
   121→    sorted_cluster_names = sorted(
   122→        cluster_members,
   123→        key=lambda cn: queue_pos.get(cluster_members[cn][0], float("inf")),
   124→    )
   125→
   126→    emitted: set[str] = set()
   127→    for cluster_name in sorted_cluster_names:
   128→        member_ids = [mid for mid in cluster_members[cluster_name] if mid not in emitted]
   129→        if not member_ids:
   130→            continue
   131→        desc = clusters[cluster_name].get("description") or ""
   132→        lines.append(f"### Cluster: {cluster_name}")
   133→        if desc:
   134→            lines.append(f"> {desc}")
   135→        lines.append("")
   136→        for issue_id in member_ids:
   137→            item = by_id.get(issue_id)
   138→            if item:
   139→                lines.extend(render_plan_item(item, plan))
   140→                emitted.add(issue_id)
   141→        lines.append("")
   142→
   143→    unclustered = [
   144→        issue_id
   145→        for issue_id in queue_order
   146→        if issue_id in ordered_ids and issue_id not in emitted and issue_id in by_id
   147→    ]
   148→    if unclustered:
   149→        if any(cluster.get("issue_ids") for cluster in clusters.values()):
   150→            lines.append("### (unclustered ordered items)")
   151→            lines.append("")
   152→        for issue_id in unclustered:
   153→            item = by_id.get(issue_id)
   154→            if item:
   155→                lines.extend(render_plan_item(item, plan))
   156→        lines.append("")
   157→    return lines
   158→
   159→
   160→def plan_skipped_section(items: list[dict], plan: dict) -> list[str]:
   161→    """Render the skipped items section, grouped by kind."""
   162→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   163→    if not skipped:
   164→        return []
   165→
   166→    by_id = {item.get("id"): item for item in items}
   167→
   168→    by_kind: dict[str, list[str]] = {kind: [] for kind in USER_SKIP_KINDS}
   169→    for issue_id, entry in skipped.items():
   170→        kind = entry.get("kind", "temporary")
   171→        by_kind.setdefault(kind, []).append(issue_id)
   172→
   173→    lines: list[str] = [
   174→        "---",
   175→        f"## Skipped ({len(skipped)} items)",
   176→        "",
   177→    ]
   178→
   179→    for kind in USER_SKIP_KINDS:
   180→        ids = by_kind.get(kind, [])
   181→        if not ids:
   182→            continue
   183→        lines.append(f"### {SKIP_KIND_SECTION_LABELS[kind]} ({len(ids)})")
   184→        lines.append("")
   185→        for issue_id in ids:
   186→            entry = skipped.get(issue_id, {})
   187→            item = by_id.get(issue_id)
   188→            if item:
   189→                lines.extend(render_plan_item(item, plan))
   190→            else:
   191→                lines.append(f"- ~~{issue_id}~~ (not in current queue)")
   192→            reason = entry.get("reason")
   193→            if reason:
   194→                lines.append(f"      Reason: {reason}")
   195→            note = entry.get("note")
   196→            if note and not get_issue_note(plan, issue_id):
   197→                lines.append(f"      Note: {note}")
   198→            review_after = entry.get("review_after")
   199→            if review_after:
   200→                skipped_at = entry.get("skipped_at_scan", 0)

1→"""Headline computation for terminal display."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from ._constants import _history_strict
     6→
     7→
     8→def compute_headline(
     9→    phase: str,
    10→    dimensions: dict,
    11→    debt: dict,
    12→    milestone: str | None,
    13→    diff: dict | None,
    14→    obj_strict: float | None,
    15→    obj_score: float | None,
    16→    stats: dict,
    17→    history: list[dict],
    18→    open_by_detector: dict | None = None,
    19→    scan_source: str | None = None,
    20→) -> str | None:
    21→    """Compute one computed sentence for terminal display."""
    22→    # Security callout prefix — prepended to any headline when security issues exist
    23→    security_count = (open_by_detector or {}).get("security", 0)
    24→    security_prefix = ""
    25→    if security_count > 0:
    26→        s = "s" if security_count != 1 else ""
    27→        security_prefix = f"\u26a0 {security_count} security issue{s} — review before other cleanup. "
    28→
    29→    # Review issues callout — all phases
    30→    review_count = (open_by_detector or {}).get("review", 0)
    31→    review_suffix = ""
    32→    if review_count > 0:
    33→        s = "s" if review_count != 1 else ""
    34→        uninvestigated = (open_by_detector or {}).get("review_uninvestigated", 0)
    35→        if uninvestigated > 0:
    36→            review_suffix = (
    37→                f" ({review_count} review issue{s} \u2014 run `desloppify show review --status open`)"
    38→            )
    39→        else:
    40→            review_suffix = f" ({review_count} review issue{s} pending)"
    41→
    42→    headline = _compute_headline_inner(
    43→        phase,
    44→        dimensions,
    45→        debt,
    46→        milestone,
    47→        diff,
    48→        obj_strict,
    49→        obj_score,
    50→        stats,
    51→        history,
    52→        scan_source=scan_source,
    53→    )
    54→
    55→    if headline is None and not security_prefix and not review_suffix:
    56→        return None
    57→    parts = security_prefix + (headline or "") + review_suffix
    58→    return parts or None
    59→
    60→def _compute_headline_inner(
    61→    phase: str,
    62→    dimensions: dict,
    63→    debt: dict,
    64→    milestone: str | None,
    65→    diff: dict | None,
    66→    strict_score: float | None,
    67→    overall_score: float | None,
    68→    stats: dict,
    69→    history: list[dict],
    70→    *,
    71→    scan_source: str | None = None,
    72→) -> str | None:
    73→    """Compute the base headline (without security prefix)."""
    74→    if scan_source == "plan_reconstruction":
    75→        return "Scan metrics unavailable. Issue inventory reconstructed from saved plan."
    76→
    77→    # Milestone takes priority
    78→    if milestone:
    79→        return milestone
    80→
    81→    # First scan framing
    82→    if phase == "first_scan":
    83→        dims = len(dimensions.get("lowest_dimensions", [])) if dimensions else 0
    84→        open_count = stats.get("open", 0)
    85→        if dims:
    86→            return f"First scan complete. {open_count} open issues across {dims} dimensions."
    87→        return f"First scan complete. {open_count} issues detected."
    88→
    89→    # Regression — acknowledge that drops after fixes are normal
    90→    if phase == "regression":
    91→        prev = _history_strict(history[-2])
    92→        curr = _history_strict(history[-1])
    93→        if prev is not None and curr is not None:
    94→            drop = round(prev - curr, 1)
    95→            return (
    96→                f"Score shifted {drop} pts — this is normal after structural changes. "
    97→                f"Rescan after your next fix to see the real trend."
    98→            )
    99→
   100→    # Stagnation — suggest which dimension to focus on
   101→    if phase == "stagnation":
   102→        if strict_score is not None:
   103→            stuck_scans = min(len(history), 5)
   104→            wontfix = debt.get("wontfix_count", 0)
   105→            # Point to the specific dimension dragging things down
   106→            lowest_dims = dimensions.get("lowest_dimensions", [])
   107→            if lowest_dims:
   108→                dim = lowest_dims[0]
   109→                if wontfix > 0:
   110→                    return (
   111→                        f"Score plateaued at {strict_score:.1f} for {stuck_scans} scans. "
   112→                        f"{dim['name']} ({dim['strict']}%) is where the breakthrough is. "
   113→                        f"{wontfix} wontfix items may also be worth revisiting."
   114→                    )
   115→                return (
   116→                    f"Score plateaued at {strict_score:.1f} for {stuck_scans} scans. "
   117→                    f"{dim['name']} ({dim['strict']}%) is where the breakthrough is."
   118→                )
   119→            if wontfix > 0:
   120→                return (
   121→                    f"Score plateaued at {strict_score:.1f} for {stuck_scans} scans. "
   122→                    f"{wontfix} wontfix items — revisit?"
   123→                )
   124→            return (
   125→                f"Score plateaued at {strict_score:.1f} for {stuck_scans} scans. "
   126→                f"Try tackling a different dimension."
   127→            )
   128→
   129→    # Leverage point (lowest dimension with biggest impact)
   130→    lowest = dimensions.get("lowest_dimensions", [])
   131→    if lowest and lowest[0].get("impact", 0) > 0:
   132→        top = lowest[0]
   133→        return (
   134→            f"{top['name']} is your biggest lever: "
   135→            f"{top['failing']} items → +{top['impact']} pts"
   136→        )
   137→
   138→    # Gap callout
   139→    if debt.get("overall_gap", 0) > 5.0:
   140→        gap = debt["overall_gap"]
   141→        worst = debt.get("worst_dimension", "")
   142→        if strict_score is not None and overall_score is not None:
   143→            return (
   144→                f"Strict {strict_score:.1f} vs overall {overall_score:.1f} — "
   145→                f"{gap} pts of wontfix debt, mostly in {worst}"
   146→            )
   147→
   148→    # Maintenance phase
   149→    if phase == "maintenance":
   150→        return (
   151→            f"Strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 — maintenance mode. Watch for regressions."
   152→        )
   153→
   154→    # Middle grind fallback — point toward next item
   155→    if phase == "middle_grind":
   156→        open_count = stats.get("open", 0)
   157→        if lowest:
   158→            top = lowest[0]
   159→            return (
   160→                f"{open_count} issues open. {top['name']} ({top['strict']}%) "
   161→                f"needs attention — run `desloppify next` to start."
   162→            )
   163→        if open_count > 0:
   164→            return f"{open_count} issues open. Run `desloppify next` for the highest-priority item."
   165→
   166→    # Early momentum fallback — celebrate trajectory
   167→    if phase == "early_momentum" and strict_score is not None:
   168→        open_count = stats.get("open", 0)
   169→        return f"Score {strict_score:.1f}/100 with {open_count} issues open. Keep the momentum going."
   170→
   171→    return None
   172→
```

1→"""Phase detection and milestone computation."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.state_io import StateModel
     6→from desloppify.state_scoring import score_snapshot
     7→
     8→from ._constants import _history_strict
     9→
    10→
    11→def detect_phase(history: list[dict], strict_score: float | None) -> str:
    12→    """Detect project phase from scan history trajectory."""
    13→    if not history:
    14→        return "first_scan"
    15→
    16→    if len(history) == 1:
    17→        return "first_scan"
    18→
    19→    strict = strict_score
    20→    if strict is None:
    21→        strict = _history_strict(history[-1])
    22→
    23→    # Check regression: strict dropped from previous scan
    24→    prev = _history_strict(history[-2])
    25→    curr = _history_strict(history[-1])
    26→    if prev is not None and curr is not None and curr < prev - 0.5:
    27→        return "regression"
    28→
    29→    # Check stagnation: strict unchanged ±0.5 for 3+ scans
    30→    if len(history) >= 3:
    31→        recent = [_history_strict(h) for h in history[-3:]]
    32→        if all(r is not None for r in recent):
    33→            spread = max(recent) - min(recent)
    34→            if spread <= 0.5:
    35→                return "stagnation"
    36→
    37→    # Early momentum: scans 2-5 with score rising — check BEFORE score thresholds
    38→    # so early projects get motivational framing even if score is already high
    39→    if len(history) <= 5:
    40→        first = _history_strict(history[0])
    41→        last = _history_strict(history[-1])
    42→        if first is not None and last is not None and last > first:
    43→            return "early_momentum"
    44→
    45→    if strict is not None:
    46→        if strict > 93:
    47→            return "maintenance"
    48→        if strict > 80:
    49→            return "refinement"
    50→
    51→    return "middle_grind"
    52→
    53→
    54→def detect_milestone(
    55→    state: StateModel, _diff: dict | None, history: list[dict],
    56→) -> str | None:
    57→    """Detect notable milestones worth celebrating."""
    58→    strict_score = score_snapshot(state).strict
    59→    stats = state.get("stats", {})
    60→
    61→    # Check T1 clear
    62→    by_tier = stats.get("by_tier", {})
    63→    t1_open = by_tier.get("1", {}).get("open", 0)
    64→    t2_open = by_tier.get("2", {}).get("open", 0)
    65→
    66→    if len(history) >= 2:
    67→        prev_strict = _history_strict(history[-2])
    68→        if prev_strict is not None and strict_score is not None:
    69→            # Crossed 90
    70→            if prev_strict < 90 and strict_score >= 90:
    71→                return "Crossed 90% strict!"
    72→            # Crossed 80
    73→            if prev_strict < 80 and strict_score >= 80:
    74→                return "Crossed 80% strict!"
    75→
    76→    if t1_open == 0 and t2_open == 0:
    77→        # Check if there were T1/T2 items before
    78→        total_t1 = sum(by_tier.get("1", {}).values())
    79→        total_t2 = sum(by_tier.get("2", {}).values())
    80→        if total_t1 + total_t2 > 0:
    81→            return "All T1 and T2 items cleared!"
    82→
    83→    if t1_open == 0:
    84→        total_t1 = sum(by_tier.get("1", {}).values())
    85→        if total_t1 > 0:
    86→            return "All T1 items cleared!"
    87→
    88→    if stats.get("open", 0) == 0 and stats.get("total", 0) > 0:
    89→        return "Zero open issues!"
    90→
    91→    return None
    92→
```

     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections.abc import Callable
     6→from typing import Any, Protocol
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
     9→
    10→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    11→# Protocol stubs for dependency-injected modules
    12→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    13→
    14→
    15→class _StateMod(Protocol):
    16→    def path_scoped_issues(
    17→        self, issues: dict[str, dict[str, Any]], scan_path: Any
    18→    ) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]: ...
    19→
    20→
    21→class _NarrativeMod(Protocol):
    22→    STRUCTURAL_MERGE: frozenset[str]
    23→
    24→
    25→class _RegistryMod(Protocol):
    26→    DETECTORS: dict[str, Any]
    27→
    28→    def display_order(self) -> list[str]: ...
    29→
    30→
    31→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    32→# Breakdown helpers (from scan_reporting_breakdown)
    33→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    34→
    35→
    36→def dimension_bar(score: float, *, colorize_fn, bar_len: int = 15) -> str:
    37→    """Render a score bar consistent with scan detector bars."""
    38→    filled = max(0, min(bar_len, round(score / 100 * bar_len)))
    39→    if score >= 98:
    40→        return colorize_fn("█" * bar_len, "green")
    41→    if score >= 93:
    42→        return colorize_fn("█" * filled, "green") + colorize_fn(
    43→            "░" * (bar_len - filled), "dim"
    44→        )
    45→    return colorize_fn("█" * filled, "yellow") + colorize_fn(
    46→        "░" * (bar_len - filled), "dim"
    47→    )
    48→
    49→
    50→def _print_score_recipe(
    51→    *,
    52→    colorize_fn,
    53→    mech_frac: float,
    54→    subj_frac: float,
    55→    mech_avg: float,
    56→    subj_avg: float | None,
    57→) -> None:
    58→    recipe_lines = [
    59→        line.strip()
    60→        for line in _score_recipe_lines(
    61→            mech_frac=mech_frac,
    62→            subj_frac=subj_frac,
    63→            mech_avg=mech_avg,
    64→            subj_avg=subj_avg,
    65→        )
    66→        if isinstance(line, str) and line.strip()
    67→    ]
    68→    if not recipe_lines:
    69→        return
    70→
    71→    label = "  Score recipe:"
    72→    if subj_avg is None or subj_frac <= 0.0:
    73→        label = "  Score recipe (objective-only):"
    74→    elif mech_frac <= 0.0:
    75→        label = "  Score recipe (subjective-only):"
    76→
    77→    print(colorize_fn(label, "dim"))
    78→    for recipe_line in recipe_lines:
    79→        print(colorize_fn(f"    {recipe_line}", "dim"))
    80→
    81→
    82→def _score_recipe_lines(
    83→    *,
    84→    mech_frac: float,
    85→    subj_frac: float,
    86→    mech_avg: float,
    87→    subj_avg: float | None,
    88→) -> list[str]:
    89→    if subj_avg is None or subj_frac <= 0.0:
    90→        return [
    91→            "overall = 100% mechanical",
    92→            f"Mechanical pool average: {mech_avg:.1f}%",
    93→        ]
    94→    if mech_frac <= 0.0:
    95→        return [
    96→            "overall = 100% subjective",
    97→            f"Subjective pool average: {subj_avg:.1f}%",
    98→        ]
    99→    return [
   100→        f"overall = {mech_frac * 100:.0f}% mechanical + {subj_frac * 100:.0f}% subjective",
   101→        f"Pool averages: mechanical {mech_avg:.1f}% · subjective {subj_avg:.1f}%",
   102→    ]
   103→
   104→
   105→def _sorted_weighted_drags(entries: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   106→    drags = [
   107→        entry
   108→        for entry in entries
   109→        if float(entry.get("overall_drag", 0.0) or 0.0) > 0.01
   110→    ]
   111→    return sorted(
   112→        drags,
   113→        key=lambda entry: -float(entry.get("overall_drag", 0.0) or 0.0),
   114→    )
   115→
   116→
   117→def _detector_progress_bar(*, pct: int, open_count: int, bar_len: int, colorize_fn) -> str:
   118→    filled = round(pct / 100 * bar_len)
   119→    if pct == 100:
   120→        return colorize_fn("█" * bar_len, "green")
   121→    if open_count <= 2:
   122→        return colorize_fn("█" * filled, "green") + colorize_fn(
   123→            "░" * (bar_len - filled), "dim"
   124→        )
   125→    return colorize_fn("█" * filled, "yellow") + colorize_fn(
   126→        "░" * (bar_len - filled), "dim"
   127→    )
   128→
   129→
   130→def _dimension_delta_entry(
   131→    name: str,
   132→    previous: dict[str, Any],
   133→    current: dict[str, Any],
   134→) -> tuple[str, float, float, float, float, float, float] | None:
   135→    old_score = previous.get("score", 100)
   136→    new_score = current.get("score", 100)
   137→    old_strict = previous.get("strict", old_score)
   138→    new_strict = current.get("strict", new_score)
   139→    delta = new_score - old_score
   140→    strict_delta = new_strict - old_strict
   141→    if abs(delta) < 0.1 and abs(strict_delta) < 0.1:
   142→        return None
   143→    return (
   144→        name,
   145→        old_score,
   146→        new_score,
   147→        delta,
   148→        old_strict,
   149→        new_strict,
   150→        strict_delta,

     2→

     4→

     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
     8→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import LOC_COMPACT_THRESHOLD

    11→from desloppify.engine._scoring.subjective.core import DISPLAY_NAMES

    18→from desloppify.engine.planning.render_items import render_queue_item_sections as _render_queue_item_sections

    20→    addressed_section as _addressed_section,

    23→    plan_skipped_section as _plan_skipped_section,

    25→from desloppify.engine.planning.render_sections import (
    26→    plan_superseded_section as _plan_superseded_section,
    27→)
    28→from desloppify.engine.planning.render_sections import (
    29→    plan_user_ordered_section as _plan_user_ordered_section,
    30→)
    31→from desloppify.engine.planning.render_sections import (
    32→    summary_lines as _summary_lines,
    33→)
    34→from desloppify.engine.planning.types import PlanState
    35→from desloppify.state_scoring import score_snapshot
    36→
    37→
    38→def _plan_header(state: PlanState, stats: dict) -> list[str]:
    39→    """Build the plan header: title, score line, and codebase metrics."""
    40→    scores = score_snapshot(state)
    41→    overall_score = scores.overall
    42→    objective_score = scores.objective
    43→    strict_score = scores.strict
    44→
    45→    if (
    46→        overall_score is not None
    47→        and objective_score is not None
    48→        and strict_score is not None
    49→    ):
    50→        header_score = (
    51→            f"**Health:** overall {overall_score:.1f}/100 | "
    52→            f"objective {objective_score:.1f}/100 | "
    53→            f"strict {strict_score:.1f}/100"
    54→        )
    55→    elif overall_score is not None:
    56→        header_score = f"**Score: {overall_score:.1f}/100**"
    57→    else:
    58→        header_score = "**Scores unavailable**"
    59→
    60→    metrics = state.get("codebase_metrics", {})
    61→    total_files = sum(metric.get("total_files", 0) for metric in metrics.values())
    62→    total_loc = sum(metric.get("total_loc", 0) for metric in metrics.values())
    63→    total_dirs = sum(metric.get("total_directories", 0) for metric in metrics.values())
    64→
    65→    deferred_total = stats.get("deferred", 0) + stats.get("triaged_out", 0)
    66→    deferred_segment = f" | {deferred_total} deferred" if deferred_total else ""
    67→    lines = [
    68→        f"# Desloppify Plan — {date.today().isoformat()}",
    69→        "",
    70→        f"{header_score} | "
    71→        f"{stats.get('open', 0)} open | "
    72→        f"{stats.get('fixed', 0)} fixed | "
    73→        f"{stats.get('wontfix', 0)} wontfix | "
    74→        f"{stats.get('auto_resolved', 0)} auto-resolved"
    75→        f"{deferred_segment}",
    76→        "",
    77→    ]
    78→
    79→    if total_files:
    80→        loc_str = (
    81→            f"{total_loc:,}"
    82→            if total_loc < LOC_COMPACT_THRESHOLD
    83→            else f"{total_loc // 1000}K"
    84→        )
    85→        lines.append(
    86→            f"\n{total_files} files · {loc_str} LOC · {total_dirs} directories\n"
    87→        )
    88→
    89→    return lines
    90→
    91→
    92→def _plan_dimension_table(state: PlanState) -> list[str]:
    93→    """Build the dimension health table rows (empty list when no data)."""
    94→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
    95→    if not dim_scores:
    96→        return []
    97→
    98→    lines = [
    99→        "## Health by Dimension",
   100→        "",
   101→        "| Dimension | Tier | Checks | Issues | Health | Strict | Action |",
   102→        "|-----------|------|--------|--------|--------|--------|--------|",
   103→    ]
   104→    static_names: set[str] = set()
   105→    rendered_names: set[str] = set()
   106→    subjective_display_names = {
   107→        display.lower() for display in DISPLAY_NAMES.values()
   108→    }
   109→
   110→    def _looks_subjective(name: str, data: dict) -> bool:
   111→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
   112→        if "subjective_assessment" in detectors:
   113→            return True
   114→        lowered = name.strip().lower()
   115→        return lowered in subjective_display_names or lowered.startswith("elegance")
   116→
   117→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   118→        ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
   119→        if not ds:
   120→            continue
   121→        static_names.add(dim.name)
   122→        rendered_names.add(dim.name)
   123→        checks = ds.get("checks", 0)
   124→        issues = ds.get("failing", 0)
   125→        score_val = ds.get("score", 100)
   126→        strict_val = ds.get("strict", score_val)
   127→        bold = "**" if score_val < 93 else ""
   128→        action = dimension_action_type(dim.name)
   129→        lines.append(
   130→            f"| {bold}{dim.name}{bold} | T{dim.tier} | "
   131→            f"{checks:,} | {issues} | {score_val:.1f}% | {strict_val:.1f}% | {action} |"
   132→        )
   133→
   134→    from desloppify.engine.planning.dimension_rows import scorecard_dimension_rows
   135→
   136→    scorecard_rows = scorecard_dimension_rows(state)
   137→    scorecard_subjective_rows = [
   138→        (name, ds) for name, ds in scorecard_rows if _looks_subjective(name, ds)
   139→    ]
   140→    scorecard_subjective_names = {name for name, _ in scorecard_subjective_rows}
   141→
   142→    # Show custom dimensions not present in scorecard.png in the main table.
   143→    custom_non_subjective_rows: list[tuple[str, dict]] = []
   144→    for name, ds in sorted(dim_scores.items(), key=lambda item: str(item[0]).lower()):
   145→        if name in rendered_names or not isinstance(ds, dict):
   146→            continue
   147→        if _looks_subjective(name, ds):
   148→            continue
   149→        custom_non_subjective_rows.append((name, ds))
   150→        rendered_names.add(name)

1→"""Dimension and empty-result rendering helpers for show command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
