    11→
    12→
    13→def _print_dimension_score(dim_data: dict, display_name: str) -> None:
    14→    """Print the health/strict score line for a dimension if available."""
    15→    score_val = dim_data.get("score") if isinstance(dim_data, dict) else None
    16→    strict_val = (
    17→        dim_data.get("strict", score_val) if isinstance(dim_data, dict) else None
    18→    )
    19→    if score_val is not None:
    20→        print(
    21→            colorize(
    22→                f"  {display_name}: {score_val:.1f}% health (strict: {strict_val:.1f}%)",
    23→                "bold",
    24→            )
    25→        )
    26→
    27→
    28→def _render_judgment(state: dict, dimension_key: str) -> None:
    29→    """Print judgment narrative (strengths, issue_character, score_rationale) if available."""
    30→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments", {})
    31→    assessment = assessments.get(dimension_key, {})
    32→    if not isinstance(assessment, dict):
    33→        return
    34→    judgment = assessment.get("judgment")
    35→    if not isinstance(judgment, dict):
    36→        return
    37→
    38→    score_rationale = judgment.get("score_rationale", "")
    39→    if score_rationale:
    40→        print(colorize(f"  Score rationale: {score_rationale}", "dim"))
    41→
    42→    strengths = judgment.get("strengths", [])
    43→    if isinstance(strengths, list) and strengths:
    44→        print(colorize("  Strengths:", "dim"))
    45→        for s in strengths:
    46→            print(colorize(f"    - {s}", "dim"))
    47→
    48→    issue_character = judgment.get("issue_character", "")
    49→    if issue_character:
    50→        print(colorize(f"  Issue character: {issue_character}", "dim"))
    51→
    52→
    53→def _render_subjective_dimension(
    54→    state: dict,
    55→    config: dict,
    56→    entity,
    57→    pattern_raw: str,
    58→) -> None:
    59→    """Show score + subjective explanation for a subjective dimension."""
    60→    lowered = pattern_raw.strip().lower().replace(" ", "_") if pattern_raw else ""
    61→    dim_data, display_name = _lookup_dimension_score(state, entity.display_name)
    62→    _print_dimension_score(dim_data, display_name)
    63→    _render_judgment(state, lowered)
    64→    print(
    65→        colorize(
    66→            f"  '{pattern_raw.strip()}' is a subjective dimension "
    67→            "— its score comes from design reviews, not code issues.",
    68→            "yellow",
    69→        )
    70→    )
    71→    dim_reviews = [
    72→        issue
    73→        for issue in (state.get("issues") or {}).values()
    74→        if issue.get("detector") == "review"
    75→        and issue.get("status") == "open"
    76→        and lowered
    77→        in str(issue.get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")).lower().replace(" ", "_")
    78→    ]
    79→    if dim_reviews:
    80→        print(
    81→            colorize(
    82→                f"  {len(dim_reviews)} open review issue(s). "
    83→                "Run `show review --status open`.",
    84→                "dim",
    85→            )
    86→        )
    87→    show_subjective_followup(
    88→        state,
    89→        target_strict_score_from_config(config),
    90→    )
    91→
    92→
    93→def _render_clean_mechanical_dimension(state: dict, entity) -> None:
    94→    """Show score + 'no open issues' for a mechanical dimension with zero issues."""
    95→    dim_data, display_name = _lookup_dimension_score(state, entity.display_name)
    96→    _print_dimension_score(dim_data, display_name)
    97→    det_list = ", ".join(entity.detectors) if entity.detectors else "none"
    98→    print(
    99→        colorize(
   100→            f"  No open issues for {entity.display_name}. Detectors: {det_list}",
   101→            "green",
   102→        )
   103→    )
   104→
   105→
   106→def _load_dimension_issues(
   107→    state: dict,
   108→    entity,
   109→    status_filter: str,
   110→) -> list[dict]:
   111→    """Load issues for all detectors in a mechanical dimension."""
   112→    all_matches: list[dict] = []
   113→    for detector in entity.detectors:
   114→        matches = load_matches(
   115→            state, scope=detector, status_filter=status_filter, chronic=False
   116→        )
   117→        all_matches.extend(matches)
   118→    seen: set[str] = set()
   119→    unique: list[dict] = []
   120→    for item in all_matches:
   121→        issue_id = item.get("id", "")
   122→        if issue_id not in seen:
   123→            seen.add(issue_id)
   124→            unique.append(item)
   125→    return unique
   126→
   127→
   128→def _render_no_matches(entity, pattern, status_filter, narrative, state, config):
   129→    """Handle no-issues case for normal and subjective views."""
   130→    print(colorize(f"No {status_filter} issues matching: {pattern}", "yellow"))

   134→            "query": pattern,
   135→            "status_filter": status_filter,
   136→            "total": 0,
   137→            "issues": [],
   138→            "narrative": narrative,
   139→        }
   140→    )
   141→    if entity.kind == "special_view":
   142→        show_subjective_followup(
   143→            state,
   144→            target_strict_score_from_config(config),
   145→        )
   146→    else:
   147→        hint = _detector_names_hint()
   148→        print(
   149→            colorize(
   150→                f"  Try: show <detector>, show <file>, or show subjective. "
   151→                f"Detectors: {hint}",
   152→                "dim",
   153→            )
   154→        )
   155→
   156→
   157→def _render_subjective_views_guide(entity) -> None:
   158→    """Print related subjective views after subjective output."""
   159→    if entity.kind == "special_view" and entity.pattern.strip().lower() in (
   160→        "subjective",
   161→        "subjective_review",
   162→    ):

   164→        print(colorize("    `show review --status open`            Per-file design review issues", "dim"))
   165→        print(colorize("    `show subjective_review --status open`  Files needing re-review", "dim"))

def issue_status_tokens(*, include_all: bool = False) -> frozenset[str]:
    """Return canonical issue-status tokens, optionally including `all`."""
    if include_all:
        return frozenset({*_CANONICAL_ISSUE_STATUSES, "all"})
    return _CANONICAL_ISSUE_STATUSES

   133→def render_historical_focus(batch: PromptBatchPayload) -> str:
   134→    focus = batch.get("historical_issue_focus")
   135→    if not isinstance(focus, dict):

   137→
   138→    selected_raw = focus.get("selected_count", 0)

   165→    def _render_entry(entry: dict) -> str:
   166→        status = str(entry.get("status", "")).strip()
   167→        summary = str(entry.get("summary", "")).strip()
   168→        note = str(entry.get("note", "")).strip()

   171→        if note:
   172→            line += f" (note: {note})"
   173→        return line
   174→
   175→    # Render active groups first (open, deferred, triaged_out)

   177→        group = grouped.pop(status_key, [])
   178→        if group:
   179→            lines.append(f"\n  {group_label} ({len(group)}):")
   180→            lines.extend(_render_entry(e) for e in group)
   181→
   182→    # Render resolved group (all remaining resolved statuses)
   183→    resolved: list[dict] = []
   184→    for status_key in list(grouped):
   185→        if status_key in _HISTORICAL_RESOLVED_STATUSES:
   186→            resolved.extend(grouped.pop(status_key))
   187→    if resolved:
   188→        lines.append(f"\n  {_HISTORICAL_RESOLVED_GROUP} ({len(resolved)}):")
   189→        lines.extend(_render_entry(e) for e in resolved)
   190→
   191→    # Any unknown statuses
   192→    for status_key, group in grouped.items():
   193→        if group:
   194→            lines.append(f"\n  {status_key} ({len(group)}):")
   195→            lines.extend(_render_entry(e) for e in group)
   196→
   197→    lines.append("")
   198→    lines.append("Explore past review issues:")
   199→    lines.append("  desloppify show review --no-budget              # all open review issues")
   200→    lines.append("  desloppify show review --status deferred         # deferred issues")

## Summary: `show --status` Filter Path and `stats` Dict Computation

I've traced both aspects of your question through the codebase. Here are the findings:

---

## 1. **The `show --status` Filter Path**

### 1.1: Argument Parsing
- **File**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/cli_support/parser_groups.py` (lines 142-196)
- The `--status` argument is defined in `_add_show_parser()` with choices validated by `_STATUS_CHOICES`
- `_STATUS_CHOICES` is computed from `issue_status_tokens(include_all=True)` (line 22)
- Valid values: `"open"`, `"fixed"`, `"wontfix"`, `"false_positive"`, `"auto_resolved"`, `"deferred"`, `"triaged_out"`, `"all"`
- Default: `"open"` (line 164)

### 1.2: Where `--status` is Resolved
- **File**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/show/scope.py` (line 173-190)
- Function: `resolve_show_scope(args)` 
- Extracts `status_filter = getattr(args, "status", "open")` (line 177)
- Returns `(ok, pattern, status_filter, scope)` tuple

### 1.3: Status Validation via `issue_status_tokens()`
- **File**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/base/enums.py` (lines 71-75)
- **Function**: `issue_status_tokens(include_all: bool = False)`
- Returns a frozenset of canonical statuses + optional `"all"` keyword
- **Gate**: The argparse `choices` list directly gates which values are accepted at CLI parse time (parser_groups.py line 163)
- No additional validation happens in the filtering logic itself—argparse enforces it upfront

### 1.4: How the Status Filter is Applied
The `status_filter` flows through this chain:

1. **Entry point** (`cmd_show` in `/desloppify/app/commands/show/cmd.py`, line 133):
   - Calls `resolve_show_scope(args)` → gets `status_filter`

2. **Matching logic** (`scope.py`, lines 193-211, function `load_matches()`):
   - Calls `build_work_queue(state, options=QueueBuildOptions(status=status_filter, ...))`

3. **Queue building** (`engine/_work_queue/selection.py`, lines 14-46, function `select_queue_items()`):
   - If `status == "open"` and not chronic → uses snapshot-based (plan-aware) queue
   - Otherwise → calls `build_issue_items()` with `status_filter`

4. **Issue-level filtering** (`engine/_work_queue/ranking.py`, lines 124-182, function `build_issue_items()`):
   - Iterates through all issues
   - Line 139: **`if not status_matches(issue.get("status", "open"), status_filter): continue`**
   - This is the actual filtering gate

5. **Status comparison** (`engine/_work_queue/helpers.py`, lines 28-29, function `status_matches()`):
   ```python
   def status_matches(item_status: str, status_filter: str) -> bool:
       return status_filter == "all" or item_status == status_filter
   ```
   - **Simple equality check**: accepts exact status match OR the magic `"all"` keyword
   - No regex, no aliases, no partial matching

### Key Points
- `deferred` and `triaged_out` are fully supported status values (defined in `Status` enum)
- Both are accepted by `issue_status_tokens()` and `argparse` 
- Both will be included when `--status deferred` or `--status triaged_out` is used
- `--status all` works as a bypass filter (returns everything except suppressed)

---

## 2. **The `stats` Dict Computation**

### 2.1: Where `stats` is Computed
- **File**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_scoring/state_integration.py` (lines 200-221)
- **Function**: `recompute_stats(state, scan_path, subjective_integrity_target)`
- This is called after scanning and before state is written

### 2.2: Counting Logic
- **Helper function** (`state_integration.py`, lines 48-62): `_count_issues(issues)`
  - Initializes: `counters = dict.fromkeys(_EMPTY_COUNTERS, 0)` where `_EMPTY_COUNTERS = tuple(sorted(issue_status_tokens()))`
  - This expands to: `['auto_resolved', 'deferred', 'false_positive', 'fixed', 'open', 'triaged_out', 'wontfix']`
  
- **Loop** (lines 53-60):
  ```python
  for issue in issues.values():
      if issue.get("suppressed"):
          continue
      status = issue["status"]
      tier = issue.get("tier", 3)
      counters[status] = counters.get(status, 0) + 1
      # ... also updates tier_stats dict
  ```

### 2.3: Stats Dict Structure
- **File**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_state/schema_types_issues.py` (lines 67-74)
- **TypedDict**: `StateStats`
  ```python
  class StateStats(TypedDict, total=False):
      total: int
      open: int
      fixed: int
      auto_resolved: int
      wontfix: int
      false_positive: int
      by_tier: dict[str, TierStats]
  ```
- **Notice**: `deferred` and `triaged_out` are **NOT in the official StateStats schema**

### 2.4: Assignment to State
- **state_integration.py**, lines 210-216:
  ```python
  state["stats"] = {
      "total": sum(counters.values()),
      **counters,  # Spreads all counted statuses
      "by_tier": {
          str(tier): tier_counts for tier, tier_counts in sorted(tier_stats.items())
      },
  }
  ```
- The `**counters` spread will **include** `deferred` and `triaged_out` counts if any issues have those statuses
- They're just not declared in the TypedDict (which uses `total=False` so extra keys are allowed)

### 2.5: How Stats are Used
Two main places use `state["stats"]`:

1. **Plan header** (`engine/planning/render.py`, lines 38-89, function `_plan_header()`):
   - Lines 65-75:
     ```python
     deferred_total = stats.get("deferred", 0) + stats.get("triaged_out", 0)
     deferred_segment = f" | {deferred_total} deferred" if deferred_total else ""
     lines = [
         ...
         f"... | {stats.get('open', 0)} open | "
         f"{stats.get('fixed', 0)} fixed | "
         f"{stats.get('wontfix', 0)} wontfix | "
         f"{stats.get('auto_resolved', 0)} auto-resolved"
         f"{deferred_segment}",
         ...
     ]
     ```
   - **Already handles** `deferred` and `triaged_out` explicitly ✓

2. **Summary lines** (`engine/planning/render_sections.py`, lines 15-28, function `summary_lines()`):
   - Lines 17-23:
     ```python
     deferred_count = stats.get("deferred", 0) + stats.get("triaged_out", 0)
     total_issues = sum(
         stats.get(key, 0)
         for key in ("open", "fixed", "wontfix", "auto_resolved", "deferred", "triaged_out")
     )
     addressed = total_issues - open_count - deferred_count
     ```
   - **Already handles** `deferred` and `triaged_out` in the total and deferred calculations ✓

### Key Points
- **Deferred and triaged_out ARE counted** in stats computation (lines 50-60 of state_integration.py)
- Both are included in `_EMPTY_COUNTERS` initialization (line 28)
- Both appear in plan header (render.py) and summary lines (render_sections.py)
- They're not in the TypedDict schema but that's OK—TypedDict with `total=False` allows extra keys
- The `by_tier` breakdown also includes these statuses (TierStats also lacks them in TypedDict but again `total=False`)

   430→def explode_to_single_dimension(
   431→    batches: list[PromptBatchPayload],
   432→    dimension_prompts: dict[str, dict[str, object]] | None = None,
   433→) -> list[PromptBatchPayload]:
   434→    """Split multi-dimension batches into one batch per dimension.
   435→
   436→    Preserves seed files and rationale — each exploded batch keeps the same
   437→    file grouping but is scoped to a single dimension. When *dimension_prompts*
   438→    is provided, each exploded batch gets a public ``dimension_prompts`` map
   439→    scoped to its single dimension.
   440→    """
   441→    prompts = dimension_prompts or {}
   442→    result: list[PromptBatchPayload] = []
   443→    for batch in batches:
   444→        dims = batch.get("dimensions", [])
   445→        if not isinstance(dims, list):
   446→            result.append(batch)
   447→            continue
   448→        for dim in dims:
   449→            exploded: PromptBatchPayload = {**batch, "dimensions": [dim]}
   450→            dim_prompt = prompts.get(dim)
   451→            if isinstance(dim_prompt, dict):
   452→                exploded["dimension_prompts"] = {str(dim): dim_prompt}
   453→            result.append(exploded)
   454→    return result
   455→

   457→def render_dimension_prompts_block(

   459→    dimension_prompts: dict[str, dict[str, object]],
   460→) -> str:
   461→    """Render inline dimension guidance so the reviewer sees the full rubric."""
   462→    if not dimensions or not dimension_prompts:
   463→        return ""
   464→    lines: list[str] = ["DIMENSION TO EVALUATE:\n"]
   465→    for dim in dimensions:
   466→        prompt = dimension_prompts.get(dim)
   467→        if not isinstance(prompt, dict):
   468→            lines.append(f"## {dim}\n(no rubric available)\n")
   469→            continue
   470→        description = str(prompt.get("description", "")).strip()
   471→        lines.append(f"## {dim}")
   472→        if description:
   473→            lines.append(description)
   474→
   475→        look_for = prompt.get("look_for")
   476→        if isinstance(look_for, list) and look_for:
   477→            lines.append("Look for:")
   478→            for item in look_for:
   479→                lines.append(f"- {item}")
   480→
   481→        skip = prompt.get("skip")
   482→        if isinstance(skip, list) and skip:
   483→            lines.append("Skip:")
   484→            for item in skip:
   485→                lines.append(f"- {item}")
   486→        lines.append("")
   487→    return "\n".join(lines) + "\n"

   490→def render_dimension_context_block(

   499→    if not dimensions or not dimension_contexts:

   518→            prefix = "[settled] " if settled else ""
   519→            lines.append(f"- {prefix}{header}")
   520→        if len(lines) > 1:
   521→            sections.append("\n".join(lines))
   522→
   523→    if not sections:
   524→        return ""
   525→
   526→    header_block = (
   527→        "## Accumulated Codebase Context\n\n"
   528→        "Previous reviews established these insights. Do not re-investigate settled\n"
   529→        "items unless you see clear evidence the code has changed. For full details\n"
   530→        "on any item, read the blind packet's `dimension_contexts.{dimension}.insights`.\n\n"
   531→    )
   532→    footer = (

   534→        "a clear header (5-10 words) and a description explaining WHY, not WHAT.\n"
   535→        "Settle items only when you're confident they're intentional.\n\n"
   536→    )
   537→    return header_block + "\n\n".join(sections) + footer

## Summary: How Desloppify Handles Issue Statuses in Narrative and Coaching

Based on my thorough search of the codebase, here are my key findings:

### 1. **Narrative System (`intelligence/narrative/`) - Status Handling**

**In `signals.py` - `count_open_by_detector()` function (lines 95-116):**
- **Critical finding**: This function ONLY counts issues with `status == "open"` that are **not suppressed**
- It explicitly filters: `if issue["status"] != "open" or issue.get("suppressed"): continue`
- **Deferred and triaged_out issues are COMPLETELY INVISIBLE** to the narrative system
- These excluded issues will not appear in:
  - Open counts by detector
  - Strategy guidance
  - Actions generation
  - Headline construction

**In `core.py` - `compute_narrative()` (lines 68-145):**
- Calls `_count_open_by_detector(issues)` which produces `by_detector` counts (line 88)
- Passes these counts to strategy, actions, and headline computation
- Since the count function filters out deferred/triaged_out, these don't reach coaching output

**In `headline.py` - Headline Generation:**
- Only references `stats.get("open", 0)` for issue counts (lines 84, 156, 168)
- Never mentions deferred or triaged_out counts in any headlines
- Security and review counts exclude deferred/triaged_out

### 2. **Stats Computation (`engine/_scoring/state_integration.py`) - Status Counts**

**In `_count_issues()` function (lines 48-62):**
- Computes per-status counters using `issue_status_tokens()` from `base/enums.py`
- **Returns ALL status types**: open, fixed, wontfix, false_positive, auto_resolved, deferred, triaged_out
- Creates: `counters[status]` for each issue not suppressed
- Also creates: `by_tier[tier][status]` breakdown

**In `recompute_stats()` (lines 200-221):**
- Calls `_count_issues()` which creates full status breakdown
- **Stats dict includes**: 
  ```python
  {
    "total": sum(counters),
    "open": N,
    "fixed": N,
    "wontfix": N,
    "false_positive": N,
    "auto_resolved": N,
    "deferred": N,
    "triaged_out": N,
    "by_tier": {
      "1": {status breakdown},
      "2": {status breakdown},
      ...
    }
  }
  ```

**Key insight**: `stats` dict in state DOES include deferred and triaged_out counts, but the narrative coaching system doesn't read these fields.

### 3. **Show Command Output (`app/commands/show/`)**

**In `render.py` - `_print_single_issue()` (lines 48-89):**
- Properly renders all status icons including:
  - `"deferred": "⏸"`
  - `"triaged_out": "△"`
- Status filtering happens in the work queue builder via `status_filter` parameter

**In `cmd.py` - `resolve_show_scope()` (lines 173-190):**
- Default status filter is "open" (line 177)
- User can pass `--status` flag to filter by any status
- The work queue builder receives this status filter

**In `scope.py` - `load_matches()` (lines 193-211):**
- Calls `build_work_queue()` with `status=status_filter`
- The queue builder filters by status, so deferred/triaged_out CAN be shown via `--status deferred` or `--status triaged_out`

**Show output summary (lines 165-210 in render.py):**
- Shows "By tier" and "By detector" breakdowns
- **Does NOT show a status breakdown** (e.g., no "N open, N deferred, N fixed" summary)

### 4. **Scan Command Output (`app/commands/scan/reporting/`)**

**In `summary.py` - `show_score_delta()`:**
- Accesses `stats = state["stats"]` (line 318)
- Uses only `stats.get("wontfix", 0)` for gap calculation (line 340)
- **No display of deferred/triaged_out counts in scan output**

**In `text.py` - Workflow guide and score explanation:**
- No mention of status breakdown or deferred/triaged_out visibility
- Focuses on overall/objective/strict/verified score tiers

### 5. **Review Command - Issue History Grouping (`app/commands/review/prompt_sections.py`)**

**In `render_historical_focus()` (lines 133-202):**
- **Groups historical issues by status explicitly**:
  ```python
  _HISTORICAL_STATUS_GROUPS = (
      ("open", "Still open"),
      ("deferred", "Deferred"),
      ("triaged_out", "Triaged out"),
  )
  _HISTORICAL_RESOLVED_STATUSES = {"fixed", "wontfix", "false_positive", "auto_resolved"}
  ```
- Groups issues into sections: "Still open", "Deferred", "Triaged out", "Resolved"
- **Review agents WILL see deferred and triaged_out issues** when reviewing historical issue context
- Instructions tell reviewers: "Do not re-report resolved or deferred items" (line 153)

### 6. **Plan Command - Deferred/Triaged_out Handling**

**In `app/commands/plan/override_skip.py`:**
- Defines `_BACKLOG_REOPEN_STATUSES = {"deferred", "triaged_out"}` 
- When moving deferred items to backlog, reopens them from these statuses
- Mentions "deferred" and "triaged_out" explicitly in messages

### 7. **Key Gaps & Inconsistencies**

| System | Deferred | Triaged_out | Visibility |
|--------|----------|-------------|------------|
| **Narrative coaching** | Invisible | Invisible | NOT in counts, headlines, actions |
| **Stats computation** | Counted | Counted | In `state["stats"]` dict but not displayed |
| **Show command** | Filterable | Filterable | Can view with `--status deferred` |
| **Show summary** | Missing | Missing | No status breakdown in output |
| **Scan output** | Missing | Missing | Not shown in scan report |
| **Review context** | Visible | Visible | Explicitly grouped in historical issues |
| **Plan/backlog** | Handled | Handled | Can reopen/move to backlog |

### 8. **Root Cause**

The narrative coaching pipeline uses `count_open_by_detector()` which **deliberately filters to only "open" status** (line 99 in signals.py). This is the single point where deferred/triaged_out are excluded from coaching output. The stats are computed correctly, but the coaching layer doesn't read the deferred/triaged_out counts from stats.

> AGENT

The `render_issues` header already shows `"{status_filter} issues"` — so when you query `--status deferred` it says "2 deferred issues matching 'review'". But when showing `--status all`, there's no breakdown by status. Let me check if `--status all` is the relevant use case here.

Actually, the real question is: when a user runs `desloppify show review` (default `--status open`), can they **see** that deferred issues exist at all? The answer is no — they only see open issues. They'd need to know to ask `--status all` or `--status deferred`.

This is where the plan header helps — when you run `desloppify plan`, you see "2 open | 1 fixed | 1 deferred". That's the high-level signal.

Let me now check the `show` command with `--status all` to see if issues display with their status icon:

> AGENT

5218 passed (24 new tests added), 3 skipped, 0 failures. Now let me summarize what I found and what gaps remain.

Here's what the trace-through revealed:

## What works correctly

| Surface | Status | Detail |
|---------|--------|--------|
| `show review --status deferred` | Works | argparse choices from `issue_status_tokens()` include deferred/triaged_out |
| `show review --status all` | Works | Shows all issues with correct icons (⏸ for deferred, △ for triaged_out) |
| `plan` header | Works | Shows "N deferred" segment when non-zero |
| `plan` summary | Works | Counts deferred/triaged_out in total; shows deferred note |
| Stats computation | Works | `_count_issues()` uses `issue_status_tokens()` which includes both new statuses |
| Review historical focus | Works | Groups by status: "Still open", "Deferred", "Triaged out", "Resolved" |
| Review deferral context | Works | Shows cycle count for deferred dimensions |
| Status icons | Works | `⏸` and `△` render in show output |
| Scoring policy | Works | All 3 modes count deferred/triaged_out as failures |
| Triage dismiss → state | Works | Sets `triaged_out` in state issues |
| Reconcile migration | Works | Syncs `open` + skip kind → correct status on every scan |
| Backlog reopen | Works | Reopens deferred/triaged_out back to open |
| Scan reappearance | Works | Deferred/triaged_out NOT auto-reopened (correct) |

## Gaps found (pre-existing, not introduced by our changes)

1. **Narrative coaching is blind to deferred/triaged_out** — `count_open_by_detector()` only counts `status == "open"`. This is actually *correct behavior*: deferred issues shouldn't drive coaching urgency. They were intentionally acknowledged and set aside.

2. **Review agents don't know about lifecycle commands** — The batch prompt doesn't mention `plan skip`/`unskip`/`backlog`. This is by design: review agents are read-only evaluators, not lifecycle managers. The CLI hints in `render_historical_focus` ("explore with `desloppify show review --status deferred`") are the right level of agent tooling.

3. **`show` command doesn't show cross-status breakdown** — When you run `show review`, it only shows issues matching the filter (default: open). There's no "also: 2 deferred, 1 triaged_out" hint. This is a potential improvement but the plan header (`desloppify plan`) is the right place for that high-level view.

4. **TypedDicts were missing new fields** — Fixed: added `deferred`/`triaged_out` to `TierStats` and `StateStats`.

None of these are bugs — the system works correctly end-to-end. The deferred/triaged_out lifecycle is fully tracked, filterable, and surfaced where it matters.

<summary>
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   The user provided a detailed implementation plan titled "Unify Issue Lifecycle Status & Surface History to Reviewers" with two major parts:
   - **Part A (A1-A9)**: Unify the Status enum by adding `deferred` and `triaged_out` statuses, making all skip operations update state status, ensuring state is always the authoritative source for issue disposition. This involved updating enums, scoring policy, skip↔status mappings, backlog/triage paths, scan reappearance behavior, rendering surfaces, and adding a data migration in reconcile.
   - **Part B (B1-B4)**: Surface issue history to reviewers by defaulting `--retrospective` to True, rewriting historical focus rendering with status grouping and CLI hints, adding dimension deferral context to review prompts, and threading plan path into the review prepare flow.
   
   After implementation, the user asked to **create a test scenario** to verify the data flows correctly through all surfaces, check if agents can dig deeper into each issue type, verify high-level status counts are visible, and confirm agents know about lifecycle tools.
   
   Finally, the user asked to **fix any issues and push to GitHub**.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - Issue lifecycle states: `open`, `fixed`, `wontfix`, `false_positive`, `auto_resolved`, `deferred` (new), `triaged_out` (new)
   - Plan skip kinds: `temporary`, `permanent`, `false_positive`, `triaged_out` — each maps to a state status
   - State is authoritative for disposition; plan.skipped continues for queue management/metadata
   - Scoring modes (lenient/strict/verified_strict) with failure status allowlists
   - Reconciliation: syncs plan.skipped → state status on every scan
   - Review blind packet system with batch prompts, historical issue focus, and dimension deferral context
   - `BooleanOptionalAction` for argparse `--retrospective`/`--no-retrospective`
   - TypedDict schemas for state persistence (TierStats, StateStats)
   - `_is_issue_alive()` in reconcile must treat deferred/triaged_out as alive

3. Files and Code Sections:

   - **`desloppify/base/enums.py`**
     - Central enum definitions for issue statuses. Added DEFERRED and TRIAGED_OUT.
     ```python
     class Status(enum.StrEnum):
         OPEN = "open"
         FIXED = "fixed"
         WONTFIX = "wontfix"
         FALSE_POSITIVE = "false_positive"
         AUTO_RESOLVED = "auto_resolved"
         DEFERRED = "deferred"
         TRIAGED_OUT = "triaged_out"
         RESOLVED = "resolved"  # Legacy on-disk value; migrated to FIXED on load.
     ```
     - `_CANONICAL_ISSUE_STATUSES` updated to include both new values
     - `_RESOLVED_STATUSES` NOT updated — deferred/triaged_out are acknowledged but not resolved

   - **`desloppify/engine/_plan/skip_policy.py`**
     - Central skip-kind policy. Updated both mapping functions.
     ```python
     def skip_kind_state_status(kind: str) -> str | None:
         return {
             "permanent": "wontfix",
             "false_positive": "false_positive",
             "temporary": "deferred",
             "triaged_out": "triaged_out",
         }.get(kind)

     def skip_kind_needs_state_reopen(kind: str) -> bool:
         return kind in {"permanent", "false_positive", "temporary", "triaged_out"}
     ```

   - **`desloppify/engine/_scoring/policy/core.py`**
     - Scoring failure status allowlists. Added deferred/triaged_out to all 3 modes.
     ```python
     FAILURE_STATUSES_BY_MODE: dict[ScoreMode, frozenset[str]] = {
         "lenient": frozenset({"open", "deferred", "triaged_out"}),
         "strict": frozenset({"open", "wontfix", "auto_resolved", "deferred", "triaged_out"}),
         "verified_strict": frozenset({"open", "wontfix", "fixed", "false_positive", "deferred", "triaged_out"}),
     }
     ```

   - **`desloppify/app/commands/plan/override_skip.py`**
     - `cmd_plan_backlog()` updated to reopen deferred/triaged_out issues in state when removing from plan tracking:
     ```python
     _BACKLOG_REOPEN_STATUSES = {"deferred", "triaged_out"}
     state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
     issues = state_data.get("issues", {})
     reopen_ids = [
         fid for fid in removed
         if issues.get(fid, {}).get("status") in _BACKLOG_REOPEN_STATUSES
     ]
     if reopen_ids:
         for fid in reopen_ids:
             state_mod.resolve_issues(state_data, fid, "open")
     ```
     - Uses `save_plan_state_transactional` when state needs updating, plain `save_plan` otherwise.

   - **`desloppify/engine/_plan/triage/apply.py`**
     - `apply_triage_to_plan()` now sets state status for dismissed issues:
     ```python
     issues = state.get("issues", {})
     triaged_out_status = skip_kind_state_status("triaged_out")
     for fid in dismissed_ids:
         issue = issues.get(fid)
         if issue and issue.get("status") == "open" and triaged_out_status:
             issue["status"] = triaged_out_status
             issue["note"] = f"Triaged out by epic triage v{version}"
     ```

   - **`desloppify/engine/_plan/reconcile.py`**
     - Added `_ALIVE_STATUSES = frozenset({"open", "deferred", "triaged_out"})` and updated `_is_issue_alive()` and `_find_candidates()` to use it.
     - Added `_sync_skipped_issue_statuses()` for data migration:
     ```python
     def _sync_skipped_issue_statuses(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> None:
         skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
         issues = state.get("issues", {})
         for fid, entry in skipped.items():
             issue = issues.get(fid)
             if issue is None or issue.get("status") != "open":
                 continue
             kind = str(entry.get("kind", ""))
             target_status = skip_kind_state_status(kind)
             if target_status and target_status != "open":
                 issue["status"] = target_status
     ```
     - Added state reopen for resurfaced issues (deferred→open) after `resurface_stale_skips`.
     - Called `_sync_skipped_issue_statuses` at top of `reconcile_plan_after_scan`.

5. Problem Solving:
   - Solved the core architectural problem: issue status was split between `plan.skipped[id].kind` and `issue.status`, with temporary/triaged_out skips leaving status as "open". Now all skip operations update state status.
   - Solved the data migration problem: existing data with open+skipped issues gets migrated on next scan via `_sync_skipped_issue_statuses` in reconcile.
   - Solved the reconcile interaction: `_is_issue_alive` had to be updated to treat deferred/triaged_out as alive, otherwise the sync step would mark issues as deferred, then supersede them.
   - Solved the resurfacing interaction: when issues resurface from temporary skip, they must also have state status changed from deferred back to open.
   - Verified through exploration agents that: `show --status deferred` works correctly, stats computation includes new statuses, narrative coaching correctly excludes deferred (by design), review agents are read-only evaluators and don't need lifecycle commands.
